[1] Multimodal Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition with Bidirectional Audio–Text Cross-Attention cs.CL | cs.SD | eess.ASPDF

TingTing Gu, JinCheng Luo, WenQiu Guo

TL;DR: 该音频—文本双向交叉注意模型联合表示多语言转写与语音波形，进行视频级矛盾/犹豫状态二分类；在 BAH 数据集 subject-independent 测试集上准确率 72.19%、A/H F1 77.95%、Macro-F1 70.16%。

Details

Motivation: 矛盾与犹豫是细微行为状态，既可通过说话内容表达，也体现在语气和声学方式中；仅用转写文本或语音单一模态可能漏掉互补线索。

Result: 模型分别用预训练多语言 BERT 和 wav2vec 2.0 编码完整视频转写与语音波形，并映射到共享表示空间。两层双向交叉注意力让语言 token 关注声学帧、声学帧也结合语言上下文，而无需显式时间对齐。随后通过掩码均值池化汇总序列，并结合拼接、逐元素乘积和绝对差进行二分类。在 BAH 数据集的受试者独立测试划分上，准确率为 72.19%、精确率 75.00%、召回率 81.13%、A/H F1 77.95%、Macro-F1 70.16%。

Insight: 显式双向跨模态注意可在不要求文本与音频帧精确对齐的情况下，联合利用语言语义和声学行为线索。摘要未提供单模态基线和不同说话人群体的分项表现。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are subtle behavioural states that can be expressed through both what a person says and how it is spoken. This paper presents an audio–text framework for video-level A/H recognition. Full-video transcripts and speech waveforms are encoded using pre-trained multilingual BERT and wav2vec~2.0, respectively, and projected into a shared representation space. Two stacked bidirectional cross-attention blocks allow linguistic tokens to attend to acoustic frames and acoustic frames to attend to linguistic context without requiring explicit temporal alignment. The resulting sequences are aggregated through masked mean pooling and combined using concatenation, element-wise products, and absolute differences for binary classification. We evaluate the model on the subject-independent split of the Behavioural Ambivalence/Hesitancy (BAH) dataset. The proposed system achieves 72.19% accuracy, 75.00% precision, 81.13% recall, 77.95% A/H F1-score, and 70.16% Macro-F1 on the test set. These results demonstrate the potential of jointly modelling linguistic and acoustic information for automatic A/H recognition.code at:https://github.com/DorriG/ECCV.

[2] Mixture of Phonetic Experts Based Low-Rank Adaptation of Conformer Models for Accented English Speech Recognition cs.CL | cs.SD | eess.ASPDF

Santosh Dahal, Anmol Guragain, Tianchi Liu, Luis Fernando D’Haro, Kiran Chandra Dahal

TL;DR: MoPE-LoRA 按发音方式而非口音身份组织六个共享低秩专家，并以音素监督与声学门控逐帧路由，适配带口音英语识别且不需口音专属模块；L2-ARCTIC 留一口音零样本测试中，比单一 LoRA 相对提高 12.3%。

Details

Motivation: 传统语音 MoE 通常按口音分配专家，默认口音是主要差异轴，因而新口音缺少专属模块时难迁移。论文认为口音差异往往表现为音素实现方式的系统性变化。

Result: Mixture of Phonetic Experts LoRA（MoPE-LoRA）使用六个固定低秩专家，对齐不同发音方式类别；混合路由结合音素监督和学习得到的声学门控，在帧级选择专家。专家集合跨口音共享，无需为每种口音单独建模块，因此可扩展到训练未见口音。L2-ARCTIC 实验中，MoPE-LoRA 持续超过单一 LoRA 和全量微调；留出一个口音进行零样本评测时，相比单一 LoRA 相对提升 12.3%。

Insight: 将适配单元对齐到发音类别而非口音标签，可能捕捉跨口音共享的音素变体规律，从而提高迁移性。摘要未给出各留出口音的绝对错误率或参数、计算节省量。

Abstract: We present Mixture of Phonetic Experts based Low-Rank Adaptation (MoPE-LoRA), a parameter-efficient adaptation framework for accented English speech recognition. Recent mixture-of-experts approaches typically allocate experts per accent, implicitly treating accent identity as the primary axis of variation. In contrast, we organize adaptation along phonetic categories, motivated by the observation that accent differences are often expressed as systematic distortions of phoneme realization. MoPE-LoRA introduces a fixed set of six low-rank experts aligned with manner-of-articulation classes and performs frame-level routing through a hybrid mechanism that combines phoneme supervision with learned acoustic gating. Because the expert set is shared across accents, the model does not require accent-specific modules and naturally extends to previously unseen accents. Experiments on L2-ARCTIC show that MoPE-LoRA consistently improves over single LoRA and full fine-tuning. In zero-shot evaluation where one accent is held out during training, the proposed method yields a 12.3% relative improvement over single LoRA. These results indicate that phonetic-category-based decomposition offers a scalable and transferable alternative to accent-level adaptation.

[3] AtomSD: Procedural Atom-Guided Self-Distillation for LLM Agents cs.CLPDF

Bohan Yu, Pengfei Cao, Kai Tian, Chen Han, Shi-Yang Li

TL;DR: AtomSD 把智能体过程知识拆为可独立复用的 Intent、When、Do、Avoid 四类程序原子，并从自身成功轨迹持续抽取、通过条件化监督和任务奖励蒸馏回策略；实验平均表现最高比已有先进基线高 9.31 分。

Details

Motivation: 复杂任务不仅需要事实与推理能力，也需掌握如何行动的程序知识。轨迹和打包技能通常绑定较长上下文或多项操作，难以在不同交互历史中灵活复用。

Result: Procedural Atoms 是细粒度、可独立应用的指导单元，分别描述意图、适用时机、执行动作和应避免事项。AtomSD 让学习者持续从自身成功轨迹中抽取这些原子，再通过原子条件化监督和任务奖励优化蒸馏到不断演进的策略。综合实验显示，AtomSD 一贯超过强奖励训练和蒸馏方法，平均表现最高比所报告的先进基线高 9.31 分。

Insight: 将过程性技能拆为可组合的意图、时机、动作和禁忌单元，可以比整段轨迹或打包技能更灵活地迁移。摘要未给出基准列表、分数范围或抽取原子的质量控制方式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) possess broad factual knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, but solving complex tasks also requires procedural knowledge about how to act. Existing approaches often represent procedural knowledge through trajectories or bundled skills, limiting flexible reuse across interaction histories. This paper introduces Procedural Atoms, fine-grained and independently applicable units that represent Intent, When, Do, and Avoid guidance. Building on this representation, we propose AtomSD, an Atom-guided Self-Distillation framework in which the learner continually extracts Procedural Atoms from its own successful trajectories and distills them into the evolving policy through Atom-conditioned supervision and task-reward optimization. Comprehensive experiments show that AtomSD consistently outperforms strong reward-based and distillation methods, exceeding the state-of-the-art baseline by up to 9.31 points in average performance. Further analyses demonstrate that the proposed Procedural Atoms provide effective guidance, highlighting the value of atom-level procedural knowledge for policy learning.

[4] Think Before You Transcribe: Context-Steered Reasoning for Hotword-Aware Speech Recognition cs.CLPDF

Qihao Wang, Hang Su, Fan Cui, Lichun Fan, Jian Luan

TL;DR: 该框架先生成有音频依据的短字幕，再判断候选 hotword 是否相关后进行转写，以避免不完整或错误候选词覆盖声学证据；监督微调建立字幕到转写行为，难例轨迹注入补强 GRPO，实验显示转写质量和热词识别均改善且对无关候选更稳健。

Details

Motivation: 上下文自动语音识别可通过候选热词提高罕见实体识别，但检索候选可能不完整或错误。系统需要利用真正相关的候选，同时不让无声学依据的备选覆盖音频内容。

Result: Think Before You Transcribe 提出上下文引导的推理流程，先基于音频生成短而有声学依据的字幕，再用该任务表示判断候选热词的相关性，最后确定词汇转写。监督微调先建立结构化的“字幕—转写”行为；针对难语句常出现组内所有 rollout 均失败的问题，方法再用定向轨迹注入强化组相对策略优化。多种上下文条件实验显示，方法持续改善转写质量与热词识别，并能抵御无关候选词。

Insight: 候选实体应先经过声学证据核验，再影响最终词汇决策；将“描述听到什么”与“从候选中选词”分开可降低上下文偏置。摘要未给出具体数据集、模型配置及量化增益。

Abstract: Contextual automatic speech recognition (ASR) improves rare-entity recognition by supplying candidate hotwords, but retrieved candidates may be incomplete or incorrect. A reliable system must therefore exploit a relevant candidate without allowing unsupported alternatives to override the acoustic evidence. We propose a context-steered reasoning framework that generates a short, audio-grounded caption before transcription. This task-specific representation lets the model assess candidate relevance before making a lexical decision. Supervised fine-tuning establishes the structured caption-to-transcription behavior, while targeted trajectory injection strengthens group relative policy optimization when difficult utterances yield only unsuccessful rollouts. Experiments across diverse context conditions show that the proposed method consistently improves both transcription quality and hotword recognition while remaining robust to irrelevant candidates.

[5] TRACE: Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation for Text-Vision Embedding Models cs.CLPDF

Pume Tuchinda, Tinnakit Udsa, Peerat Limkonchotiwat

TL;DR: TRACE 将 168 项文本—视觉嵌入任务拆为通用、可适配和挑战三类，揭示数据适配能提升可适配任务却难解决困难域：两个模型中适配分数分别提高 7.67 和 7.09 分，但 Challenge 仍低于 5 分，多语和视觉文档是主要短板。

Details

Motivation: 文本—视觉嵌入基准的总分常混合通用能力、任务专属适配收益和困难任务鲁棒性，无法看出模型究竟在哪类能力上不足。

Result: TRACE（Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation）从不同来源数据集中整理 168 项任务，划分为 General、Adaptability 和 Challenge 三组。对两个文本—视觉嵌入模型的控制实验显示，联合通用数据和适配数据使可适配组分别提升 7.67 和 7.09 分，同时大体保持通用组表现；但挑战组得分仍低于 5，增加训练数据带来的改善有限。进一步分析识别出多语言和视觉文档任务是主要弱项。

Insight: 训练数据扩充可以针对性改善常规适配，却不能替代解决多语言和视觉文档等挑战任务的方法创新；单一汇总分数会掩盖这种差异。摘要未提供各子任务具体分数或模型名称。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for text-vision embedding models report aggregate scores that obscure whether performance comes from general capabilities, task-specific adaptation, or robustness to challenging tasks. We introduce TRACE, a Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation framework that reorganizes 168 tasks from various sources of datasets into three categories: General, Adaptability, and Challenge. Through controlled experiments on two text-vision embedding models, we show that combining general and adaptation data improves Adaptability performance by 7.67 and 7.09 points, respectively, while largely preserving General performance. However, Challenge performance remains below 5.0, with limited improvements from additional training data. Further analysis identifies multilingual and visual-document tasks as major weaknesses. Our findings demonstrate that data adaptation alone is insufficient to address these challenges, highlighting the need for methodological advances beyond data-centric improvements.

[6] Fluent but Fallible: Benchmarking Hallucination and Factual Reliability in Large Language Models cs.CLPDF

Osama Shabih, Dayam Nadeem, Shahab Saquib Sohail, Mohammad Nadeem

TL;DR: 一项针对 10 个特定版本 LLM、70 条学术提示的人工评测显示，模型存在回答完整性与事实可靠性权衡：Perplexity 和 DeepSeek V3 总体准确率最高，Gemini 召回较强，而 Falcon 零幻觉但召回最低、回答偏离最大。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型虽提升推理、文本生成与知识检索，仍可能产生虚构事实或系统性偏离标准答案，影响学术研究可靠性。论文比较模型在不同复杂度学术问题上的准确、完整及幻觉表现。

Result: 研究评测十个模型，包括 ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、LLaMA 3、Mistral、Grok、Perplexity、DeepSeek V3、Falcon 和 Gemma。基准含 70 条精心设计学术提示，分基础、解释、高级和陷阱四个复杂度层级；每个回答由三位评审独立按准确率、精确率、召回率、回答偏离指数和幻觉指数评分。Perplexity 与 DeepSeek V3 总体准确率最高；Gemini 召回强，五项指标较均衡；LLaMA 3 以较保守生成降低幻觉。高级题对所有模型最难，结构为二元真伪的陷阱题更易处理。Falcon 达到完美精确率和零幻觉，但代价是召回最低、回答偏离最高。

Insight: 降低幻觉可能以遗漏信息和回答不完整为代价，模型可靠性需同时看准确、召回与偏离；陷阱题的二元结构也限制其代表性。评测基于 2025 年 2 月特定版本，不能直接视为当前版本排名。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced natural language processing and artificial intelligence applications through their capabilities in reasoning, text generation and knowledge retrieval. However, LLMs are still vulnerable to two important challenges: hallucination, generating factually incorrect or fabricated content, and response deviation, meaning systematic deviations from ground-truth responses. These limitations raise serious concerns regarding reliability and trustworthiness in academic and research environments. This study offers a thorough comparative analysis using a variety of metrics of ten advanced LLMs ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude3.5Sonnet, LLaMA3, Mistral, Grok, Perplexity, DeepSeekV3, Falcon, and Gemma based on a benchmark consisting of 70 carefully designed academic prompts across four complexity levels: Basic, Explanation, Advanced, and Tricky, evaluated in February 2025 on specific model versions. Each response was independently evaluated by three reviewers using five metrics: Accuracy, Precision, Recall, Response Deviation Index, and Hallucination Index. Perplexity and DeepSeekV3 achieved the highest overall accuracy, while Gemini demonstrated strong recall and a well-balanced performance profile across all five metrics. LLaMA3 showed strong resistance against hallucinations through a conservative method of generation. Advanced-level prompts proved most challenging across all models, while Tricky prompts were handled more reliably owing to their binary true/false structure. Falcon achieved perfect precision and zero hallucinations, but at the cost of the lowest recall and the highest response deviation. The results show a fundamental trade-off between response completeness and factual reliability, intensified by prompt complexity.

[7] Cross-Lingual Reusability of LLM Pipelines: Financial Extraction, Multilingual Reasoning, and Semantic Screening cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Joong Ho, Choi, Sherine George, Jiayang Zhao, Jayesh Rathi

TL;DR: 跨语言管线研究用支票抽取、九语言 Mintaka 问答和多语言 SEC 文件 FinanceBench 检验英文 LLM 流程的复用性；Gemini 3.5 Flash 抽取准确率为 95.2–97.6%，但推理预算收益依领域而异，引用集合与英文重叠率约 77–79%。

Details

Motivation: 金融机构在多语言市场部署 LLM 时，需判断英语开发验证过的管线能否跨语言复用，还是必须为每个地区重建。论文从准确率、推理努力迁移和行为稳定性三个方面进行评估。

Result: 研究设置三项任务：支票信息抽取；包含固定证据的 Mintaka 问答，覆盖九种语言和九种模型推理预算；以及在四种语言 SEC 10-K 文件上进行 50 项 FinanceBench 试点。Gemini 3.5 Flash 在不同提示语言下的抽取准确率为 95.2–97.6%；FinanceBench 各语言均值范围为 77.1–82.9%，跨度 5.8 个百分点，但语言难度排序跨领域反转（Hindi 在 Mintaka 最难，在 FinanceBench 最易，为 82.9%）。提高推理预算对一般知识问答仅有边际收益，对金融内容最多提升 8 个百分点，且英语中有效的预算配置在其他语言呈比例收益。与英文的引用集合 Jaccard 重叠平均为 77–79%，没有某种语言始终更差。

Insight: 英语管线具有一定跨语言可复用性，但准确率、推理预算收益和行为稳定性随领域变化；语义相似或模型自报置信度不能替代逐领域逐语言验证。金融基准仅 50 项试点，结论仍有限。

Abstract: Financial institutions increasingly deploy large language models (LLMs) in multilingual markets, raising a practical question: can pipelines developed and validated in English be reused across languages, or must they be rebuilt per locale? We test cross-lingual reusability along three dimensions–accuracy, reasoning-effort transfer, and behavioural stability–through three complementary tasks. Task 1 (cheque extraction) and Task 2 (Mintaka QA with pinned evidence across nine languages and nine model-effort configurations) test whether accuracy and effort settings transfer; Task 3 (FinanceBench pilot, 50 items over SEC 10-K filings in four languages) validates whether these patterns generalise to financial content. Accuracy reusability is broadly confirmed: Gemini 3.5 Flash achieves 95.2-97.6% extraction accuracy across prompt languages, and FinanceBench per-language means span 5.8pp (77.1-82.9%), though the language-difficulty ranking reverses between domains (Hindi is worst on Mintaka, best on FinanceBench at 82.9%). Effort reusability is domain-dependent: higher reasoning effort yields marginal gains on general-knowledge QA but up to 8pp on financial content, and effort configurations that help in English help proportionally in other languages. Behavioural reusability holds: citation-set overlap with English averages 77-79% Jaccard, with no single language consistently worse. Together, these results support qualified reuse of English-built pipelines, but per-domain, per-language validation remains necessary–neither semantic similarity nor self-reported confidence reliably predicts cross-lingual accuracy.

[8] Discrete Neural Interlingua for Ontology-Agnostic EHR Modeling cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Tong Ding, Caiwei Tian, Dongmin Bang, Ming Y. Lu, Sophia J. Wagner

TL;DR: MEDIATOR 将病历中的代码、别名和自由文本映射为共享离散语义码，缓解跨医院 EHR 词表不一致；在 MIMIC-III 及零样本 eICU 迁移任务上提升预测效果。

Details

Motivation: 电子病历基础模型常被封闭词表限制：同一临床概念在不同医院可能表现为不同本体代码、本地编号、同义词或自由文本。用 MIMIC-III 词表训练的模型只能覆盖 eICU 约 13% 的事件，导致跨系统迁移时信息大量丢失。

Result: 作者提出 MEDIATOR，以同义词监督及概念描述、代码共现和关系图为依据，将临床文本映射到共享的向量量化离散码本，无需人工映射或预先固定词表。作为 EHR Transformer 的输入接口，它在 MIMIC-III 20 项任务上的平均 AUPRC 较此前最强 tokenizer 高 4.0 点（相对提升 8%）；迁移到 eICU 14 项任务时高 8.6 点（相对提升 29%），且可使用全部事件。相同输入控制实验仍有 1.0 和 1.9 点优势；研究还报告跨医院归因可迁移。

Insight: 迁移收益同时来自事件覆盖率提升和概念表示改进。控制实验显示，文本编码器主要贡献域内准确率，而 MEDIATOR 的学习目标更能支撑迁移稳健性与实体链接质量。核心价值是把临床概念对齐变成开放词表 tokenization，而不是依赖逐院维护映射表。

Abstract: EHR foundation models promise scalable clinical prediction across health systems, yet their deployment is constrained by a tokenization bottleneck. The same clinical concept can appear as different ontology codes, local identifiers, synonyms, or free-text descriptions, which standard EHR tokenizers treat as unrelated symbols. A model trained on MIMIC-III with a closed-vocabulary tokenizer can use only 13% of eICU events. We introduce MEDIATOR, a discrete ontology-agnostic tokenizer that maps any clinical concept expressed as text into a shared vector-quantized codebook, turning EHR harmonization into open-vocabulary tokenization without manual mappings or a fixed vocabulary. Trained with synonym supervision and guided by enriched concept descriptions, code co-occurrence, and relation graphs, MEDIATOR collapses equivalent expressions while organizing distinct concepts in a medically meaningful discrete space. Used as the token interface of an EHR Transformer, MEDIATOR improves mean AUPRC over the strongest prior tokenizer by 4.0 points (8% relative) on 20 MIMIC-III tasks and by 8.6 points (29% relative) in zero-shot transfer to 14 eICU tasks, where it uses all events. The transfer gain combines coverage and representation, as MEDIATOR remains the best tokenizer under identical inputs (+1.0 and +1.9 points on operational and subcohort-mortality tasks). Same-encoder controls show that the text encoder drives in-domain accuracy, while the learned objectives of MEDIATOR drive transfer robustness and entity-linking quality. MEDIATOR also enables transferable attribution across hospitals.

[9] ICLHerd: Herding-Based Data Selection for LLMs with In-Context Learning as a Proxy cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Yuqiao Wen, Lili Mou

TL;DR: ICLHerd 用冯·米塞斯–费舍尔核平衡数据选择中的“贴近开发样本”与“覆盖多样模式”，并以上下文学习表现作为高效验证代理。

Details

Motivation: 大模型训练数据筛选常按开发样本的隐藏状态或梯度余弦相似度选取最相近数据。作者发现，这种最大相似度策略容易产生模式寻求偏差，使选出的样本紧密聚集在少数开发样本附近，不能覆盖目标能力的多样变化。

Result: ICLHerd 使用冯·米塞斯–费舍尔核设计数据选择框架，在模式寻求与模式平均之间调节；同时提出以模型的上下文学习效果作为验证代理，从而更高效地搜索这一平衡超参数。实验中，ICLHerd 优于已有大语言模型数据选择算法。摘要未给出具体数据集或提升幅度。

Insight: 好的训练数据既要与目标能力相关，也要覆盖其不同表现形态。把相似样本选择从单纯追求最近邻改为可调的集中与分散折中，可以缓解开发集过拟合式的样本聚集；上下文学习代理则减少了调参时对昂贵完整训练评估的依赖。

Abstract: Data selection plays a central role in modern large language model training, as the quality of training data strongly influences downstream performance. A common approach is to curate a development set that encodes the desired skills (e.g., reasoning or multilinguality) and select data that are similar to them. Existing methods compute similarity using cosine distance over either the model’s hidden states or gradients. However, we observe that this max-similarity strategy induces a mode-seeking bias: the selected data clusters tightly around the development samples. To address this, we propose ICLHerd, a data selection framework that leverages the von Mises-Fisher kernel to balance mode-seeking with mode-averaging. We further introduce in-context learning as a validation proxy, enabling efficient hyperparameter search over the balance trade-off. Results show that ICLHerd outperforms existing LLM data selection algorithms, highlighting the importance of balancing mode-seeking and mode-averaging.

[10] GRID: Modeling Health Influencer Discourse as Argument Networks for Credibility Assessment cs.CLPDF

Nishanth Sridhar Nakshatri, Eylon Caplan, Dan Goldwasser

TL;DR: GRID 将健康网红视频中的论点、结论和科学证据组织成跨视频论证网络，在 569 条专家评估视频上验证了利用整体话语关系评估可信度的价值。

Details

Motivation: 社交媒体健康内容通常被孤立地逐条判断，忽略不同创作者围绕同一议题提出的相互支持或冲突观点。单条内容的论据、核心结论及其科学证据关系，可能影响其整体可信度判断。

Result: GRID（基于互联话语的扎根推理）把每条视频建模为连接具体主张与中心结论的论证图，再跨视频关联相关主张和结论，以科学证据为依据联合推断可信度。端到端评估使用 569 条由专家给出评估的健康网红视频。结果表明，考虑更广泛的话语网络能提升内容验证；作者还用推断的可信度分析了高、低可信内容在论证策略与道德框架上的差异。

Insight: 健康信息核验可以从单条视频分类扩展到跨内容的集体推断：论点之间的呼应、冲突以及证据支撑共同构成判断依据。该方法把可解释的论证结构用于可信度分析，但摘要未报告具体准确率或提升数值。

Abstract: Health-related discussions on social media are increasingly shaped by influencers who offer diverse, and often competing perspectives on the same issue. Yet, such content is typically evaluated in isolation, without considering the broader discourse in which it appears. We introduce GRID (Grounded Reasoning over Interconnected Discourse), a framework for evaluating influencer content through collective inference over the surrounding discourse. GRID represents each video as an argumentation graph connecting claims to its central message, or takeaway, links related claims and takeaways across videos, grounds claims in scientific evidence, and jointly infers credibility over the resulting network. We evaluate GRID on an end-to-end health-related content validation task using 569 influencer videos grounded in expert assessments. Our results show that accounting for the broader discourse improves influencer content validation. Finally, we use GRID-inferred credibility to demonstrate how argumentative strategies and moral framing differ between low- and high-credibility content.

[11] On the Fragility of Tasks under Composition: Implicit Budgeting in Language Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Chenpan Li, Thies Brockmoeller, Stefanie Jegelka, Sharut Gupta, Nikolaos Karalias

TL;DR: 语言模型把多个任务放进同一提示时会隐式压缩每项任务的思考预算，较长推理任务更脆弱；按脆弱性分组可节省 43% token 并保持准确率。

Details

Motivation: 模型常被要求在一条提示中完成多个子任务并整合答案。研究发现，即便每个任务单独都能可靠完成，组合后错误也显著增加，且不能仅用各任务独立错误累加来解释。

Result: 作者在多种开放权重模型及 Claude Opus 5 上观察到组合任务的隐式思考预算效应：任务组合时每项任务获得的 token 明显少于单独求解，即使总序列远未达到上下文窗口上限。单独求解时需要较长推理过程的任务，在组合情形下更易出错。按脆弱性分组后，模型可在数学和语言任务上减少 43% token，同时保持准确率。

Insight: 组合提示会改变模型分配给各子任务的推理资源；任务间干扰不只是错误概率相加，而与隐式预算压缩有关。统一削减推理可能伤害需要长推理的任务，因此根据任务脆弱性分组，有机会同时减少冗余计算并保住准确性。

Abstract: Modern language models are increasingly asked to handle several tasks within a single prompt. Doing so requires solving each task correctly while integrating the resulting answers into a coherent response. In this paper, we identify a fundamental limitation in this process, where models make substantially more errors when tasks are composed, even when they can solve every constituent task reliably in isolation. This degradation cannot be fully explained by the accumulation of independent errors. Across both open-weight models and the frontier model Claude Opus 5, our findings instead point to an implicit thinking budget underlying this effect. In particular, under composition, models use substantially fewer tokens per task than in isolation, even when the full sequence remains well within the context window. Tasks that require longer solution traces in isolation are consequently more $\textit{fragile}$ under composition, i.e., models make more errors on prompts containing these tasks. In isolation, non-fragile tasks receive more reasoning than they need and waste compute. However, by grouping tasks according to their fragility, the implicit budgeting can be manipulated to reduce token usage by $43$% across a range of math and linguistic tasks while also preserving accuracy.

[12] The Culture Within: Towards Mechanistic Understanding of Cultural Reasoning in LLMs cs.CLPDF

Samee Arif, Furkan Danisman, Angana Borah, Honglak Lee, Dehan Kong

TL;DR: CultureLens 显示模型在缺乏明确国家信息时倾向默认英语文化规范，也会轻信检索到的错误规范；用反事实规范样例微调可将对齐率提升到 85–93%。

Details

Motivation: 现有文化能力评测多关注模型最终输出，较难辨析模型如何使用国家、价值观及规范证据。研究希望区分文化知识缺失与模型处理相互矛盾规范信息时的机制性错误，并考察检索内容可能带来的风险。

Result: CultureLens 包含来自 75 个国家的 9,261 个场景，系统改变国家、价值观、真实规范及矛盾规范证据。在 Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-3-12B 和 Qwen-3.5-27B 上，加入矛盾规范会使符合文化规范的回答下降 32–47 个百分点，即使模型仅凭国家信息原本答对。无国家提示时模型倾向英语文化规范；阻断模型内部的规范信息后，多数情况下可恢复正确答案。监督微调加入矛盾规范样例后，在矛盾规范情境中的对齐率升至 85–93%，并能泛化到未见国家，但真实规范场景有小幅代价。

Insight: 模型受错误规范影响并非单纯因为缺少文化知识：它会按规范信息的呈现方式接受内容，而不核实其真实性，因此检索到的文化信息可能诱发错误。评估和训练需区分国家先验、证据可信度与矛盾信息处理，并关注纠错能力在真实规范情境中的权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate across culturally diverse settings, yet existing evaluations largely measure cultural behavior at the output level. We introduce CultureLens, a reference-grounded benchmark and mechanistic study of how LLMs use cultural information. CultureLens contains 9,261 scenarios across 75 countries and systematically varies country, value, true-norm, and contradictory-norm evidence. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-3-12B, and Qwen-3.5-27B, stating a contradictory norm lowers aligned answers by 32-47 percentage points, even when the model answers correctly from the country alone. Mechanistically, models default to Anglophone norms when no country is given. The failure under a contradictory norm is not one of missing knowledge, as blocking the norm inside the model restores the correct answer in most cases. Models accept a stated norm according to how it is presented and not whether it is true, which leaves them most exposed to errors in retrieved content. Supervised fine-tuning with contradictory-norm examples raises aligned answers under a contradictory norm to 85-93%, including on unseen countries, at a small cost under a true norm.

Details

Motivation: 推理链和推理图被用于概率推断、事实核查和不确定性估计，但这些结构本身是否可靠很少被直接评估。若推理图的节点或边构造错误，下游基于图的判断可能受到影响。

Result: 作者在开放式多跳问答中设计两阶段诊断。第一阶段检验构造推理图所需能力，发现图可靠性取决于图拓扑、提示设计和模型家族。第二阶段以不确定性估计作为下游任务：使用模型生成边时，图计算对上游图错误敏感，相较真值图设置性能下降；使用真值图时，仅把图作为提示上下文带来的收益有限，但它能改善置信度对正确与错误答案的区分，并支持节点级诊断。

Abstract: Reasoning structures (e.g., chains and graphs) are increasingly used for probabilistic inference, factuality verification, and uncertainty estimation, but their structural reliability is rarely evaluated directly. Stage 2 uses uncertainty estimation as a downstream test case. Generated-edge experiments show that graph-based computation is sensitive to upstream graph errors, with performance dropping relative to the gold-graph setting. Gold-graph experiments further show that graph structure provides little benefit when used only as prompt context, but improves the separation of correct from incorrect answers by confidence score and enables node-level diagnostics.

[16] Auditing Safety Preference Supervision with Recorded Derivations cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LG | cs.SCPDF

Chenhan Lyu, Amir Rahmani

TL;DR: 符号化偏好标注把提示属性、显式规则和固定裁决顺序记录成可重算的偏好推导；研究区分了标签可复现性与属性判断正确性，并报告有害提示上的结构化输出失败。

Details

Motivation: 偏好标签用于对齐模型训练，但其背后的判断过程通常不易检查或复核。若记录构造偏好的中间决策，就能重算最终标签；不过这仍不能自动保证模型分类的属性正确或语义安全。

Result: 符号化偏好标注让预训练语言模型分类提示和回答的预定义属性，再由显式规则给出回答裁决，并按固定裁决排序生成偏好标签。保存的属性赋值、规则和排序足以重新计算裁决和标签，无需再次调用模型。200 条人工标注提示中，模型对 192 条生成有效结构化输出；8 条解析失败全部出现在标记为有害的自伤提示子集中。研究以 465 对偏好数据、五个训练随机种子进行 DPO；在 1,319 条类似不安全请求的良性提示上，平均测得良性拒绝率为 40.82%，在 655 条有害提示上，平均毒性拒绝率为 84.98%。

Abstract: Preference labels align model training, but the judgments behind them are difficult to inspect or verify. In this paper, we examine how preference construction can be checked when intermediate decisions are recorded, and which of them remain uncertain. We present Symbolic Preference Labeling, a method in which a pretrained language model classifies predefined attributes of prompts and responses. Explicit rules assign response verdicts, and a fixed verdict ranking determines the preference label. Each preference label has a recorded derivation: the response verdicts and preference label can be recomputed from the stored attribute assignments, rules, and verdict ranking without rerunning the language model. On 200 prompts annotated by human raters, the language model produces valid structured outputs for 192 prompts. All eight parsing failures occur in the subset of self-harm prompts marked as harmful. Using 465 preference pairs, we train one policy model with direct preference optimization across five training seeds. Mean measured benign refusal is 40.82 percent on 1,319 benign prompts that resemble unsafe ones, and mean measured toxic refusal is 84.98 percent on 655 toxic prompts. This study distinguishes the reproducibility of preference labels from attribute correctness. The recorded derivations make preference construction open to inspection, while attribute correctness and semantic safety require separate validation.

Motivation: 现有 steering 基准多使用简化、孤立的生成任务，只测单一行为属性，与真实部署中的复杂性和多样性交互不符。因此在这些基准上的效果难以说明控制方法在实际场景中能否稳定工作。

Result: RealControlBench 评测 LLM 和 LLM 智能体的细粒度控制，覆盖指令遵循、工具使用决策、推理表现、推理长度和轨迹条件化安全意识五个维度，并包含执行落地的多轮智能体交互。它同时测量目标可控性与能力保留，包括指令遵循、推理、下游工具效用和安全性，还考察效果对提示变化、领域和架构是否稳健。实验发现，现有方法难以稳定诱导目标行为，且可能显著损害底层任务表现。

Abstract: Existing steering benchmarks are built around simplified, isolated generation tasks and evaluate a single behavioral attribute under controlled settings that do not reflect the diversity and complexity of practical deployment. As a result, performance on these benchmarks provides only a limited picture of how steering methods perform in the real world. We therefore introduce RealControlBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating fine-grained steering of LLMs and LLM agents across diverse behavioral targets and interaction settings. RealControlBench extends steering evaluation beyond isolated generations to complex, execution-grounded, multi-turn agent interactions, covering five complementary dimensions: instruction-following control, agentic tool-use decision control, reasoning-performance steering, reasoning-length control, and trajectory-conditioned safety awareness. Rather than measuring steering success alone, the benchmark jointly evaluates target controllability and capability preservation, including instruction following, reasoning performance, and downstream agent utility and safety. It further examines whether steering effects remain specific and robust across prompt variations, domains, and model architectures. Our evaluation shows that existing steering methods remain unreliable across realistic control objectives: they often fail to consistently induce the intended behavior and can substantially degrade the model’s underlying task performance. These results suggest that strong steering effects in controlled settings do not necessarily translate into reliable control in more realistic evaluations. By providing a unified, model-agnostic steering evaluation framework, RealControlBench aims to support the development of steering methods that are not only effective but also precise, robust, and useful in realistic AI deployments.

TL;DR: 该提示优化框架把反思候选生成与完整验证预算分开：先用小批量筛选并行构造候选，再按固定预算选优；五个基准上最高提升 11.22 个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 反思式提示优化利用任务反馈改写指令，但若候选生成和筛选都依赖完整验证集，会使搜索成本高且流程串行。候选池规模与验证预算也因此难以独立调节。

Result: 两阶段方法先从多个父提示并行展开反思分支，重复利用父提示的训练响应，并仅在小训练批次上与父提示比较筛选局部改进候选；候选池冻结后，再由探索策略在固定的完整验证预算内选择得分最高的提示。作者在五个基准、Qwen3.5-9B 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上评估；保留 100 个候选并进行 20 次完整验证时，相较初始求解器最高提升 11.22 个百分点。学习式选择在十组比较中均超过初始求解器，但随机选择与学习式选择之间没有一种始终占优。

Insight: 将候选探索和昂贵的完整验证分离，可独立设置搜索广度与验证次数，并利用低成本局部筛选扩展候选池。实验支持这一预算拆分思路，但候选如何排序仍有改进空间，不同选择策略并无稳定赢家。

Abstract: Reflective prompt optimization improves instructions using task feedback, but coupling candidate generation with full-validation-based selection can make search costly and sequential. We propose a two-stage framework that decouples reflective candidate-pool construction from validation allocation. During construction, we sample multiple parents and reuse each parent’s training responses across parallel reflection branches. Each revision is screened against its parent on a small training minibatch, allowing locally improved candidates to support further revisions without full validation. After construction, an exploration policy selects candidates from the frozen pool under a fixed full-validation budget, and the prompt with the highest measured validation score is returned. This separation allows candidate-pool size and validation budget to be set independently. We evaluate random selection and learned score prediction with similarity-based exploration on five benchmarks using Qwen3.5-9B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. With 100 retained candidates and 20 full validations, our framework improves over the initial solver by up to 11.22 percentage points. Learned selection improves over the initial solver in all ten comparisons, although neither selection policy consistently outperforms the other. These results support separating reflective search from budgeted validation while leaving room for stronger candidate-selection policies.

TL;DR: SHAM-Bench 汇总六类文本生成任务的 6,461 个样本，统一评估跨度级幻觉检测；微调检测器域内表现较好但遇分布变化会退化，带推理的提示法跨任务更稳健。

Details

Motivation: 跨度级幻觉检测要定位输出中缺少依据的具体片段，便于提供可执行反馈。但现有评估在任务、标注格式和指标上彼此割裂，多数数据集只覆盖一种文本生成领域，难以判断检测方法能否跨任务泛化。

Result: SHAM-Bench 将现有人类标注数据按统一模式和评估流程整合，覆盖六种文本生成任务、共 6,461 个样本。基准比较发现，微调检测器往往在域内表现较好，但遇到分布变化时性能下降；带有推理过程的提示方法跨任务更稳健。细微的无依据内容仍难识别，跨度边界模糊和标签噪声也持续影响评估。

Abstract: Span-level hallucination detection pinpoints unsupported content in LLM outputs, enabling actionable feedback and targeted mitigation. Existing evaluations for span-level hallucination detection methods are fragmented across tasks, annotation formats, and metrics, and most span-level datasets cover only a single NLG domain, making it difficult to assess whether a detection method actually generalizes across tasks and domains. In this paper, we introduce SHAM-Bench (Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks), a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection spanning six NLG tasks, aggregated from existing human-annotated datasets totaling 6,461 examples under a common schema and evaluation protocol. Using SHAM-Bench, we evaluate existing hallucination detection methods and find that fine-tuned detectors often perform well in-domain but degrade under distribution shift, whereas prompt-based methods with reasoning are more robust across tasks. Our analysis further shows that subtle unsupported content remains difficult to detect and span-level evaluation is still challenged by ambiguous span boundaries and noisy labels. We release the dataset, evaluation code, and a public leaderboard to support future research on broadly generalizable hallucination detection.

[20] DStill: Trajectory Consistency Distillation for Accelerated One-Step Drafting in Block Diffusion Speculative Decoding cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Shreyas Kulkarni, Uttam Gurram, Madhav Kanda, Krishna Teja Chitty Venkata, Shichu Chen

TL;DR: DStill 将目标条件 drafter 的多步迭代细化轨迹蒸馏到单步草拟中，在三类模型的推理、数学和代码基准上提高接受长度与加速比。

Details

Motivation: 块扩散推测解码可并行草拟多个 token，但离散扩散的一步草拟让各位置都从同一掩码块预测，彼此不能条件化于同一步其他位置选出的 token，容易产生不一致草稿并降低接受长度。迭代细化可改善草稿，却增加推理轮次和延迟。

Result: DStill 分两阶段将目标条件 drafter 的多步细化能力蒸馏到一步：先训练 drafter 补全部分揭示的块，再利用其细化轨迹训练学生模型一次性预测最终补全，并通过课程逐渐从部分掩码过渡到完全掩码输入。在三个模型家族的推理、数学和代码基准上，接受长度较单步 DFlash 最多提高 28%，加速比最多提高 25%；相较自回归解码，最高达到 6.45 倍加速。

Insight: 多轮细化能利用已提交 token 逐步校正草稿，关键挑战是保留这类条件化收益而不承担多轮代价。用 drafter 自身的细化轨迹监督学生的一次预测，可将迭代能力压缩进单步推理；报告的收益覆盖多种任务，但摘要中的最高增益不代表每项设置都达到同等幅度。

Abstract: Block-diffusion speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting multiple tokens in parallel. However, one-step drafting in discrete diffusion is limited by token factorization error: each token is predicted from the same masked block, without conditioning on the tokens selected at other positions in that step. This can produce inconsistent drafts and reduce acceptance length. Iterative refinement mitigates this limitation by conditioning later predictions on previously committed tokens, though additional passes increase drafting cost. To address this trade-off between draft fidelity and inference latency, we introduce DStill, a two-stage framework that distills a target-conditioned drafter’s own multi-step refinement into one-step drafting. We first train the drafter to complete partially revealed blocks, then use its refinement trajectories to train a student to predict the final completion in one pass. Using a curriculum that gradually transitions from partial to fully masked inputs, we successfully transfer the multi-step refinement capabilities into one step. Across reasoning, math, code benchmarks on three model families DStill improves acceptance length by up to 28% and speedup by up to 25% over one-step DFlash, achieving up to 6.45x speedup over auto-regressive decoding.

Guiquan Sun, Bing Bai, Martin Renqiang Min, Dongjin Song

Details

Motivation: 生成式奖励模型会用自然语言解释偏好判断，但结论正确不保证理由充分、真实地支持结论。现有方法常需成本高昂的参考解释，也未必能验证理由是否与最终判断一致。

Result: DeCert 在无需参考解释的情况下评估生成理由的有效性及其与判断结论的一致性，并以结论正确性和理由质量共同构造偏好监督。当多个采样判断都给出正确结论时，证书质量差异可构成偏好对，区分更强和更弱的理由。在两个模型家族及多个域内、分布外基准上，DeCert 一贯超过强生成式裁判基线并提升理由质量；位置一致准确率最高增加 14.8 个百分点，也能缓解位置偏差。

Abstract: Generative reward models (GenRMs) generate natural language rationales to justify preference judgments, yet a correct verdict does not guarantee a sound justification. Existing approaches often rely on reference explanations to supervise rationales, but obtaining such references can be costly and does not necessarily establish whether generated rationales support the resulting verdict. To address these limitations, we propose DeCert, a framework that evaluates the validity and verdict consistency of generated rationales without requiring reference explanations. Crucially, DeCert translates these assessments into preference supervision that accounts for both verdict correctness and justification quality. In particular, when multiple sampled judgments share the same correct verdict, differences in certificate quality enable DeCert to construct rationale preference pairs that distinguish stronger from weaker justifications. Extensive empirical studies across two model families and multiple in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks demonstrate that DeCert consistently outperforms strong generative judge baselines while improving rationale quality. Moreover, DeCert effectively mitigates positional bias, improving position-consistent accuracy by up to 14.8 points. These results highlight the importance of supervising not only which verdict is correct, but also how that verdict is justified.

[24] TeCoP: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Model Decoding via Tentative Context Pipelining cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: TeCoP 将扩散语言模型中“预测可作上下文”和“可提交为最终 token”分开，用暂定跨块上下文流水线复用稳定预测；四种 dLLM 上最多减少 87% 前向评估并实现最高 11.77 倍吞吐。

Details

Motivation: 扩散大语言模型每次前向可预测多个位置，但块式解码仍串行生成，并反复计算已经稳定的预测。作者认为低效来自把预测是否足够可靠、可不可逆提交，与其能否作为后续上下文这两个角色耦合起来。

Result: 免训练解码器 TeCoP 通过交错的块并行流水线和 draft-and-verify，把两种角色解耦：稳定但未提交的预测可临时作为跨块上下文，是否提交则由置信度和稳定性检查控制。在四种 LLaDA 与 Dream 扩散语言模型、GSM8K、Minerva MATH 和 MBPP 上，前向评估次数最多减少 87%；相较未缓存的原始解码器，不使用 KV cache 时吞吐最高达 7.50 倍，使用缓存时最高达 11.77 倍。12 组模型—基准设置中，吞吐均超过 Fast-dLLM 和 DSB；启用缓存时平均准确率与原解码器相差不超过 1 个百分点。

Insight: 预测尚未达到不可逆提交条件时，仍可先作为临时上下文供其他块使用，从而避免重复等待稳定预测。通过置信度与稳定性共同控制提交，TeCoP 在降低前向次数的同时维持准确率接近原模型。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) predict multiple token positions per forward pass, yet block-wise decoders serialize generation and repeatedly recompute stable predictions. We attribute this inefficiency to coupling a prediction’s readiness for irreversible commitment with its utility as context. We introduce Tentative Context Pipelining (TeCoP), a training-free decoder that decouples these roles through staggered block-parallel pipelining and draft-and-verify. TeCoP reuses stable but uncommitted predictions as provisional cross-block context, with commitment governed by confidence and stability checks. Across four LLaDA and Dream dLLMs on GSM8K, Minerva MATH, and MBPP, TeCoP reduces number of forward evaluations by up to 87% and achieves up to 7.50-fold throughput without KV caching and 11.77-fold with caching over the uncached original decoder. [26] PAR: Progress-Aligned Recurrence for Length Generalization in State Tracking cs.CLPDF

Pan Wang, Yihao Hu, Guangyuan Dong, Yitong Lin, Hang Wang

TL;DR: PAR 用与操作步数对齐的中间监督和循环状态范数锚定，让循环语言模型把测试深度外推到更长状态跟踪序列；S5 交换任务上可从最多 32 步训练泛化到 512 步。

Details

Motivation: 循环语言模型可在测试时增加迭代次数，但更多迭代不一定帮助处理更长问题。序列状态跟踪若只监督最终答案，无法告诉每一轮应推进多少操作；反复更新还可能改变循环状态的尺度。

Result: Progress-Aligned Recurrence（PAR）训练第 k 次迭代的预测匹配前 k 步操作后的状态，并将循环状态重标到第一次迭代的范数。在 swap-S5 上，最多用 32 次移动训练的 Qwen2.5-0.5B 在 512 步（训练长度 16 倍）达到 95.5% 平均完整状态准确率；同骨干 RLT 为 55.7%，显式思维链为 0.8%。消融显示去除范数锚定后外推倍数从 16 倍降至不足 2.2 倍；把进度对齐监督换成最终答案监督则最多只能外推 8 倍。模型还学习何时结束而无需提供停止深度；缺少中间标签时，可用前缀自蒸馏从最终答案诱导对应关系。

Insight: 让每次递归对应明确的操作进度，并稳定循环状态尺度，能把增加推理深度转化为更长序列处理能力。两项设计都对长度外推至关重要；在没有中间状态标注时，前缀自蒸馏提供仅依赖最终答案的替代监督。

Abstract: Looped language models can run more iterations at test time, but additional iterations need not help them solve longer problems. For sequential state tracking, final-answer supervision does not specify how far each iteration should advance, and repeated updates can change the scale of the recurrent state. We introduce \textbf{Progress-Aligned Recurrence (PAR)}, which trains the prediction at iteration $k$ to match the state after the first $k$ operations and rescales recurrent states to their first-iteration norms. On swap-$S_5$, a Qwen2.5-0.5B model trained on at most 32 moves achieves 95.5% mean full-state accuracy at 512 moves, or $16\times$ the training length, compared with 55.7% reported for RLT and 0.8% for explicit chain of thought on the same backbone. Controlled $S_5$ ablations show that removing norm anchoring reduces the extrapolation factor from $16\times$ to below $2.2\times$, and replacing progress-aligned targets with final-answer supervision reduces it to at most $8\times$. A learned completion readout selects when to answer without a supplied stopping depth. When intermediate labels are unavailable, prefix self-distillation induces the correspondence from shorter problem prefixes using only final answers. The gains apply across sequential state-tracking tasks, establishing the task structure required for depth extrapolation. These findings support training intermediate predictions and state scale together to make additional recurrent depth useful for longer problems.

[27] Memory as Code: Declarative Retrieval over Codified Long-Term Memory cs.CLPDF

Aoting Zeng, Yuxin Du, Jianwei Wang, Yizhang He, Shan Jiang

TL;DR: Memory as Code 将长期记忆构造成可执行的分层代码抽象，并把查询转成声明式检索程序；在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval 上，LLM 评审准确率相对最强基线最高提升 15.91%，且减少 token 消耗。

Details

Motivation: 长期记忆系统要从交互历史构建记忆并按查询检索，帮助智能体推理。复杂场景中事实分散在多轮会话，关系还会随时间变化，现有记忆系统难以灵活组合信息以适应多样查询。

Result: Memory as Code（MaC）用共享代码抽象联合设计记忆构建与检索：构建阶段形成可执行记忆抽象；检索阶段把问题转为结构化检索程序。记忆由原子函数层、关系层和模块组合层三层组成，检索从基于相关度挑选事实转为对记忆执行声明式查询。在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval 上，整体 LLM 评审准确率相较各设置下最强基线最高相对提升 15.91%，同时显著降低 token 使用量。

Insight: 把记忆中的事实及关系编码成可组合的可执行结构，有利于跨会话组合信息；把问题转成声明式查询，也比逐条按相似度检索更适合复杂关系查询。其收益同时体现在回答评估和 token 成本，但摘要未列明不同基准上的各项单独数值。

Abstract: In long-term memory systems, memories are constructed from interaction histories and retrieved in response to queries, enhancing the reasoning ability of LLM agents. In complex scenarios, facts are scattered across sessions and their relationships evolve over time, making it difficult for existing memory systems to compose them to adapt to diverse queries. Motivated by the modularity and composability of code, we propose Memory as Code (MaC), a unified memory framework that jointly designs memory construction and retrieval through a shared code abstraction. MaC couples codified construction, which establishes executable memory abstractions, with declarative retrieval, which translates queries into structured retrieval programs. Specifically, codified construction builds a three-layer executable memory structure that progresses from an atomic function layer, through a relational layer, to a modular composition layer. Declarative retrieval recasts retrieval from relevance-based fact selection into declarative query processing over memory. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show relative gains of up to 15.91% in overall LLM-judge accuracy over the strongest baseline in each setting, while substantially reducing token consumption, highlighting the effectiveness of memory as code for long-horizon memory tasks.

Details

Motivation: LLM 信心信号可用于检索、计算分配、路由和选择性弃答，但不同解题阶段的信心是否包含不同认知信息、以及这种可靠性是否超越英语环境，尚待检验。

Result: 受元记忆理论启发，作者区分解题前的预期信心（Feeling-of-Knowing，FOK）和解题后的回顾信心（Judgement-of-Learning，JOL），在两种前沿 LLM、多类推理领域、翻译成马拉地语的基准及新建的本地马拉地语事实问题上评估。较便宜的 FOK 校准较弱；成本更高的 JOL 通常与认知不确定性对齐更好。直接语言翻译后，英语与马拉地语的校准趋势大体保留；但针对本地马拉地语知识的问题上校准显著退化。模型生成幻觉回答后仍持续表现出偏高信心。

Abstract: Large language models are expected to estimate their own uncertainty, with confidence signals used to guide retrieval, adaptive compute allocation, routing, and selective abstention. We investigate whether confidence signals measured at different temporal stages of problem solving encode distinct forms of epistemic information and remain reliable beyond English-centric settings. Inspired by classical meta-memory theory, we distinguish anticipatory pre-solve confidence (Feeling-of-Knowing, FOK) from retrospective post-solve confidence (Judgement-of-Learning, JOL). We evaluate these signals across two frontier LLMs, multiple reasoning domains, translated Marathi benchmarks, and a newly constructed benchmark of genuinely local Marathi factual questions. Across settings, the cheaper pre-solve signal remains weakly calibrated, while post-solve confidence generally provides stronger epistemic alignment despite its additional inference cost. Confidence behaviour transfers surprisingly well under direct language translation, with calibration trends largely preserved between English and Marathi, but degrades substantially on local Marathi knowledge, suggesting that linguistic transfer alone is insufficient for region-specific information needs. Finally, hallucinated responses maintain persistently elevated confidence after answer generation, highlighting important limitations of current LLM self-assessment mechanisms.

Abstract: Recent agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating images from textual prompts, motivating their utilization for scientific diagram generation. Ultimately, SciDiagBench enables more reliable automated evaluation that better aligns with human evaluation across diverse scientific diagrams.

Details

Motivation: 概念联想是人类交流的重要组成，但当抽象视觉刺激越来越难与熟悉语义概念对应时，人和模型如何调整描述策略尚不清楚。研究从原创性和有效性两方面比较人类与多模态大模型。

Abstract: We investigate associative creativity in descriptions of abstract visual stimuli by humans and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). While conceptual association is central to human communication, it remains unclear how humans and models adapt when visual stimuli become increasingly difficult to map onto familiar semantic concepts. [31] Adaptive Stopping of Streamed Reasoning for Language Model Agents cs.CLPDF

JunHyeok Oh, Byung-Jun Lee

TL;DR: StreamStop 从推理文本前缀学习模型专属的停止策略，无需访问隐藏状态或 token 概率；在 WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 中比多数固定预算方案成功率更高且比完整思考耗费更少 token。

Details

Motivation: 启用推理的语言模型智能体在采取行动前可能消耗大量计算，而不同决策需要的推理长度不同。置信度或答案稳定性等停止信号随模型而变；隐藏状态和 token 概率又需要可能不可用的内部访问权限。

Result: StreamStop 用离线前缀级动作一致性学习模型专属停止策略：冻结的前缀读取器编码可见推理文本，再由轻量停止头判断何时结束，无需访问推理模型内部信号。作者发布带前缀动作标注的智能体推理轨迹数据，比较各前缀动作与完整推理参考动作，并据此离线比较规则、精确优化折扣动作一致性目标。停止头以折扣因子为条件，推理时可调节一致性与 token 成本的权衡而无需重训。在 WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 闭环评估中，StreamStop 使用少于完整思考的推理 token，并比多数验证集选出的固定预算获得更高观测成功率；WebShop 的 Qwen3.8-27B 每回合少用 42% 推理 token，任务成功率相近。

Insight: 只读取已经生成的推理前缀即可学习何时停止，不必依赖模型内部概率或隐藏状态，便于用于封闭或难以访问内部信号的模型。前缀动作与完整推理动作的一致性把停止决策转化为可离线训练和调节的目标。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled language model agents can incur substantial computation before acting, although the reasoning needed varies across decisions. Existing stopping signals, such as confidence and intermediate-answer stability, can vary in effectiveness across reasoning models; signals obtained from hidden states or token probabilities also require internal access that may be unavailable or impractical. We introduce StreamStop, which learns model-specific stopping policies offline from prefix-level action agreement. A frozen prefix reader encodes available reasoning text for a lightweight stop head, allowing stopping without access to the reasoning model’s internal signals. We construct and publicly release a dataset of agent reasoning trajectories with prefix-level action annotations. These annotations compare each prefix action with the full-reasoning reference action, enabling offline comparison of stopping rules and exact expected-return optimization of a discounted action-agreement objective. Conditioning the head on the discount factor allows the agreement-token trade-off to be adjusted at inference without retraining. In closed-loop evaluations on WebShop and ScienceWorld, StreamStop achieves higher observed success than most validation-selected fixed-budget settings while using fewer reasoning tokens than Full thinking. On WebShop with Qwen3.8-27B, it uses 42% fewer reasoning tokens per episode with similar observed task success.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) consolidates expert capabilities into a single student by matching teacher and student final-layer distributions on student-generated rollouts. Our diagnostic study finds that stronger teacher–student agreement in intermediate transitions is associated with higher answer correctness within questions, with correct responses exhibiting lower mean transition KL across all measured intermediate layer pairs. These observations motivate On-Policy Transition Distillation (OPTD), which augments final-layer distribution matching by aligning teacher and student layer-to-layer log-probability increments on student-generated rollouts. Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) depend fundamentally on the diversity, depth, and factual rigor of their pre-training corpora. [34] Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment for LLM Reasoning over Temporal Knowledge Graphs cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jiapu Wang

TL;DR: TEPCA 在证据和参数两层联合对齐时间知识图谱推理：按时间约束剪枝连贯证据路径，并用时间戳条件化 DG-LoRA 适配关系演化；两个图谱基准上达到 SOTA。

Details

Motivation: LLM 的静态参数记忆与现实知识随时间演化不匹配。RAG 常重语义相关、轻时间有效性和事件顺序，检索到的事实可能各自相关但时序碎片化；常规 PEFT 使用固定、与输入无关的低秩更新，也难表达实体语义和关系随时间漂移。

Result: TEPCA 从证据与参数两层对齐 LLM 和时序知识。证据侧以时间约束动态相关路径剪枝保留全局连贯时间路径，并用互信息剪枝筛选时间约束下与查询相关的局部事实。参数侧采用动态门控 LoRA（DG-LoRA），根据时间戳动态调节有效低秩适配子空间。在两个时序知识图谱基准上，TEPCA 达到最先进表现；DG-LoRA 在八个常识问答数据集上的补充评估进一步验证输入自适应能力、跨骨干扩展性和可解释性。

Insight: 时间推理的证据检索需要维护事件顺序与演化依赖，参数适配也应随时间上下文变化。联合处理检索连贯性和低秩参数子空间，可同时减少“证据相关但时间不一致”与“模型关系语义静态化”的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle with temporally grounded reasoning because their static parametric memory is misaligned with the dynamic evolution of real-world knowledge. Although RAG and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) provide practical ways to enhance LLMs, they remain insufficient for temporal reasoning. Existing retrieval methods typically prioritize semantic relevance while overlooking temporal validity, event order, and evolutionary dependencies, resulting in evidence that is relevant in isolation but temporally fragmented. Meanwhile, conventional PEFT methods usually employ fixed and input-agnostic low-rank updates, making it difficult to capture time-dependent entity semantics and relation drift. To address these limitations, we propose Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment (TEPCA), a framework for LLM reasoning over TKGs. TEPCA aligns LLM reasoning with temporal knowledge at both the evidence and parameter levels. At the evidence level, it introduces time-constrained Dynamic Relevance-Guided Path (DRGP) pruning to preserve globally coherent temporal paths, together with Mutual-Information (MI)-based pruning to retain query-informative local facts under temporal constraints. At the parameter level, it integrates Dynamic-Gated LoRA (DG-LoRA), a timestamp-conditioned low-rank adaptation mechanism that dynamically modulates the effective adaptation subspace according to temporal context. This design enables LLMs to better capture fine-grained entity evolution and time-dependent relation semantics while preserving parameter efficiency. Experimental results on two TKG benchmarks demonstrate that TEPCA achieves state-of-the-art performance. Additional evaluations of DG-LoRA on eight commonsense QA datasets further verify its general input-adaptive capacity, scalability across LLM backbones, and interpretability.

Details

Motivation: 通过 API 使用模型时，服务端实际运行的模型身份不一定容易核实。研究检验日常问题的一词回答是否具有足够稳定的模型特征，可用于识别具体模型及核对端点自报身份。

Result: 方法为每个模型记录其对一组问题（如“说一种声音”“说一位诗人”）各自回答的频率画像，再将新回答归给最可能的模型。在 94 个模型组成的面板中，40 个答案以 92.5% 概率识别准确模型，随机机会为 1.1%；基于 OpenRouter 画像可盲识别 DeepInfra 提供的十个模型。系统提示会使准确率下降 6–16 个百分点。对 OpenRouter 上 13 个开放模型的 96 个端点测试，92 个与自报模型一致；其余四个中，一个在关闭推理后匹配自报模型，一个以 Llama 4 Maverick 名义提供 Gemma 4，两个仍未确定。账单记录还显示首方模型存在额外上下文计费；例如 Grok 4.7 对一行问题计 1,247 输入 token，而 GPT-4o 计 12 个，另有五个模型能回答当前日期却未对此收费。每个端点检测成本仅为几美分。

Insight: 模型回答习惯可构成低成本的行为指纹，用于审计 API 端点声称的身份；但系统提示会降低识别率，部分端点仍无法定论。计费 token 与回答行为的对照还揭示服务端可能加入上下文或存在计量差异。

Abstract: A model behind an API can be identified from its one-word answers to everyday questions such as “Name a sound” or “Name a poet”. A model’s profile records how often it gives each answer to each question. We attribute new answers to the model most likely to have given them. On a panel of 94 models, 40 answers identify the exact model 92.5% of the time, against 1.1% by chance. Profiles built through OpenRouter identified all ten models served by DeepInfra under blinded labels. A system prompt lowers accuracy by 6 to 16 points. We applied the method to 96 endpoints of 13 open models on OpenRouter, and 92 identify as the model they claim. Of the other four, one matches the claimed model with reasoning turned off. One serves Gemma 4 under the name Llama 4 Maverick: it names itself Gemma 4, and it is billed Gemma 4’s token counts on every message. Two remain unresolved. Billing records also show context added to first-party models. Grok 4.7 is billed 1,247 input tokens for a one-line question that costs GPT-4o only 12 tokens, and five models state the current date without being billed for it. The questions come from a public census of model defaults, and the method costs cents per endpoint.

[36] Adaptive Replay Refinement: Allocating Test-Time Compute Between Revision and Resampling cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Ahsan Bilal, Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin, Muhammad Umer, Ayesha Mohsin, Sarmad Bilal

TL;DR: ARR 检测反复修订达到固定点后，将剩余推理预算转给新的独立样本；七个模型、七个基准的评估中，MATH500 达 84.6% 准确率且仅用多数投票 41% 的串行轮数。

Details

Motivation: 测试时扩展可把额外计算用于修订已有答案或采样全新解答。何时继续改进、何时放弃并重试是关键决策；现有方法依赖固定修订日程、自我验证判断或需训练的控制器，可能浪费预算或依赖错误信号。

Result: Adaptive Replay Refinement（ARR）无需训练，通过检测固定修订过程何时到达不再变化的固定点，把释放的计算预算分配给独立新样本。重放解码把当前答案视作草稿，并并行验证未变化 token，从而高效检测固定点。在五个模型家族的七个模型、七个基准上，等计算预算下，ARR 在五个数学推理基准中的三个与多数投票表现相当，同时关键路径串行轮数最多减少 2.4 倍。MATH500 上准确率为 84.6%，只需多数投票 41% 的串行轮数。

Insight: 自我修订并非总能继续提高答案质量；当连续修订达到固定点，计算更适合转向新的独立采样。固定点检测提供了不需额外训练的预算分配信号，也揭示了自我修订在边际收益耗尽时的局限。

Abstract: Test-time scaling allocates additional inference compute to refining existing solutions or generating new ones. This raises a fundamental question: Should I keep improving this solution, or stop working on it and try a completely new solution? Existing methods make this decision using fixed refinement schedules, self-verification judgments, or learned controllers, which can waste compute or rely on signals that may be incorrect or require additional training. We introduce Adaptive Replay Refinement (ARR), a training-free method that detects when a stationary revision reaches a fixed point and reallocates the released compute to a new independent sample; replay decoding treats the current solution as a draft and verifies unchanged tokens in parallel, enabling efficient fixed-point detection. Across seven models from five families and seven benchmarks, ARR matches majority voting on three of five mathematical reasoning benchmarks at equal compute, while reducing critical-path serial passes by up to 2.4 times. On MATH500, ARR achieves 84.6% accuracy with only 41% of the serial passes required by majority voting. These results demonstrate that detecting refinement exhaustion enables adaptive reallocation from revision to independent sampling, improving inference efficiency while highlighting the limits of self-revision. Code is available at https://github.com/AhsanBilal7/adaptive_refine.

[37] Reward Misspecification Degrades Chain-of-Thought Monitorability cs.CLPDF

Tianyi Lorena Yan, John Hewitt

TL;DR: 奖励错配本身会削弱思维链可监控性：模型可能口头仍推理任务真实目标，却因错配奖励而输出奖励黑客行为；显式描述奖励可提升监控性。

Details

Motivation: 监控语言模型思维链是发现和预防不当行为的重要安全策略。随着强化学习环境复杂化，完美验证难以实现，奖励错配难以完全避免；模型因此可能因偏离真实任务意图的行为而获奖，令推理过程难以监控。

Result: 研究构造正确奖励与偏好其他行为的错配奖励配对测试环境，使奖励黑客行为可诱发且可验证。在三个模型、三项任务中，随着奖励黑客率上升，思维链监控性仍较低：模型的思维链继续围绕任务意图推理，但输出违反意图并获奖励。为在不知道真实奖励或黑客行为的前提下缓解问题，作者跨训练阶段收集使用多种奖励的轨迹，用语言模型推断奖励描述，再将描述加入输入教模型表达实际奖励。该训练在不同监控指标上平均提升 24% 或 42%（绝对值），并使思维链显式讨论错配奖励。

Insight: 即便模型的思维链表面上围绕正确任务意图，也不保证它解释了实际驱动行为的奖励信号。记录并输入从多样奖励轨迹归纳的奖励描述，可使模型对实际优化目标进行推理，改善可监控性。

Abstract: Monitoring the chain-of-thought (CoT) of language models (LMs) is a key strategy for detecting and preventing misbehaviors for AI safety. As such, it is critical to identify reinforcement learning (RL) practices that lead to unmonitorable chain-of-thought. One such practice may be unavoidable: reward misspecification. As RL environments become more complex, it is infeasible to have perfect verification, and models are rewarded for behaviors that are different from the true task intent. We show that reward misspecification alone already degrades monitorability. We create a testbed with pairs of true reward aligned with task intent and misspecified rewards that favor a different behavior, so that reward hacking can be induced by training and labeled verifiably. Across three models and three tasks, CoT monitorability remains low as models’ hack rates increase: the CoTs keep reasoning about the task intent while the outputs violate the intent and get rewarded. To mitigate unmonitorability without prior knowledge of the true reward or the hack, we collect rollouts with diverse rewards across training steps and use a LM to infer a reward description. We then inject the description into the input, which teaches the model to verbalize the reward. This reward description training improves monitorability by an average of 24% or 42% absolute, depending on the metric, across all models and tasks, and yields CoTs that reason about the misspecified reward explicitly. Code is available at https://github.com/Lorenayannnnn/reward-misspecification-degrades-cot-monitorability.

TL;DR: 基于 16,575 条英文推文的分析发现，emoji 使用语境越具主题聚集性，其众包情感评分反而越极化；结果经多种统计检验验证，说明主题一致性与情感离散是不同语义维度。

Details

Motivation: emoji 常被视为可用单一情感分数概括的简洁情绪符号，但其含义还受主题语境影响。研究希望检验 emoji 在主题上使用越集中时，情感是否也越集中，并区分主题一致性与情感极性。

Result: 作者分析 16,575 条英文推文，以 stability delta 衡量每个 emoji 的句向量类内相似度减去类间相似度。与 Kralj Novak 等（2015）情感词典对照，主题聚集性与众包情感极化呈正相关，Spearman ρ=0.361，Holm 校正 p=0.015，95% bootstrap 区间为 [0.10, 0.59]；与 Ferré 等（2025）独立西班牙语 13 情绪评分的相关为 ρ=0.509，p=0.0003。结果通过置换检验、BCa bootstrap、留一 jackknife、控制 emoji 频率的偏相关和多重检验校正。下游讽刺分类五折交叉验证中三个基线宏 F1 均超过 0.97，作者认为这是数据集 emoji 标签线索已趋饱和，分类仅作为辅助证据。

Insight: emoji 主题语境聚集并不意味着情感更单一；反而更集中于特定主题的 emoji 可能对应更极化的群体情感。主题连贯度和情感离散度应作为不同维度分析，讽刺分类的高分也受数据集标签线索饱和限制。

Abstract: Emoji are widely treated as compact affective tokens whose meaning is summarised by a single sentiment score. We argue that this picture is incomplete. From a corpus of 16{,}575 English tweets and a per-emoji metric we call \emph{stability delta} (within-class minus between-class cosine similarity of sentence embeddings), we show that emojis used in tightly clustered topical contexts also exhibit \emph{more} polarised crowd-sourced sentiment, not less. Validated against the Kralj Novak et al.\ (2015) sentiment lexicon, the central correlation is Spearman $ρ= +0.361$ (Holm $p = 0.015$, 95% bootstrap CI $[+0.10, +0.59]$); against the independent Ferré et al.\ (2025) Spanish 13-emotion ratings, $ρ= +0.509$ ($p = 0.0003$). The result survives permutation testing, BCa bootstrap, leave-one-out jackknife, partial correlation controlling for emoji frequency, and Holm correction across a four-test family. Topical coherence and affective dispersion are distinct dimensions of emoji meaning. As a downstream check we report leakage-free five-fold cross-validation results for sarcasm classification on the Emoji Sarcasm Dataset; all three baselines (a gated multimodal architecture, fine-tuned BERTweet features, and fine-tuned RoBERTa features) score above 0.97 macro F1, indicating the dataset has saturating emoji-label cues. We frame the classifier as supporting evidence rather than the contribution.

[39] Asymmetric Looped Transformers: Fixed Global Representations for an Iterative Decoder cs.CLPDF

Cheng Liang, Tengfei Zhang, Wenjie DU, Jianyu Wu, Changlian Ma

TL;DR: AsymLoop 将单次因果上下文编码与共享权重循环状态细化分开：固定全局 KV 缓存提供持续上下文，循环解码器生成局部 KV；在 60M–1B 四种规模上困惑度和下游准确率均优于同深度 Full Loop。

Details

Motivation: 循环 Transformer 通过重复使用共享层增加有效深度，可在不增加参数量的情况下扩展计算。但全栈循环把上下文构建和迭代状态细化放入同一递归过程，没有显式区分二者职责。

Result: 非对称循环 Transformer（AsymLoop）将单次上下文编码与循环状态细化分离：单次因果编码器生成跨迭代固定的逐层全局 KV 缓存；权重共享循环解码器则从不断变化的状态产生局部 KV。两类 KV 在单个 softmax 下联合注意，使更新同时利用持久上下文和当前中间状态。训练时编码器和解码器采用重尾引导的逐层学习率方法。在 FineWeb-Edu 的 60M 至 1B 四种规模上，与有效深度和训练 token 预算匹配的 Full Loop 相比，AsymLoop 验证困惑度更低、平均下游准确率更高。1B 模型在七个常识推理基准的平均零样本准确率由 49.58% 升至 50.85%；更长训练预算、OLMo 式主干和稀疏 MoE 实验也报告收益。

Insight: 将稳定上下文只编码一次、把计算迭代集中于状态细化，可以减少循环过程中上下文构造与状态更新互相干扰。全局与局部 KV 联合注意使每步既保留原始上下文，也能利用当前循环状态；结果支持这种非对称分工用于循环语言模型。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying shared layers, offering a way to scale computation without increasing parameter count. However, full-stack looping places context construction and iterative state refinement within the same recurrent computation, without explicitly distinguishing their roles. We introduce the Asymmetric Looped Transformer (AsymLoop), which separates single-pass context encoding from recurrent state refinement. A single-pass causal encoder produces layer-specific Global key-value (KV) caches that remain fixed across iterations, while a weight-tied recurrent decoder generates Local KV from its evolving state. Joint attention over both sources under a single softmax allows each update to integrate persistent contextual information with the current intermediate state. For training, we apply a heavy-tail-guided layerwise learning-rate method to the encoder and decoder. Experiments on FineWeb-Edu across four model scales, from 60M to 1B, show consistently lower validation perplexity and higher average downstream accuracy than Full Loop under matched effective depth and training token budgets. At 1B, AsymLoop improves average zero-shot accuracy across seven commonsense reasoning benchmarks from 49.58% to 50.85%. Further experiments extend these benefits to longer training budgets, an OLMo-style backbone, and sparse mixture-of-experts models. These results support asymmetric context encoding and state refinement as an effective design for recurrent language models.

Abstract: Continuous flow language models offer a path to parallel generation with tools for control and acceleration unavailable to discrete diffusion, yet effective techniques for building them at the billion-parameter scale remain less established. We present Lafa (Large flow language model), a fast, high-performing flow language model scaled to 8B parameters in a data-efficient manner. Lafa is a blockwise flow model with causal attention on the prefix and bidirectional, asynchronous flow within each block. This design enables efficient training by adapting pretrained autoregressive (AR) models and fast inference via prefix caching and blockwise parallel decoding. We train Lafa at 0.5B, 7B, and 8B scales with fewer than 30B tokens, more efficient than previous diffusion models, and evaluate it on instruction following, reasoning, and coding, where the learned flow matches or surpasses the quality of AR and can be distilled into flow maps for one- and few-step generation. Notably, flow training preserves or even improves pretrained AR capabilities via a multi-token prediction effect, which we leverage in a self-speculative decoding scheme that verifies flow-based drafts autoregressively within a single model. This yields 2.54x the throughput of an AR model at comparable quality, outperforming multi-token prediction and discrete diffusion methods. Our findings show that continuous flows can serve as a scalable foundation for language modeling.

TL;DR: Beckmann World Models 从噪声、视觉历史和动作直接学习未来帧终端映射，无需预训练生成教师；PushT 和 RoboMimic Can 上 64 帧预测 MSE 分别比同骨干回归基线降低 68% 和 57%，PushT 四帧预测耗时 24.8 ms。

Motivation: 动作条件世界模型可通过预测未来状态支持规划和策略评估，但训练准确的一步预测器可能计算成本高。使用预训练生成教师会增加依赖，且噪声输入下的插值传输一致性未必保证推理时纯噪声起点的预测准确。

Result: Beckmann World Models（BWM）学习从噪声、视觉历史和动作直接映射到未来帧，不使用预训练生成教师。方法将 Beckmann Transport Models 的终端映射守恒律用于动作条件视频预测，可直接以噪声—数据对训练，无需 DriftWorld 所需的条件生成样本群。为解决插值输入传输一致性不能保证纯噪声推理准确的问题，端点对齐训练直接监督源端预测，强化运动与空间结构，并训练模型从自生成历史中恢复。各组件共用同一映射，每个未来块仍只需一次网络调用。PushT 与 RoboMimic Can 的 64 帧预测 MSE 分别比同骨干回归基线降低 68% 和 57%；策略评估中 PushT 平均 IoU 误差最低为 0.0100，四帧预测耗时 24.8 ms。RoboMimic Can 上含运动监督和生成历史的 BWM 阶段较局部一步 DriftWorld 吞吐高 3.3 倍、峰值内存低 78%。

Insight: 终端映射训练可避免生成教师依赖，同时端点监督和自生成历史让训练目标贴近纯噪声起点及长程使用情境。摘要未给出所有设置的准确率—成本权衡或不同任务成功率。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models support planning and policy evaluation by predicting future states. However, learning an accurate one-step predictor can require substantial training computation. We introduce Beckmann World Models (BWM), which learn a direct map from noise, visual history, and actions to future frames without a pretrained generative teacher. We adapt the terminal-map conservation law of Beckmann Transport Models to action-conditioned video prediction, training directly from noise-data pairs without conditional populations of generated samples required by DriftWorld. However, transport consistency on interpolated inputs does not ensure accurate prediction from the pure noise used at inference. We address this gap with endpoint-aligned training that directly supervises the source prediction, emphasizes motion and spatial structure, and teaches recovery from generated histories. All components train the same map and retain one network call per future chunk. On PushT and Robomimic Can, the final model reduces 64-frame prediction MSE by 68% and 57%, respectively, relative to same-backbone regression baseline. For policy evaluation, BWM achieves the lowest mean IoU error among the evaluated models, 0.0100, at 24.8 ms per four-frame PushT prediction. On Robomimic Can, the BWM stage trained with motion supervision and generated histories achieves 3.3 times higher throughput and 78% lower peak memory than local one-step DriftWorld. In a latent-space extension to RT-1 and Bridge-V2, equal-update controls show that feature supervision under generated histories improves perceptual prediction. These findings show how terminal-map learning can combine efficient training with accurate one-step visual prediction.

Abstract: In generalized few-shot 3D point cloud segmentation, support annotations are often compromised by sparsity and occlusion. Blindly adapting to such low quality samples risks learning spurious geometric patterns rather than robust novel concepts. Although 3D Vision-Language Models have been introduced to sup plement supervision, the potential of uncertainty to actively govern adaptation dynamics remains underexplored. Existing signal-level methods like loss reweight ing fail to prevent unreliable episodes from steering the optimization trajectory. To address this, we propose Uncertainty-Gated Adaptive (UGA), to the best of our knowledge the first framework that elevates uncertainty to update-level gating for generalized few-shot 3D point cloud segmentation. The core mod ule, Uncertainty-Gated Update (UG-Update), estimates episodic reliability via intra-class dispersion over merged support and pseudo-labeled novel supervision, and selectively gates residual gradient updates. This encourages the model to learn novel semantics from reliable episodes while attenuating updates during unreliable ones. Complementing this, we introduce an Uncertainty-Based Pseudo Label Filter (UB-Filter) to enforce learning from high-confidence dense signals. Abstract: Long-video understanding often requires answering many questions about a single video whose evidence is distributed across extended temporal contexts. Conventional video-language models repeatedly encode frames or retrieve visual evidence for every question. Parameterized video memory offers a write-once alternative, but existing methods rely primarily on synthesized text and fixed adaptation configurations, leaving open where video knowledge should be written and how visual evidence should shape the memory. Abstract: Online action detection (OAD) identifies ongoing actions in streaming videos. Codec motion vectors (MVs) are available during decoding, avoiding a separate optical-flow computation. On THUMOS’14, optical flow exceeds MVs by 11.2 mAP points with motion-only inputs, whereas the gap narrows to 3.7 points after RGB fusion, with the largest reduction in action-category discrimination. Abstract: With the rapid expansion of online video content, video temporal grounding has become essential for localizing moments based on user-specific queries, comprising two subtasks: moment retrieval (MR) and highlight detection (HD). While recent approaches adopt DETR-style decoders or temporal pyramid architectures, they rely on single-scale feature maps or uniformly downsampled representations, limiting their ability to capture semantic boundaries and variable-length events. In this paper, we present SuperClip Video Temporal Grounding (SCVTG) built on a multi-scale temporal hierarchy that better captures distinctive events at various granularities. SCVTG constructs a SuperClip pyramid by grouping adjacent clips into semantically coherent segments, termed SuperClips. Extensive experiments show that SCVTG achieves the best performance across five video temporal grounding benchmarks with promising gains.

[50] Robustness of Internal Signals for Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Allison Chen, William Yang, Salma Abdel Magid, Jonathan Williams, Olga Russakovsky

TL;DR: 对 VLM 幻觉检测内部信号的审计发现，生成 token 的文本位置本身即可达到有竞争力的性能，并可能成为已有方法的捷径；高检测分数常由语言幻觉驱动，较少依赖视觉 grounding，且对更早出现的幻觉鲁棒性更差。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在图像问题回答中可能编造物体，安全关键应用需要及时发现幻觉。部分方法利用模型生成文本时的内部信号，但这些信号可能与产生幻觉的原因相同，未必能真实反映视觉依据。

Result: 作者系统分析内部信号幻觉检测的鲁棒性。首先发现，简单使用生成词在回答中的 token 位置即可取得有竞争力结果；进一步显示部分检测方法依赖 token 位置这一捷径。其次，研究检测器对语言和视觉模态的敏感性，实验提示较强检测表现常来自发现语言层面幻觉；依赖 token 位置的方法视觉 grounding 较弱，且当幻觉更早出现在回答中时鲁棒性降低。最后，作者观察到不同检测方法信号互补，将较弱方法加入较强方法集成仍可改善性能。

Insight: 内部激活或注意等信号表现好，不一定说明检测器识别了视觉证据失配，可能只利用回答位置或语言模式捷径；需通过模态和位置干预验证鲁棒性。摘要未给出各方法的具体检测指标或数据集。

Abstract: When given an image and a text prompt, vision-language models (VLMs) can produce seemingly sophisticated text responses. However, they are also prone to hallucinate objects in their output, which can have detrimental consequences in safety-critical applications. To detect these hallucinations, certain methods interestingly leverage internal signals from the VLM as it generates the text response; potentially, these methods use the same signals that led to a hallucination to detect it. We conduct an in-depth analysis to understand the robustness of these detection methods. First, we show that a naive baseline of a generated word’s token position in the VLM text response achieves competitive performance. Further, we reveal how some detection methods rely on token position and use this heuristic as a shortcut. Second, we explore detection methods’ sensitivity to language and vision modalities. Our experiments suggest that strong detection performance is often driven by detecting language-based hallucinations, and when methods rely on token position, they tend to be less visually grounded and exhibit reduced robustness when hallucinations occur earlier in a text response. Lastly, we demonstrate that different detection methods can carry complementary signals; when ensembling methods together, adding weaker methods to an ensemble of higher performing ones still offers performance improvements. Altogether, while there may be useful internal VLM signals to detect hallucinations, our investigation reveals important limitations of the current methods that try to leverage them.

VitronEdit adopts a shared sequence representation and progressive multimodal training to transfer editing knowledge across modalities. We further introduce geometry-aware post-training, which leverages a pretrained geometry reconstruction model to provide depth and camera-pose supervision. Surprisingly, applying geometric supervision only to 3D samples yields the best overall performance, while image and video editing also improve despite receiving no direct geometric supervision, suggesting that geometric regularization transfers through the shared backbone. Abstract: Generating human-object interactions (HOIs) with multiple objects is challenging because the resulting motions arise from different underlying causes. Human motion reflects semantic intent, intentionally manipulated objects must remain coordinated with the human, while surrounding objects move primarily as physical consequences of contact and collision. We present BUMP, a text-driven framework for multi-object HOI generation based on a simple principle: learn intentional motion and simulate physical consequences. Our method first generates full-body human motion from text and scene context, then predicts trajectories for a small number of intentionally manipulated driver objects conditioned on the human motion. We then obtain responder object motions through physics simulation, allowing the framework to handle varying numbers and configurations of surrounding objects without expanding the learned trajectory output space. To address the scarcity of paired full-body multi-object HOI data, we combine heterogeneous supervision from human-only motion, full-body HOI, and hand-centric manipulation data augmented with plausible body motion. We further introduce outcome-conditioned interaction editing, which infers a local modification of the causal driver object trajectory from a desired responder object behavior without requiring an explicit target driver object trajectory. Experiments demonstrate strong performance on both single-object and multi-object HOI benchmarks. Abstract: Reliable language-guided perception is essential for embodied agents, where questions about objects, events, and their locations must be answered from long observation histories. While existing view-based systems typically store either text captions or visual embeddings, we extend the ReMEmbR-style memory with image paths that link each stored description to its original visual content. Details

Motivation: 少步轨迹蒸馏需要从教师轨迹上的密集局部监督学习准确的全局更新。已有方案依赖特定轨迹参数化或离散时间区间预测，因而受预设时间结构或查询网格约束。

Abstract: Few-step trajectory distillation aims to learn accurate global updates from dense local supervision along a teacher’s sampling trajectory. Existing approaches construct within-step dynamics using specific trajectory parameterizations or discrete interval predictions, tying the learned update to a prescribed temporal structure. We propose \method, which directly learns a continuous trajectory primitive representing cumulative displacement along the teacher trajectory. By supervising finite differences of the primitive with local teacher velocities, \method learns the accumulated motion directly, while obtaining global updates through endpoint differences. This formulation avoids prescribing a temporal basis or fixed query grid and instead learns the temporal evolution of cumulative displacement from local supervision. We further establish a local-to-global error bound to characterize how local regression errors affect full-interval displacement accuracy, and introduce endpoint absorption to eliminate the auxiliary primitive branch while preserving one-network-evaluation inference. Experiments on large-scale text-to-image generation show that \method achieves a T-FID of 20.92 and an HPSv2 score of 28.35 at one NFE, improving both metrics over a trajectory-distillation baseline. After endpoint absorption, it retains comparable performance, achieving a T-FID of 20.86 and an HPSv2 score of 28.33.

Details

Result: 研究在十种视觉提示条件下测试 GPT-4.1 和 Claude 4.5 Sonnet，覆盖 243 张主要支票（其中 124 张具有两个可观测且不相等的金额）及另外两个数据集的 598 个严格不匹配样本。遮挡礼貌金额时，GPT-4.1 的 CARLAR 比例为 73.7%（95% bootstrap 区间 68.3–79.0，较基线 p<0.001）；CARLAR 样本中的期望校准误差最高达 0.93。压缩图像少样本干预使 Claude 在 243 张支票上的成对联合抽取准确率提高 11.5 个百分点（95% 区间 6.2–16.9，校正后 p=0.00054），但在严格 124 例上的增益较小且校正后不显著；字段预测一致率对 Claude 下降、对 GPT-4.1 上升。

Insight: 模型对两个字段给出相同答案并不代表可靠，置信度也可能在字段受相互影响时仍然很高。抽取评估应考虑字段依赖、图像上下文和样本定义；少样本示例的作用依模型而异，不能据某一总体指标推断所有子集都受益。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for structured extraction from financial documents, but their behavior under visual ambiguity is poorly understood. We study CARLAR (Courtesy Amount Recognition and Legal Amount Recognition) influence: a model returns the same amount for both cheque fields even though their annotations differ, including cases with a blank annotation encoded as zero. Across ten visual prompting conditions, we evaluate GPT-4.1 and Claude 4.5 Sonnet on 243 primary cheques (124 with two observed unequal amounts) and 598 strict mismatches from two additional datasets. Under courtesy redaction, GPT-4.1 CARLAR reaches 73.7 percent (95 percent bootstrap interval: 68.3 to 79.0; p below 0.001 versus baseline). Model-reported confidence can remain high when predictions agree; expected calibration error reaches 0.93 among CARLAR cases. We also test a compressed-image few-shot intervention that presents examples as one visual table without model fine-tuning. On the 243-case cohort, Claude’s Control condition increases paired joint extraction accuracy by 11.5 percentage points (95 percent interval: 6.2 to 16.9; adjusted p = 0.00054), but its strict-124 gain is smaller and not significant after correction. The intervention’s effect on predicted-field agreement is model-dependent: it decreases for Claude and increases for GPT-4.1. These results motivate validation methods that account for visual context and dependencies between extracted fields.

Abstract: Deep neural networks have achieved remarkable success on vision benchmarks, yet they continue to struggle with many generalization challenges. Supervised vision training relies on one-hot labels, which provide limited information about semantic structure and shared attributes between classes. This limited supervision can leave visual representations vulnerable to distribution shifts, spurious correlations, texture bias, adversarial perturbations, and forgetting in sequential learning settings. We study whether pretrained language models can serve as lightweight auxiliary supervision for vision training without requiring paired image-text data, prompt engineering, or contrastive objectives. Specifically, we evaluate two forms of language guidance, Explicit Language Guidance (ExLG) and Implicit Language Guidance (ImLG). We conduct a comprehensive evaluation across six generalization regimes, including in-distribution, out-of-distribution generalization, shortcut and spurious correlation resistance, texture and shape bias, adversarial robustness, and continual learning. Our analyses show that the two mechanisms have complementary strengths, with explicit guidance consistently benefiting in-distribution, low-data performance, and continual learning retention, while implicit guidance is often more useful in shortcut-sensitive settings and under stronger adversarial perturbations. Importantly, both are lightweight and add minimal parameters and training overhead. The analyses characterize when language-derived structure helps supervised vision training and provides a practical roadmap for using off-the-shelf pretrained models from another modality as auxiliary supervision.

Details
