Result: 研究发现，生成前隐藏状态包含可恢复的视觉上下文适当披露粒度，即使最终回答与之不一致，不同披露级别在共同隐藏状态方向上呈有序结构。基于此，作者设计推理时“路由并引导”框架：序数粒度路由器选择披露级别，条件化引导模块在解码过程中施加对应隐藏状态干预。四种 VLM 骨干上的实验改善了披露与视觉上下文的对齐，并平衡过度和不足披露；通用能力基准仅有适度变化，且方法可迁移到分布和隐私风险分类不同的数据集。

Insight: 隐私控制不必只依赖生成后的文本过滤；模型内部已编码图像证据支持的披露粒度，可在解码时据此调节输出。按序数级别显式路由有助于同时避免泄露过多位置细节和无端压低精度，但仍需关注跨数据集和通用能力上的迁移表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer sensitive information such as geolocation from subtle visual cues, creating privacy risks when responses disclose more detail than the visual context warrants. We study visual-contextual privacy in geolocation disclosure, where a VLM should reveal location information only at the granularity appropriate to the visual context depicted in the image. We first show that this contextually appropriate granularity is recoverable from pre-generation hidden states, even when generated responses are misaligned, with disclosure levels exhibiting an ordered structure along a common hidden-state direction. Motivated by this, we propose an inference-time route-and-steer framework: an ordinal granularity router maps visual-contextual privacy representations to a disclosure route, and a disclosure-level-conditioned steering module applies route-specific hidden-state interventions during decoding. Across four VLM backbones, our method improves visual-context-aware disclosure alignment, better balances over- and under-disclosure, and incurs only modest changes on general capability benchmarks. It further transfers to another dataset with a distinct data distribution and privacy-risk taxonomy.

[60] Look Before You Reason: Implicit Visual Thinking for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning cs.CVPDF

Zerui Chen, Changrui Chen, Fei Ni, Jiankang Deng

TL;DR: LoRe 在单轮 VLM 推理前隐式定位关键图像区域、强化对应 patch 的 KV cache，再结合强化学习教模型利用视觉线索；多个多模态基准上提升推理且额外开销较低。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在需要细看小区域或容易忽略区域的任务上仍受限。缩放、裁剪或代码调用等显式图像思考虽有效，但多轮交互造成重复 token 计算、推理变慢，也把区域检查与依赖该区域的推理拆开。

Result: Look Before You Reason（LoRe）在单轮交互中预测与问题相关的图像区域，将区域与相应 patch 特征对齐，并增强相关键值缓存条目，使后续解码直接使用这些线索，无需额外图像输入或显式工具调用。强化学习进一步引导模型寻找有信息量的区域并在推理中利用它们。作者还提出物体中心多模态推理基准 COCO-ObjQA；在三个具挑战性的多模态基准上，LoRe 持续提升 VLM 推理表现，额外推理开销较低。

Insight: 细粒度视觉推理可以通过选择性强化模型内部已有的图像 patch 表征来完成，不一定需要反复裁剪并重新发起多轮调用。关键是让模型选择相关区域并在后续推理中实际使用，而不仅仅是生成一个区域定位结果。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal reasoning, yet they remain limited on tasks requiring fine-grained understanding of small or easily overlooked image regions. Recent thinking with images approaches mitigate this limitation by allowing models to inspect images through operations such as zooming, cropping, or code execution. While effective, their multi-turn interactions with VLMs introduce redundant token computation and slow inference, and separate region inspection from the reasoning step that depends on it. To address these issues, we propose Look Before You Reason (LoRe), an implicit visual thinking framework for efficient visual reasoning within a single-turn VLM interaction. Given an image and a question, LoRe predicts the most relevant image region, aligns it with the corresponding image patch features, and strengthens the associated key-value (KV) cache entries, enabling subsequent decoding to directly exploit the selected visual cues without additional visual inputs or explicit tool calls. We further employ reinforcement learning to guide the VLM toward identifying informative image regions and leveraging them during reasoning. To support fine-grained evaluation of multimodal reasoning, we introduce COCO-ObjQA, a large-scale benchmark for object-centric multimodal reasoning. Experiments on three challenging multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that LoRe consistently improves VLM reasoning performance with low additional inference overhead.

[61] On the Limits of Scale: Inverse Scaling in Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

TL;DR: 对 19 个 VLM、8 个基准的系统分析发现，模型变大并不保证所有子任务变好：375 组子任务—模型对呈逆向缩放，影响多图空间推理、视觉感知和 STEM 等领域。

Details

Motivation: 总体尺度规律通常认为增加模型规模、数据和算力会带来性能提升，但总平均可能掩盖特定问题类别上大模型反而不如同系列小模型的现象。研究系统检验视觉语言模型是否存在这种子任务级逆向缩放。

Result: 研究评估四个家族的 19 个 VLM，在八个多模态基准（九个评测拆分）上每模型回答 29,386 道问题，共 558,334 次模型—问题评估。跨家族模型两两逐题比较后，发现 375 组子任务—模型对存在逆向缩放，涉及 4,342 个小模型答对而大模型答错的独特查询；该模式也在独立开发的架构间复现。13 组子任务—数据集在多个家族中持续出现，较符合任务驱动而非单一模型特性。多图空间推理、视觉感知和大学 STEM 受影响突出，部分准确率差距超过 20 个百分点；图表理解相对不易出现该现象。

Insight: 按整体平均判断模型规模收益会掩盖能力局部退化；模型选择应根据具体任务类别，而不能默认更大的 VLM 对每项能力都更强。多家族复现及跨家族持续的子任务现象，支持逆向缩放与任务特性有关。

Abstract: Scaling laws suggest that increasing model size, training data, and compute generally yields monotonic performance improvements. While this often holds at the aggregate level, we present systematic evidence that vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit inverse scaling at the subtask level: specific question categories on which larger models perform worse than smaller counterparts within the same model family. We evaluate 19 models from four distinct model families across eight multimodal benchmarks (nine evaluation splits), answering 29,386 questions per model, 558,334 model-question evaluations in total. Using per-question pairwise analysis across all model pairs in each family, we identify 375 subtask-model-pair combinations exhibiting inverse scaling, spanning 4,342 unique queries where a smaller model answers correctly but a larger model fails, and we find that these patterns replicate across independently developed architectures. We identify 13 subtask-dataset pairs that show inverse scaling across multiple families, consistent with the phenomenon being task-driven rather than model-specific. The most affected domains in our study include multi-image spatial reasoning, visual perception, and university-level STEM knowledge, with accuracy gaps exceeding 20 percentage points in several cases, while diagram understanding appears comparatively resistant. These findings challenge the assumption that increasing model size uniformly improves multimodal capabilities and suggest that model selection should be task-aware.

Insight: 适配信号可从当前样本的去噪残差中估计，而不必回到源数据或获取额外标注。先估计样本级噪声，再构造对比训练对，有助于针对分布偏移更新模型；连续样本共享噪声特性时还可逐步复用适配。

Abstract: Although deep learning-based point cloud denoising methods have demonstrated impressive results in recent years, their effectiveness usually degrades substantially in real-world scenarios where the noise distribution of test point clouds deviates from that of the training set. To address this challenge, we propose a test-time adaptive denoising (TTAD) framework that enables existing denoising approaches to adapt to unknown noise patterns at test time without relying on additional supervision or retraining from source data. Taking a pre-trained denoising model and a test noisy point cloud as input, TTAD first employs a residual-driven noise modeling (RDNM) module to estimate the sample-specific noise distribution through analyzing the deviation between the noisy input and its pseudo-clean version denoised by the initial model. Then, a noise-aware contrastive adaptation (NACA) module is designed to build sample-specific noisy-noisier test-time training pair from the denoised point cloud, and update the model parameters over a few adaptation steps per sample at test time through contrastive learning. Furthermore, TTAD naturally extends to a continual TTAD (CTTAD) setting, facilitating efficient progressive refinement across sequential test samples with consistent noise distributions, while reducing the computational cost per sample. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly improves the generalization ability of existing denoising models under unseen noise, achieving superior performance across multiple evaluation metrics.

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection aims to detect both seen and unseen categories by leveraging the semantic knowledge of vision-language models. However, existing methods often struggle with small objects due to insufficient preservation of high-resolution details, ineffective information aggregation across feature levels, and limited region-level discriminability. To address these challenges, we propose FocusOVD, an open-vocabulary object detector that enhances small-object representations without modifying the frozen VLM backbone. Abstract: 4D scene graphs require persistent object identity when an object disappears inside an opaque carrier and later reappears as a fresh detection. When appearance and target-centric motion are unavailable, identity recovery must rely on relational history. We introduce CVG-SGM, a perception-frontend-compatible transfer–maintain–read memory. Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language Models (PVLMs) achieve strong zero-shot recognition but often suffer performance degradation under target-domain shift. Unsupervised adaptation provides a practical way to improve target-domain performance without labeled data, but it remains challenging because pseudo-labels generated by the model can be unreliable and no labeled validation set is available for model selection or early stopping. We propose a simple framework for unsupervised PVLM adaptation that combines progressive margin-based subset selection with cascaded consistency learning. The subset selection strategy constructs a class-balanced pseudo-labeled set using prediction margins and gradually expands it during training, enabling adaptation to start from reliable supervision anchors before incorporating broader target-domain data. Cascaded consistency learning further exploits unlabeled samples by using temporally smoothed teacher predictions on weakly augmented views to supervise student predictions on strongly augmented views, with confidence filtering to reduce the influence of uncertain pseudo-labels. To preserve the pretrained embedding structure, the framework updates only normalization parameters in the visual encoder and class prototypes initialized from text prompts. Experiments across multiple recognition benchmarks with both CLIP and SigLIP backbones show consistent improvements over zero-shot baselines and competitive performance against recent unsupervised PVLM adaptation methods. These results suggest that progressive pseudo-label selection and teacher-guided consistency provide an effective and lightweight strategy for adapting PVLM classifiers without labeled target data.

Motivation: 情绪相关 EEG 模式会随时间、频段、电极和脑区变化；多种生理信号又同时包含各自特有和跨模态共享的情感信息。如何保留各模态线索并利用互补信息，是多模态情绪识别的两个相关挑战。

Result: GMSI-Net 是以 EEG 为中心的多模态框架。TSSR 编码器建模多尺度时空通道模式、微分熵特征及脑区局部到全局关系；MSR 分支通过双向跨模态注意力与可学习门控控制信息交换并保留模态特征；MIR 分支以自适应加权对比损失对齐共享情感表示，外围生理信号由多尺度 Conv-Transformer 编码。在 DEAP 被试内随机十折中，效价和唤醒指标为 96.21（1.47）与 96.41（1.60）；MAHNOB-HCI 上为 94.10（2.86）与 94.48（3.77）。

Insight: 该设计把跨模态共享情绪信息与每种模态的独有线索分开处理，避免融合时一味对齐而抹去互补信号。摘要报告的结果来自被试内随机十折，不能据此直接推断跨被试泛化表现。

Abstract: Multimodal physiological signals provide complementary cues for emotion recognition. They also raise two related challenges. Emotion-related EEG patterns vary across time, frequency bands, electrodes, and brain regions. Different physiological modalities also contain both modality-specific and shared affective information. This paper presents GMSI-Net, an EEG-centered multimodal emotion recognition framework. It has three key components. The temporal-spatial-spectral-region (TSSR) encoder models multiscale temporal-channel patterns, differential entropy features, and local-to-global brain-region relations. The modality-specific representation (MSR) branch uses bidirectional cross-modal attention and learnable gates. It controls information transfer while retaining modality-specific cues. The modality-invariant representation (MIR) branch learns a shared affective representation. An adaptively weighted contrastive loss aligns this representation across modalities. Independent multiscale Conv-Transformer encoders process the peripheral physiological signals. GMSI-Net is evaluated on DEAP and MAHNOB-HCI using within-subject random ten-fold cross-validation. The model reaches 96.21 (1.47) and 96.41 (1.60) for valence and arousal on DEAP. It reaches 94.10 (2.86) and 94.48 (3.77) on MAHNOB-HCI. These results indicate that combining EEG features with complementary physiological information can improve emotion recognition.

However, these captions may include redundant semantic details, introducing noise when retrieving the target image and leading to suboptimal retrieval results. In the second stage, a critic re-rank module further evaluates the retrieved candidates from the perspectives of modification consistency and reference preservation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework for the ZS-CIR. Our code is available at: https://github.com/dxlabskku/LM-CIR.

We instead propose to anchor evaluation in the available ground truth by extracting verifiable claims from each explanation and verifying them against the corresponding annotations. We instantiate this idea as AnomalyJev, a lightweight framework that extracts verifiable claims from contextualised representations using ground-truth-defined queries and pointer based heads, without token generation. Result: TAME 采用不对称模态专家结构：文本特征作为固定且始终启用的共享专家，音频—文本和视频—文本分支作为按需路由的辅助专家。轻量 Prophet Heads 估计每种模态在单个样本上的预测不确定性，路由器据此进行可靠性加权融合。在 CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI 和 CH-SIMS 上，多数指标达到最先进表现；相较混合专家基线，参数量最多减少 78.7%，FLOPs 最多降低 41.2%。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to understand user-generated videos by integrating multiple modalities. Existing methods often treat modalities as symmetric, overlooking the fact that language provides a dominant semantic prior while nonverbal signals are complementary and noise-prone. To address this, we propose a Text-Anchored Modality Experts with Uncertainty-Guided Routing, termed TAME, that reformulates MSA as an uncertainty-guided refinement paradigm, where the language prior is calibrated and enhanced by selectively trusted nonverbal evidence. Specifically, TAME adopts an asymmetric Mixture-of-Modality-Experts framework, in which textual features act as a fixed and always-activated shared expert, while audio-textual and video-textual cross-modal branches serve as dynamically routed auxiliary experts. Furthermore, we introduce lightweight Prophet Heads to quantify the sample-level predictive uncertainty of each individual modality, and this uncertainty metric is further incorporated into the modality router to realize reliability-aware expert aggregation and fusion. Experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS datasets demonstrate that TAME achieves state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics while reducing parameter count by up to 78.7% and FLOPs by up to 41.2% over the mixture-of-expert baseline. %These results confirm that asymmetric expert construction with uncertainty-guided routing is an effective paradigm for MSA.

Abstract: Aiming to modify textual content while preserving the original style, Scene Text Editing (STE) has significant practical value in many applications. Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D object detection is a core perception service for autonomous driving, but deployment requires robustness to point-cloud corruption while meeting latency and memory requirements. We therefore formulate LiDAR perception provisioning as constructing a detector that remains accurate under point-cloud corruptions while meeting constraints on inference latency and peak GPU memory. To achieve robustness, we first craft corrupted samples that mimic extreme weather conditions. Then we train candidate detectors offline by ordering clean and corrupted samples by perturbation sensitivity, through which detectors learn more from hard corrupted samples. We estimate perturbation sensitivity using an ensemble of seven heterogeneous 3D detectors by tracking changes in confidence, BEV overlap, and unmatched detections. To improve efficiency, we narrow the network and use offline neighborhood-density measurements to determine how many points and neighbors each sampling stage processes. We evaluate our method using AP (Average Precision) under clean and corrupted conditions, inference latency, and peak allocated GPU memory. On KITTI for Car detection with PointRCNN, our method improves Moderate AP under corruption by 3.32 points over the original detector while reducing mean latency by 19.8% and peak allocated GPU memory by 53.9%. Across two datasets, two detector architectures, and three object classes, our method improves AP under corruption by 4.03 points on average over the corresponding original detectors, while reducing mean inference latency and peak allocated GPU memory by 14.4% and 38.0%, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/drunklibai/robust-efficient-lidar-perception.

Abstract: Simulation-ready world reconstruction demands physical interactivity beyond visual realism, which existing black-box 3D generation fails to deliver. At the same time, NeoMCP exposes these capabilities as structured, tool-callable APIs for vision-language agents to visually inspect, edit code, and iteratively refine programs. Through a hierarchical coordinator-worker architecture, the framework scales seamlessly from standalone articulated objects to complex compositional scenes. Ziwei Zhao, Yuhang Liu

Result: OTFS 将帧选择表述为结构化证据分配问题，把多个证据源映射到视频帧；为体现重要证据应保留、冗余帧无需吸收质量的不对称性，使用半非平衡熵最优传输目标，并设计 OTFS-Sinkhorn 高效求解器。之后通过精确动态规划，以覆盖为目标提取帧子集，构成免训练两阶段流程。单证据源情形下，方法诱导出 Gibbs 型帧分布，使 Q-Frame 的温度缩放分布成为其特例。三个长视频理解基准实验中，OTFS 持续超过强免训练基线。

Insight: 帧选择可视为多类推理证据如何分配到有限视觉预算的优化问题，而不只是给每帧打一个相关度分数。半非平衡传输允许重要证据被保留而冗余帧不必获得同等关注，覆盖感知子集抽取再把分配转成可用帧集。

Abstract: Long-video understanding with multimodal LLMs is fundamentally constrained by the mismatch between video length and the model’s limited visual context budget, making frame selection essential for practical reasoning. Existing query-aware methods typically rely on heuristic pipelines: they assign one-dimensional query-conditioned scores to frames and then apply sampling, ranking, or extraction rules to form the final subset. While effective in practice, such methods are generally not derived from an explicit optimization objective and struggle to capture the heterogeneous evidence required for complex reasoning. In this paper, we revisit long-video frame selection from a principled optimization perspective. We formulate it as a structured evidence-allocation problem and propose OTFS, an optimal transport framework that maps multiple evidence sources onto video frames. To reflect the asymmetry of this problem—important evidence should be preserved, whereas redundant frames need not absorb mass—we instantiate this view with a semi-unbalanced entropic optimal transport objective and an efficient solver, OTFS-Sinkhorn. Combined with an exact dynamic program for coverage-aware subset extraction, OTFS yields a practical two-stage training-free pipeline. We further show that, in the single-source case, our formulation induces a Gibbs-type distribution over frames, making Q-Frame’s temperature-scaled distribution a special case of our framework while also providing a broader perspective for understanding related prior methods. Experiments on three long-video understanding benchmarks show that OTFS consistently outperforms strong training-free baselines. Codes are available at https://github.com/barry664/OTFS.

[78] VLA-Retain: Benchmarking Cross-Dimension Effects of Vision Tuning in VLAs cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Borui Xu, Han Chen, Bingsheng He, Ming Wu, Jason Zeng

TL;DR: VLA-Retain 检查五种 VLA 视觉微调在七类扰动上的跨维度保留效果，发现目标域收益常不能预测其他条件表现，10 组模型—域配置中有 8 组平均非目标收益为负。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型常通过在目标域数据上微调视觉模块改善机器人操作，但这种适配对其他观察条件的鲁棒性影响不清楚。已有基准只测未微调模型，适配研究又通常只报告目标域增益，无法分离迁移、遗忘及模型架构差异。

Result: VLA-Retain 比较五种开放 VLA 模型及其在 Camera-view 或 Sensor-noise 数据上微调后的检查点，在 LIBERO-Plus 七个扰动维度上评测。目标视觉微调并非局部作用：10 个模型—领域配置中，8 个平均非目标增益为负。例如 Camera-view 微调下，X-VLA 目标提升 69.4 个百分点、其他维度平均下降 4.6 点；OpenVLA-OFT 分别提升 39.3 点和 1.4 点。回退不能仅由无扰动任务性能下降解释；更广训练覆盖提高总体收益但未消除维度回退。DELTA 式特征保持使 X-VLA 非目标均值升至 +1.2 点，却使 OpenVLA-OFT 降至 −23.1 点，显示策略依模型而异。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models transfer knowledge from large-scale pretraining into robot control, but their manipulation performance remains sensitive to changes in observation conditions, which recent work addresses by fine-tuning the visual module on target-domain data. However, it remains unclear how such adaptation affects robustness beyond the target condition, as existing robustness benchmarks only evaluate models without fine-tuning and adaptation studies only report the target gain. Measuring this cross-dimension effect requires separating target gains from off-target changes, isolating adaptation effects from architectural differences, ruling out generic forgetting, and comparing tuning choices. Therefore, we introduce VLA-Retain, a benchmark that compares five open VLA models, tuned on Camera-view or Sensor-noise data, with their own base checkpoints on LIBERO-Plus dataset spanning seven perturbation dimensions. Our study reveals four findings. First, targeted vision tuning is not local, and its target gain does not predict retention. Mean off-target gain is negative in 8 of 10 model–domain configurations. Under Camera-view tuning, X-VLA gains $69.4$ percentage points on the target but loses $4.6$ points on average elsewhere, whereas OpenVLA-OFT gains $39.3$ and $1.4$ points, respectively. Second, these regressions are not explained only by performance loss on the same tasks without perturbations. Third, broader training coverage raises overall gain over target-specific training but does not eliminate dimension-level regressions. Fourth, tuning strategies are model-dependent, as DELTA-style feature preservation raises X-VLA’s mean off-target gain to $+1.2$ points but lowers OpenVLA-OFT’s to $-23.1$. Overall, these findings show that target-domain success alone is insufficient and highlight cross-dimensional retention as a key criterion for targeted VLA adaptation.

Zongji Wang

Details

Motivation: NeRF 用连续体辐射场实现高质量新视角合成，但密度场所隐含的几何可能有噪声或不准确，因为不同形状与辐射组合都能解释相同输入图像。

Result: 作者为标准 NeRF 增加自监督跨射线一致性几何分支：从密度场采样候选表面点，从不同方向生成相交于该点的虚拟射线，并用 NeRF 渲染其外观；依据可见性加权的跨射线颜色一致性约束隐式几何。方法还惩罚外观预测不一致候选点的 opacity，并鼓励局部法线平滑。约束由 NeRF 自身预测得到，不需要深度或表面法线真值。在 Replica 八个场景上，平均 Chamfer 距离较 vanilla NeRF 降低 30.8%。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve high-quality novel-view synthesis by representing a scene as a continuous volumetric radiance field. However, the geometry encoded by the density field of vanilla NeRF can be noisy and inaccurate, since different combinations of shape and radiance may explain the same input images. To reduce this ambiguity, we augment vanilla NeRF with a geometry-optimization branch based on self-supervised cross-ray consistency. Specifically, this branch samples candidate surface points from the density field, generates virtual rays that intersect at each point from different directions, and renders their appearance using the NeRF network. Based on the visibility-weighted color consistency across these rays, we constrain the implicit geometry. We further regularize the implicit geometry by penalizing opacity at candidates with inconsistent appearance predictions and encouraging local normal smoothness. These geometric constraints are derived from the NeRF predictions and require no ground-truth depth or surface-normal supervision. Experiments on eight Replica scenes show that the proposed method reduces the mean Chamfer distance by 30.8% compared with vanilla NeRF.

[82] GARRET: Generative Feedback for Region Retrieval in Visual Documents cs.CV | cs.IRPDF

Weijian Jian, Siyang Chen, Yuguang Yang, Yajun Zhang, Chunyu Xie

TL;DR: GARRET 用可微生成反馈从页面—问题—答案三元组学习细粒度视觉文档区域检索，无需查询—区域标注；在 200,682 区域基准上 macro nDCG@10 达 0.4126。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation over visually rich documents requires evidence at a finer granularity than entire pages. Existing visual retrievers typically index or return full pages, whereas training region-level retrievers often requires costly query-to-region annotations or noisy model-generated labels. We introduce GARRET, a region-retrieval framework trained from (page, question, answer) triplets through differentiable generative feedback. A shared context-preserving retriever produces page and region multi-vector representations. During training, their retrieval scores define a soft routing distribution over candidate region crops. An auxiliary vision-language model attends to each crop separately, aggregates crop-specific contexts using the routing weights, and predicts the question followed by the answer under a next-token prediction objective. Gradients through the routing distribution assign query-conditioned credit to the indexed region representations, without requiring query-to-region annotations. At inference, the auxiliary model is removed. GARRET also reuses the contextualized region representations, together with page summary tokens, to construct a compact page representation. We introduce ViDoRe-V3-Region, a corpus-wide benchmark containing 200,682 fixed candidate regions across eight domains. GARRET achieves a macro nDCG@10 of 0.4126, outperforming the strongest visual baseline by 2.94 points. Result: LaLa-World 是语言条件潜在世界模型，将数值动作和视觉变化都转成语言描述，使单一未来状态预测器能联合利用仿真与机器人轨迹以及更广泛的图像、视频数据；预测器基于语言模型构建，以理解语言条件并预测未来潜在状态。它的平均 MPC 规划性能高于按域或任务分别训练的现有潜在世界模型；加入图像和视频数据还能提升平均规划表现及对动作措辞变化的鲁棒性。与训练条件匹配的 RAEv2 视频生成基线相比，规划表现相当，但训练成本低 6 倍、规划速度快 13.9 倍。预训练模型初始化的 LaLa-Policy 在 LIBERO 和 RoboCasa 上可与 Cosmos Policy 竞争，推理最高快 5.5 倍。

Abstract: This paper shows that JEPA-style latent world models (LWMs) can learn across diverse domains and data sources by adopting the language-conditioned interface of video generation models. We establish this approach as an effective and efficient basis for planning with imagination and downstream policy fine-tuning. To this end, we introduce LaLa-World, a Language-conditioned Latent World model that learns from simulation and robot trajectories together with broader image and video data. Two designs make this work. First, we describe both numerical actions and visual changes in language, enabling joint training of a single future predictor across heterogeneous datasets. Second, we build the predictor on a language model, allowing it to interpret language conditions while predicting future latent states. The resulting predictor achieves higher average MPC planning performance than existing LWMs trained separately for each domain or task. Adding image and video data further improves average planning performance and robustness to action phrasing. In a matched comparison with an RAEv2-based video generation model baseline, LaLa-World reaches comparable planning performance with $6\times$ less training cost and $13.9\times$ faster planning. Beyond planning, the pretrained LaLa-World serves as an initialization for LaLa-Policy, which jointly predicts actions and future visual representations. Abstract: Anticipating how users will interact with their surroundings is essential for proactive systems that provide timely assistance in AR, VR, and embodied applications. Prior work typically forecasts hand-object interactions directly from historical observations, leaving intention and contact geometry implicit. However, similar observation histories can precede semantically or geometrically distinct futures. Inspired by behavioral evidence that gaze can provide early cues for underlying intention, we propose Gaze2HOI, a gaze-aware framework that formulates hand-object interaction forecasting as a hierarchical problem comprising intention inference, contact grounding, and coupled hand-object motion generation. Specifically, Gaze2HOI first weights gaze cues by fixation strength and recency, and uses them to modulate the visual representations of a vision-language model (VLM) through Temporal Gaze Saliency Modulation (TGSM), thereby reducing semantic ambiguity in intention inference. Abstract: Multispectral aerial object detection aims to exploit spectral-spatial cues to address object detection challenges in complex aerial scenes. Existing methods mainly focus on spectral-spatial decoupling and object-aware spectral-spatial fusion to improve spectral utilization. However, two critical issues remain underexplored: 1) severe foreground-background imbalance caused by large scene coverage hinders reliable object recognition; 2) aerial objects exhibit substantial local variations in scale, orientation, and structure, which hinder accurate localization. To address these issues, we propose SGLDet, a spectral-aware global-local dynamic modeling framework for multispectral aerial object detection, which is motivated by the observation that object semantics are jointly determined by global spectral consistency and scene context, while precise localization requires dynamic feature modulation conditioned on the input spectral and spatial properties. Abstract: Endoscopic video contains state transitions induced by camera displacement, deformable tissue, and instrument interaction, yet existing pre-training methods treat dynamics implicitly. Inspired by action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive world models and by the observation that endoscopic optical flow contains both shared image-plane displacement and localized residual motion, we reformulate passive endoscopic video pre-training as causal motion-guided latent-state prediction. We present EndoWorld-JEPA, to our knowledge the first latent world-model pre-training framework designed for general-purpose endoscopic video representation learning. In place of recorded actions, calibrated poses, or robot kinematics, the model conditions prediction of the next latent state on motion observed up to the current state. Its Optical Motion-guided module separates dominant displacement from residual motion and converts the resulting evidence into an ordered temporal motion condition and spatial local-motion weights. Complementary global and motion-local predictors use these outputs: the temporal condition modulates both latent transitions, whereas the local weights define an auxiliary motion-local state that extends prediction from scene-level evolution to spatially selective dynamics. Patch-wise motion weighting emphasizes localized change while retaining anatomical context, and past-motion-window dropout randomly withholds the complete motion condition to reduce dependence on unreliable motion. This global–local formulation learns predictable visual–motion dynamics in representation space without reconstructing stochastic appearance artifacts. Evaluation shows that a common pre-trained checkpoint achieves strong transfer across four downstream benchmarks, supporting motion-guided global–local latent prediction as an effective pre-training paradigm for endoscopic video.

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) ranks an image gallery using either an image query or a textual query. In image-to-image (I2I) ReID, viewpoint, illumination, occlusion, and background changes can make distractors appear more similar than the true match. In text-to-image (T2I) ReID, incomplete or ambiguous descriptions may omit key visual evidence. We study this problem for vision-language model (VLM) based ReID, where attributes such as clothing color and carried objects provide useful fine-grained matching cues. However, directly using attribute-prompt similarities as retrieval scores is unreliable because templated prompts can be biased, correlated, and poorly calibrated. We therefore propose \textbf{PR}ompt-guided \textbf{A}ttribute-based \textbf{I}nference-time \textbf{S}imilarity \textbf{E}nhancement (\texttt{PRAISE}), a query-side test-time adaptation framework for VLM-based ReID. Instead of treating prompts as scores, \texttt{PRAISE} uses a transferable attribute-prompt bank to construct an \emph{Attribute-Conditioned Subspace} (ACS): a query-dependent low-rank subspace induced by the most compatible attribute prompts. ACS provides a controlled attribute-conditioned geometry for reshaping query–gallery affinities and guiding lightweight inference-time updates without identity or attribute labels. Experiments across multiple I2I- and T2I-ReID benchmarks show that \texttt{PRAISE} consistently improves VLM retrieval backbones and outperforms both generic and ReID-specific TTA baselines.

Abstract: Egocentric action transfer aims to decouple the semantic action from egocentric observations and reproduce them in new visual contexts, enabling scalable world simulation, embodied policy learning, and robotic data generation. However, these methods introduce geometric estimation errors, making it difficult to preserve physically plausible interactions when transferred to new scenes. Details

Motivation: 视频分割中的类别遗忘要求部署模型输出无法重构已删除类别。现有泄漏评测逐帧检查，可能忽略多帧输出聚合后暴露的信号，甚至在单帧预测看似与从头训练模型一致时仍存在恢复风险。

Result: 研究显示，在 VSPW 及多数 Virtual KITTI 2 方法中，攻击者对两秒片段输出场进行平均即可恢复被遗忘类别；时间打乱控制仍保留泄漏，说明信号主要来自跨帧冗余而非物体轨迹。作者提出 PACE，以严格置换不变的池化对抗判别器和双向时间一致性目标直接抑制冗余。在 VSPW 上，池化攻击者对 person 和 car 的 AP 分别降至 18.79 和 12.10；Virtual KITTI 2 上 car 为 9.11。后者在保留可用效用且通过判定安全的方案中抑制最强；VSPW car 的 mIoU 保持 19.8，而达到相似遗忘效果的基线最高仅 8.3。

Insight: 逐帧看不出明显差异，并不代表视频级信息已被遗忘；跨帧冗余可在时间聚合后泄露类别线索。评估需使用时序攻击者，遗忘方法也应针对输出冗余进行优化，并同时报告删除效果与剩余任务效用。

Abstract: Class unlearning for video segmentation aims to ensure that a removed category cannot be reconstructed from a deployed model’s outputs. Current leakage evaluation algorithms inspect one frame at a time, and we show that this understates leakage by a wide margin: an attacker that averages a model’s output field over a two-second clip recovers the forgotten class from every method we evaluate on VSPW and most on Virtual KITTI2, including models whose per-frame predictions are visually indistinguishable from a retrain oracle (a model trained from scratch without the forgotten class). We attribute the recoverable signal to cross-frame redundancy rather than object trajectory, establish this with a time-shuffled control that leaves leakage intact, and introduce PACE, an unlearning method that suppresses redundancy directly using an exactly permutation-invariant pooled adversarial discriminator and a two-sided temporal consistency objective. Across VSPW and Virtual KITTI2, PACE lowers pooled attacker AP to $18.79$ (\texttt{person}) and $12.10$ (\texttt{car}) on VSPW and $9.11$ (\texttt{car}) on Virtual KITTI~2, achieving on the latter the strongest suppression among verdict-safe methods retaining usable utility, while retaining $19.8$ mIoU on VSPW \texttt{car} against at most $8.3$ for any baseline achieving comparable erasure.

TL;DR: ReCam 将完整历史视频潜变量按局部时空块压缩成可被 DiT 注意力访问的记忆 token，并用双指针去噪减轻自回归时间漂移；VBench-Long 总分 80.1，离屏主体重现一致性指标均领先。

Details

Motivation: 长时视频生成要保留不断增长的历史，但直接使用整段 token 序列成本高；限制或压缩历史上下文又可能丢失未来生成所需信息。自回归展开还容易累积时间漂移。

Result: ReCam 把历史潜变量的局部时空块分组成向量，直接映射到扩散 Transformer 的高维隐藏空间，形成数量较少的记忆 token。模型通过原生自注意力使用这些 token，降低 token 数同时保留对完整历史各块的访问。为缓解漂移，作者提出双指针去噪，为近期上下文和目标帧设置不同噪声日程。在评估方法中，ReCam 的 VBench-Long 总分最高，为 80.1；在 R2M-Bench 静态场景重访上有竞争力，并在主体离屏后重现的四项一致性指标全部领先，其中服装一致性 4.09，最佳基线为 3.03。

Insight: 长视频记忆需要同时控制序列长度和保留历史访问能力；按时空块形成记忆 token 可以压缩表示但不只保留最近片段。将近期上下文与目标帧使用不同去噪日程，有助于减少自回归生成的时间漂移。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation requires retaining an ever-expanding history, but conditioning on its growing token sequence is costly. Existing methods restrict or compress historical context, potentially losing information needed for future generation. This work proposes ReCam, which groups local spatiotemporal blocks of historical latents into vectors and projects them directly into the high-dimensional hidden space of a Diffusion Transformer. The model learns to use the resulting memory tokens through native self-attention, reducing token count while retaining block-level access to the full history. To mitigate temporal drift during autoregressive rollout, we further introduce dual-pointer denoising with separate noise schedules for recent context and target frames. Among the evaluated methods, ReCam achieves the highest VBench-Long overall score of 80.1 and competitive static-scene revisitation performance on R2M-Bench. Abstract: Moving from controllable videos to genuinely playable worlds requires persistent world state, executable game rules, and low-latency interaction. Abstract: VLMs for medical image interpretation are typically evaluated on clean inputs, despite the progressive evidence loss that can occur in high stakes clinical settings. Existing robustness studies show that performance can degrade while confidence remains high, but do not establish when a previously reliable model-case pair ceases to behave reliably. We introduce a baseline-anchored framework that traces baseline-correct cases through progressive image degradation to identify an empirical evidence boundary, and define mirage as continued commitment at this boundary, regardless of correctness. Across three VLMs on three medical datasets, only MedGemma provides a sufficiently large baseline-correct pool for trajectory analysis. On VQA-RAD, the only dataset in which the question text varies, language-only answerability is associated with baseline correctness (AUROC 0.677) and with commitment at the boundary (AUROC 0.653 to 0.670). Abstract: Examining existing benchmarks for grounding in 3D VLMs reveals a pervasive “semantic leakage” in the evaluation and prompting protocols: a heavy reliance on bounding boxes as spatial prompts or representation primitives. P$^3$-VLM utilizes three complementary pathways (location-, task-, and context-aware), enabling strong reasoning about scenes without any bounding box dependencies. The key benefit of point-based learning is a consistent improvement across settings: P$^3$-VLM achieves state-of-the-art performance under point-based grounding while also improving robustness, OOD generalization, and performance on ungrounded scene-centric 3D VQA benchmarks, reflecting its reduced reliance on bounding-box shortcuts and resulting stronger visual-spatial reasoning.

Result: 作者在 CUB-200 上提出紧凑的可解释性比较框架，对比分类监督和图注生成监督编码器，并采用两阶段控制协议，最终让两者都优化相同分类任务。通过 attention rollout 和梯度加权 rollout 生成显著图，以五个指标衡量忠实度（正、负扰动）和定位（关键点强调、背景归因、Earth Mover 距离）。跨随机种子和解释方法，图注监督的表示对移除显著区域更敏感、更贴合标注物体部件、较少依赖背景；分类监督对移除低显著区域则略更稳健。Oxford-IIIT Pet 消融在主要指标上呈现一致趋势。

Insight: 不同监督方式可能产生准确率相似但结构特性不同的表示；解释性指标能补充下游分类分数，测出模型实际依据哪些区域。图注监督更重视显著部件，分类监督则对低显著区域移除略稳健，说明训练目标决定了不同的表示偏好。

Abstract: Understanding how supervision signals shape semantic visual representations remains challenging, particularly in fine-grained, low-data settings. Standard evaluations of vision models rely on downstream accuracy, which often fails to capture structural differences in learned representations. We propose a compact explainability-driven framework for comparing encoders trained with classification-only and caption-generation supervision on CUB-200. Using a controlled two-stage protocol in which both encoders are ultimately optimized for the same classification task, we enable a direct comparison of the resulting representations. Saliency maps are generated using attention rollout and gradient-weighted rollout and evaluated with five metrics covering faithfulness (positive and negative perturbation) and localization (keypoint emphasis, background attribution, and Earth Mover’s Distance). Across random seeds and explanation methods, caption supervision yields representations that are more sensitive to the removal of salient regions, better aligned with annotated object parts, and less reliant on background regions, whereas classification supervision produces representations that are slightly more robust to the removal of low-saliency regions. An ablation study on the Oxford-IIIT Pet dataset shows consistent trends across key metrics. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework makes supervision-induced representational differences observable and quantifiable beyond downstream accuracy alone. More broadly, it provides a systematic basis for assessing learned representations and comparing alternative training and transfer-learning strategies.

Details

Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型希望通过“弯曲的喙”等概念让人检查和纠正分类，但常规 CUB 基准只测类别和概念准确率，无法说明类别预测是否来自公开概念分数，也不能确认概念是否由对应视觉证据而非物种共现特征支持。

Result: 作者审计 CUB-200-2011 上的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）和概念嵌入模型（CEM）。标注噪声较大：类别级训练标签与逐图概念标注仅在约 82% 图像—概念对上一致；仅用逐图概念标注训练的分类器物种准确率约 37%。泄漏探针发现 CEM 的预测无法由概念分数重构，缺失类别信息藏在内部嵌入中。消融将概念分数中的类别信息来源归纳为感受野、共享/专属表示、依赖范数的评分和任务监督。将 ResNet50 限制为局部 BagNet patch 后，概念分数可恢复的准确率从 72% 降至 43%，接近真值标注所支持水平；联合训练又提高该分数，但牺牲概念准确率。定位与遮挡实验还显示局部概念分数会用头部代替身体、翅膀或尾部概念，遮挡支持部件时 CEM 分数反应很小。

Insight: 高类别或概念准确率并不代表模型决策可解释；类别信息可能泄漏进概念分数或隐藏嵌入，概念也可能由相关部件代理而非目标证据支撑。CUB 标注噪声较高，因此不应过度解读不同模型的准确率差距，评估需检查证据定位和信息流。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise predictions that humans can inspect and correct through concepts such as “curved beak”. Yet on CUB-200-2011, their standard benchmark, progress is measured like that of ordinary classifiers: by task and concept accuracy. Neither reveals whether the label is predicted from the exposed concept scores, or whether a concept is supported by visual evidence of that concept rather than by co-occurring features such as cues to the species as a whole. We audit where the accuracy of CBMs and Concept Embedding Models (CEMs) on CUB comes from. The CUB annotations are too noisy to explain it: class-level training labels agree with per-image annotations on only ~82% of image–concept pairs, and classifiers trained on per-image annotations reach only ~37% species accuracy. Leakage probes show that CEM predictions cannot be reconstructed from their concept scores; the missing class information sits in internal embeddings. We identify four design axes through which class information can enter concept scores—receptive field, shared versus concept-specific representations, norm-dependent scoring, and task supervision. An ablation shows that most of it enters through non-local features: restricting a ResNet50 backbone to local BagNet patches lowers the accuracy recoverable from concept scores from 72% to 43%, close to what ground-truth annotations support, while joint training raises it again at the cost of concept accuracy. Localization and occlusion show that locality makes grounding measurable but does not guarantee it: under class-level labels, local concept scores use the head as a proxy for body, wing, and tail concepts, and CEM concept scores barely respond to occluding their supporting part. Task and concept accuracy should thus support, not drive, the evaluation of CBMs, and given the noisy annotations, CUB accuracy gaps should not be overinterpreted.

Jarek Liesen, Matthew T. Jackson, Benjamin Ellis, Shimon Whiteson, Jakob N. Foerster

Motivation: 高质量扩散 Transformer 的瓶颈逐渐转向模型吞吐。自然图像常有大面积背景，许多 token 对场景贡献小、容易去噪，但标准 DiT 仍对所有 token 静态、均匀地执行计算，没有利用图像稀疏性。

Result: MoDDiT 在架构上学习将 token 分别路由通过 MLP 和自注意力模块，让 MLP 的计算分配随 token、图像和去噪时间动态变化。使用 MoDDiT 从头训练图像生成模型时，训练和推理计算成本显著下降，最稀疏设置仅处理 12.5% token；提高稀疏度与模型深度后，相比不路由基线可降低成本并改善生成表现。学习到的分配可解释地反映视觉内容和噪声水平。方法也用于视频生成，路由分配与运动相关，并用于把稠密双向视频模型蒸馏成稀疏自回归学生。

Insight: 不同视觉区域及去噪阶段对计算需求不同，动态 token 路由可把算力集中在更重要或更难处理的部分。该机制不仅节省图像生成计算，也与视频运动结构相关，并可支持从稠密双向模型向稀疏自回归模型蒸馏。

Abstract: Real-time video generation, interactive world models, and on-device image synthesis are almost a reality. Diffusion Transformers (DiT) achieve excellent sample quality, shifting the bottleneck to the throughput of the models themselves. Natural images are inherently sparse, as large portions of an image are often background, contribute little to the scene content, and are easy to denoise. DiTs fail to exploit this sparsity, as they process image tokens statically and uniformly. We propose MoDDiT, a simple architecture modification that learns to route tokens through both MLP and self-attention operations. By routing tokens through both blocks independently, MoDDiT allows for dynamic compute allocation in MLPs that varies across tokens, images, and denoising timesteps. Using MoDDiT to train image generation models from scratch significantly reduces computational costs of training and inference, processing as few as 12.5% of the tokens. By increasing sparsity and model depth, MoDDiT reduces costs and improves generation performance over unrouted baselines. The learned allocation is interpretable, revealing how computation varies with visual content and noise level. We also use MoDDiT to train video generation models, where allocation correlates with motion, and to distill a dense bidirectional video model into a sparse autoregressive student.

Abstract: Variable voxel spacing changes more than image resolution: it alters the discrete sampling lattice, tensor geometry, and anatomical evidence available to a 3D segmentation network. We therefore propose LatticeRank, a training-only framework that aligns candidate posteriors in physical space and allocates consistency supervision by voxel-spacing order: among endpoint-matched candidates, the auxiliary loss concentrates on the coarsest requested grid, while inference retains a single network forward with no candidate branch or ensemble. Abstract: Feedforward reconstruction of dynamic driving scenes requires integrating observations across multiple views and time steps into a coherent 3D representation. PointForward established state-of-the-art feedforward driving reconstruction by lifting image observations into sparse 3D queries, demonstrating the effectiveness of point-aligned scene representations. However, these queries are largely processed independently, limiting information exchange across space and time, while direct global attention among tens of thousands of point queries is computationally prohibitive. In this work, we present \textbf{PointForward V2}, which advances point-aligned representation to point-aligned interaction. Our key idea is to mediate query interaction through compact, scene-structured registers. For each dynamic instance, queries from different frames are aligned in an object-centric canonical space and interact through instance-specific Dynamic Instance Registers. We further employ 4D positional encoding that combines aligned spatial coordinates with original timestamps, enabling spatially aligned yet temporally distinguishable interactions. Abstract: Smartphone clinical images provide a low-cost data source for computer-aided skin lesion classification in resource-constrained settings, but images alone cannot fully capture patients’ medical histories and lesion symptoms. TL;DR: CALM 先离线标定自回归视频模型各注意力层—头的合并风险，再按推理时缓存预算动态分配 token 合并比例；三个模型上同等内存预算下，视频 LPIPS 相对原视频约降低 21%。

Details

Motivation: token 合并可将时序冗余 token 聚类并保留少量代表，降低自回归视频生成的 KV 缓存占用。但现有方法通常对所有层和注意力头使用相同比例，而不同层头对合并敏感度不同且相互相关。

Result: CALM（层—头合并校准分配）通过离线校准改进在线缓存压缩。由于穷举所有层头预算分配代价过高，作者发现注意力输出误差与端到端视频质量强相关，可作为分配风险的轻量代理；据此评估策略并构造固定层头风险图。推理时给定缓存预算，CALM 使用动态规划确定各层头的合并比例。在三种自回归视频模型上，同等内存预算下均优于均匀 token 合并：生成视频相对原视频的 LPIPS 约降低 21%，且视频质量分数更高。

Insight: 不同层头对 token 合并的容忍度既不相同也并非独立，因此统一合并比例会浪费缓存预算或损害质量。离线风险标定把昂贵的质量评估转成轻量代理，在线再依据缓存约束求解分配。

Abstract: Token merging is an effective approach to reducing KV-cache memory consumption in autoregressive video generation by clustering temporally redundant tokens and retaining only a small set of representatives. Existing methods, however, typically apply a uniform merging ratio across all attention layers and heads. We observe that different layers and heads exhibit substantially different yet jointly correlated sensitivities to token merging, and that these sensitivity patterns remain consistent across prompts. Based on this observation, we introduce CALM, a Calibrated Allocation for Layer-Head Merging that uses offline calibration to improve online cache compression. Exhaustively evaluating all possible layer-head allocations is prohibitively expensive. To enable efficient calibration, we show that attention-output error strongly correlates with end-to-end video quality and can therefore serve as a lightweight proxy for allocation risk assessment. We evaluate different allocation strategies using this proxy and construct a fixed layer-head risk map offline. Given a cache budget at inference time, CALM uses dynamic programming to determine the best allocation of merging ratios. Experiments across three different autoregressive video models demonstrate that CALM consistently outperforms uniform token merging under the same memory budget, by reducing the LPIPS to original videos by around 21% with higher video-quality scores.

Mayimunah Nagayi, Alice Scaria Khan, Tamryn Frank, Rina Swart, Clement Nyirenda

TL;DR: 该工作流从食品包装照片检测营养、配料和产品身份区域，以 OCR 与结构化抽取执行南非草案 R3337 规则；65 个验证产品中，选择性 VLM 复核将覆盖率从 51.7% 提升至 65.0%，准确率保持 97.4%。

Details

Motivation: 依据南非草案 R3337 对食品包装照片筛查警示，需要营养成分、配料和产品形态等多类证据。证据不完整、冲突或不确定时，强行自动判定可能产生不安全遗漏或错误警示。

Insight: 将检测、证据抽取、确定性规则和选择性 VLM 复核分层，可在提高自动处理覆盖率的同时保留弃答机制。高总体准确率并不意味着恢复证据全都正确，因此需单独审计证据级错误，并在不确定时允许人工复核。

Abstract: Applying South Africa’s draft R3337 warning criteria to food-package photographs requires nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. This study presents a region-guided evidence extraction and abstention-aware screening workflow for draft R3337. A three-class detector localises nutrition information panels, ingredient lists and product-identity regions, after which PaddleOCR and structured extraction recover nutrient values, ingredient evidence and product-form information. Recovered evidence is aggregated and assessed using deterministic draft R3337 rules. When evidence is incomplete, uncertain or conflicting, the workflow abstains through Review or Insufficient evidence rather than forcing an automatic outcome. For qualifying unresolved cases, a selective vision-language model (VLM) review stage using Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct reassesses unresolved evidence. Accepted evidence is merged and the same deterministic rules are reapplied. The workflow was applied to 442 products represented by 3,929 images. Evaluation used a manual validation set of 65 products. Before selective VLM review, 31 of 60 decidable products received automatic outcomes and 30 were correct, giving 51.7% coverage and 96.8% accuracy among automatic outcomes (selective accuracy). Selective VLM review resolved eight additional products, increasing coverage to 65.0%. Of 39 automatic outcomes, 38 were correct, giving 97.4% selective accuracy. The single false warning was retained from the rules-only workflow, while no unsafe warning omissions occurred after selective review. Evidence-level evaluation nevertheless identified incorrect VLM-recovered nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. These findings show that selective VLM review can recover unresolved evidence, while deterministic screening and explicit abstention remain necessary for reliable image-based draft R3337 screening.

Result: 作者分析以不同目标和领域预训练的四种 ViT-B/16：DINOv3、SigLIP2、MerMED-FM 和 BiomedCLIP。它们用 LoRA 适配脑肿瘤分割，再在六个数据集上评估分割及阿尔茨海默病分类，并结合注意力散度、权重变化和激活 patching 分析。四者分割均值 Dice 相近，为 78–81%，但迁移方向不同：适配提升 DINOv3 的阿尔茨海默病分类，却损害其余三者。修改幅度最大层并非因果关键层，早中层已足以恢复分割。仅在这些层使用 LoRA 可匹配或接近全层分割表现，同时减轻三个受影响骨干的分类退化。

Abstract: Adapting Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to medical imaging via LoRA has become standard practice, yet model selection is guided almost entirely by downstream task metrics, treating backbones with similar performance as interchangeable. We investigate whether equal performance implies equal internal representational shift. We analyse four ViT-B/16 backbones, DINOv3, SigLIP2, MerMED-FM, and BiomedCLIP, pre-trained with different objectives (self-distillation vs. contrastive vision-language) and on different data domains (natural vs. medical images). We adapt each to brain tumour segmentation and evaluate on six datasets covering both segmentation and Alzheimer’s disease classification. Using attention divergence, weight-change analysis, and activation patching, we find that the four models converge to similar segmentation performance (mean Dice 78-81%) through markedly different internal dynamics, yet diverge in cross-task transfer: segmentation adaptation improves Alzheimer’s disease classification for DINOv3 but degrades it for the other three backbones. We reveal a dissociation between modification magnitude and causal importance: the most heavily modified layers are not the causally relevant ones, with early-to-mid layers identified as sufficient for segmentation recovery. Restricting LoRA to these layers matches or approaches full-layer segmentation while reducing classification degradation in the three affected backbones, providing a mechanistically grounded strategy that is more parameter-efficient and more robust than standard full-layer adaptation.

Runmin Zhang, Binglin Ren, Zhu Yu, Fudong Wang, Jiangke Lin

TL;DR: SplatFlux 将观察视频与条件生成内容统一映射到预训练视频潜空间，再前馈解码为 4D Gaussian；静动态解耦表示和蒸馏伪标签帮助构建时序一致场景，在重建与生成基准上达到 SOTA。

Details

Motivation: 视频 4D 重建和条件 4D 生成常被分开处理，但观察视频与条件生成内容都可表示于同一预训练视频潜空间。如何从该潜表示高效构建紧凑、时序连贯的 4D 场景是关键。

Result: SplatFlux 是前馈潜变量到 4D Gaussian Splatting 框架，同时支持视频 4D 重建和条件生成。它采用静态—动态解耦的 4D Gaussian 表示，为每个高斯原语显式预测动态概率，从而分离静态结构与运动区域，并通过静态聚合和动态运动插值前馈构建完整 4D 场景。为避免昂贵标注，作者设计辅助蒸馏策略生成动态掩码和光流伪线索，以学习静动态分解与动态高斯运动。多项 3D/4D 重建和生成实验显示，SplatFlux 达到最先进表现。

Insight: 共享视频潜空间允许把重建和条件生成统一为同一解码问题；显式动态概率再将稳定背景与运动区域拆开处理。伪动态掩码和光流蒸馏降低了时空分解对人工标注的依赖。

Abstract: This work presents SplatFlux, a feed-forward latent-to-4DGS framework for both video-based 4D reconstruction and conditional 4D generation. Our key observation is that observed videos and conditionally generated content can be represented in the same pretrained video latent space, allowing both tasks to be formulated as latent-to-4DGS decoding. To construct compact and temporally coherent 4D scenes, SplatFlux introduces a static-dynamic decoupled 4D Gaussian representation that explicitly predicts dynamic probabilities for Gaussian primitives. This representation separates static scene structures from moving regions, enabling complete 4D scene construction in a feed-forward manner through static aggregation and dynamic motion interpolation. To learn static-dynamic decomposition and dynamic Gaussian motion without costly annotations, we further develop an auxiliary distillation strategy that provides pseudo dynamic masks and optical-flow cues. Extensive experiments across 3D and 4D reconstruction and generation demonstrate that SplatFlux achieves state-of-the-art performance.

To support Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis (OSVEA), we construct the Oddly Satisfying Video Dataset (OSVD), comprising 12,000 annotated 5-second clips. Building on OSVD, we propose PI-MLLM, a psychophysics-inspired multimodal large language model that integrates Modality-Specific Dynamics Alignment (MSDA) and Coalition-Based Audio-Visual Attribution (CAVA). Experiments show that PI-MLLM improves both viewer-side emotion prediction and sensory-grounded explanation quality, suggesting the value of physical sensory cues to video emotion analysis.

Result: ABACUS 提供数字图像编辑基准、协议和专用评分指标。评测不让 VLM/MLLM 为编辑打分或数物体，仅在罕见歧义裁剪图的类别确认中使用 VLM；实例级协议将编辑结果与经检测器共识确认的全景真值核对，分别检查 add、remove、change 操作是否达到准确目标数量，以及其余场景是否保持。对领先图像编辑器的评测发现，数量编辑失败显著，且随场景复杂度和物体数量增加而恶化；既有基准常未发现或误判这些错误。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models can now add, remove a specified number of objects or change their category while preserving the scene. Yet these models often fail to produce the correct number, and these failures grow sharply as scenes become more complex and the requested count increases. Current benchmarks largely miss this problem: they score edits with holistic similarity metrics or with vision-language model (VLM) judges that are themselves poor at counting, so numerical errors go unnoticed or are graded incorrectly. As a result, the true numerical ability of today’s editing models has never been accurately measured. We address this issue with ABACUS, a new benchmark, evaluation protocol and dedicated scoring metric for numerical image editing. Our protocol is judge-free: no VLM or MLLM scores an edit or counts objects (a VLM only confirms the category of rare ambiguous crops). Instead, it verifies each edit at the instance level against panoptic ground truth confirmed by detector consensus, checking both that the exact requested count was achieved and that the rest of the scene was preserved, across the add, remove and change operations. [114] PACE: Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit for Training-Free Response Timing in Streaming Video Understanding cs.CVPDF

Siqi Guo, Yuxia Chen, Wenbo Zhu, Yangguang Ji, Zhenxiang Jiang

TL;DR: PACE 为流式视频问答提供免训练的回答时机控制：先规划需要观察的事件，再检查答案稳定性、引用帧支持度和事件是否结束；在 ProactiveVideoQA 与 OVO-Bench Forward 上提升 PAUC 和准确率。

Details

Motivation: 流式视频理解不仅要回答什么，还要决定何时回答。证据逐步到达，事件尚未结束就提前作答是常见错误。现有方法常需额外训练；免训练方法又多依赖单一标量门控，未明确表示答案何时才算最终成立。

Result: PACE 将流式错误分为三类：采样变化导致答案不稳定；答案稳定但当前视频证据不支持；当前证据支持答案但事件尚未结束。框架无需训练，先为问题制定观察计划和完成信号；视频流推进时，在信息量较高的时刻调用现成视频 LLM，并检查草稿稳定性、引用帧落地情况及事件完成状态，将核验后的观察记入事件账本。在 ProactiveVideoQA 和 OVO-Bench Forward 上，跨专有及开源骨干模型，相较官方协议最高提升平均 PAUC 5.9 点、平均准确率 6.2 点，达到最先进表现。

Insight: 回答稳定不代表已有充分视觉依据，依据充足也不代表事件已经结束；流式系统应分别检查这三种条件。先规划观测目标，再以引用帧核验和事件完成状态决定输出时机，可以避免只依赖答案置信度过早回应。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires a model to decide not only what to answer but also when to answer. Evidence arrives progressively, and recent benchmarks identify premature responses, issued before the queried event has concluded, as a recurring failure mode. Existing approaches often learn response timing through additional training, while training-free alternatives typically rely on scalar gating signals that do not explicitly represent when an answer becomes final. We organize chunk-wise streaming errors into three patterns: sampling variation can make an answer unstable, a stable answer may lack support in the observed video, or an answer may be supported by the current evidence while the event remains unfinished. We propose PACE (Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit), a training-free framework designed around these three failure modes. Given a question, PACE drafts a plan specifying what to observe and what will signal completion. As the stream unfolds, it invokes an off-the-shelf video LLM at informative moments, evaluates each draft for stability, grounding in its cited frames, and completion, and records verified observations in an event ledger. Experiments on ProactiveVideoQA and OVO-Bench Forward show that PACE improves over the official protocols by up to 5.9 average PAUC points and 6.2 average accuracy points across proprietary and open-source backbones, yielding state-of-the-art performance.

Details

Insight: 在缺失类别上借用相关类别视觉知识可提升召回，但无约束迁移容易造成负迁移。以语义相关性和残差一致性作为证据门，能大幅降低负迁移风险，同时带来少量支持类性能代价。

Abstract: Realistic few-shot adaptation of medical vision-language models (VLMs) can leave low-prevalence classes without any labeled support. The training-free SS- Text+ solver remains operational in this setting, but its zero-support prototypes receive no visual adaptation and fall back to a purely textual class prototype. We in- troduce Gated Knowledge-Guided Prototype Transfer (GProtoT), a pre-repulsion module that transfers supported-class visual residuals to missing classes using VLM similarity and an ontology-based clinical distance. A validation-free evidence gate scales transfer based on semantic relevance and residual agreement, thereby limiting unsupported extrapolation. In 50 paired realistic one-shot trials on MESSIDOR with FLAIR features, severe and proliferative diabetic retinopathy were absent from every support set. These results indicate that evidence-based gating provides a safety mechanism for the knowledge source. GProtoT is based on SS- Text+ and requires no gradients, validation data, or additional model inference, of- fering a low-compute path toward accountable adaptation under incomplete clini- cal supervision. [117] RayCo4D: Ray-Conditioned Continuous Motion Model for Monocular 4D Dynamic Reconstruction cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yuanxiang Ni, Xianliang Huang, Ruihan Hou, Feng Xie, Hao Zhang

TL;DR: RayCo4D 将单目轨迹观测与潜在 3D 运动状态分开建模，用相机射线约束参考时刻点查询，并以连续时间 SE(3) 状态和加速度先验融合间断轨迹；在快速运动和缺失轨迹下提升时空一致性。

Details

Motivation: 从单目视频重建动态 3D 场景需要从噪声且间歇可见的观测推断运动。长程轨迹可提供几何约束，但如何将覆盖零散的观测整合为共享、连续的运动仍困难。

Result: RayCo4D 将视觉测量与潜在三维运动状态显式分离。每条轨迹关联一个受相机射线约束的参考时刻 3D 查询；图像与深度观测通过局部多对多关联约束共享潜在运动节点。各节点在稀疏支持时刻维护 SE(3) 位姿和物体坐标系速度，并用加速度白噪声过程先验耦合相邻状态、定义连续时间插值，使跨可见性区间的观测共同约束同一状态序列。推断出的共享运动通过双四元数混合传递给高斯原语，参考时刻锚定的局部 SE(3) 残差再表示超出共享骨架的细粒度非刚性形变。多样单目动态场景实验显示，方法新视角合成质量有竞争力，并在快速运动和轨迹不完整时提升结构及时间一致性，支持连续时间渲染。

Insight: 通过共享潜在运动节点连接间断轨迹，可让不同可见区间共同约束连续时序状态；加速度过程先验则提供物理平滑的连续时间插值。将共享运动与局部形变分开，有利于兼顾场景整体一致性和非刚性细节。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from monocular video requires inferring motion from noisy and intermittently visible observations. Long-range tracks provide valuable geometric constraints, but integrating their fragmented temporal coverage into shared, continuous motion remains challenging. We introduce \textbf{RayCo4D}, a ray-conditioned continuous motion model for monocular 4D reconstruction that explicitly separates visual measurements from latent 3D motion states. Each track is associated with a reference-time 3D query constrained to its camera ray, and its image and depth observations constrain shared latent motion nodes through local many-to-many associations. Each node maintains an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ pose and body-frame velocity at sparse support times. A white-noise-on-acceleration process prior couples neighboring states and defines continuous-time interpolation, allowing observations across visibility intervals to jointly constrain the same latent state sequence.The inferred shared motion is transferred to Gaussian primitives through dual-quaternion blending, while reference-anchored local $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ residuals capture fine-scale non-rigid deformation beyond the shared scaffold. Experiments on diverse monocular dynamic scenes show that RayCo4D achieves competitive novel-view synthesis quality, improves structural and temporal consistency under rapid motion and incomplete tracks, and supports continuous-time rendering.

[118] CountVideoBench: A Benchmark to Evaluate Prediction Biases on the Object Counting task by Video-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Marc Chapus, Carlos Crispim-Junior, Véronique Eglin, Atilla Baskurt

TL;DR: CountVideoBench 用 2,600 段静态物体、受控相机运动视频覆盖 5–50 共 26 种计数，发现 16 个视频语言模型准确率均不超过 27.6%，且各模型偏好不同整数。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频问答基准虽包含计数题，但真值数量严重偏向小数值：九个基准的中位数最多为 5，三个最常见数值合计占 45%–93%。因此均值准确率被常见数量主导，稀有数量上的系统偏差不易显现。

Result: CountVideoBench 收录 2,600 段合成视频，覆盖区间 [5,50] 中 26 种数量，每种 100 段；按相邻数量分为六组，便于近邻比较。每段只含一类静止物体和受控相机运动，以排除物体运动并降低识别混淆。作者零样本评测九个架构家族的 16 个视频语言模型，没有模型 Exact Match 超过 27.6%。不同模型的准确率随数量变化形式不一，从单调下降到先升后崩溃，说明数量增大带来的难度不是失败的唯一主因；每个模型还把预测集中在自己偏好的整数上，准确率峰值出现在这些数字，邻近数量上则骤降。

Insight: 均衡覆盖数量并设计相邻数量对照，才能揭示常见数值先验导致的计数偏差。模型的偏好整数各不相同，且准确率曲线不都随数量单调下降，因此不能把计数失败简单归结为数量越大越难。

Abstract: Object counting in videos remains a poorly characterized capability of multimodal large language models. Counting questions are included in many existing VideoQA benchmarks, but the ground-truth count distributions in these benchmarks are heavily skewed toward small values. Across the nine benchmarks we survey, the median ground-truth count is at most five, and the three most frequent values together account for between 45% and 93% of the videos. The resulting mean accuracy is then dominated by the most frequent counts; a value-dependent bias at a rare count contributes negligibly to the average and remains invisible. We introduce CountVideoBench, a benchmark of 2,600 synthetic videos covering 26 cardinalities in the interval [5, 50] (100 videos each), grouped into six clusters of consecutive counts that span the range while enabling near-neighbor comparisons. Each video contains a single class of static objects under a controlled camera motion, removing object motion and reducing recognition as confounds. We evaluate sixteen video-language models from nine architecture families in a zero-shot setting. No model exceeds 27.6% Exact Match. The dependence of accuracy on cardinality varies across models, ranging from monotonic decline to a non-monotonic peak-and-collapse pattern, which rules out increasing difficulty with cardinality as the dominant explanation of counting failure. Every model further concentrates its predictions on its own set of preferred integers, with accuracy peaks coinciding with these values and collapsing at adjacent counts. These findings highlight the need for balanced, diagnostic counting benchmarks to reliably assess this fundamental visual capability.

[119] RACE: Learning Multimodal Search through Referential Abstraction and Compositional Evidence cs.CVPDF

Eric Tao, Minhao Sun, Liyan Liu, Zhijing Wu, Xiaohan Yu

TL;DR: RACE 用图像条件化任务合成建立“图像—目标—答案”依赖，并通过指代抽象和组合证据迫使搜索智能体查看视觉证据；仅用 12K 生成轨迹微调后，十个多模态基准平均准确率由 53.5 升至 56.0。

Details

Motivation: 训练多模态搜索智能体需要任务本身确实依赖视觉搜索。合成问题中的文本身份线索和未用上的目标证据可能让模型走捷径，削弱视觉搜索和证据整合学习。

Result: RACE 是图像条件任务合成框架，显式构建图像到目标、目标特定证据到答案的依赖。指代抽象将泄露身份的文字替换为具体视觉指示或需要搜索候选后才能解析的模糊关系指称；组合证据则将一张图中的多个实体事实或跨图实体事实连成联合问题，要求答案整合所有指定贡献。搜索中新发现的图像也可决定后续搜索目标或提供答案证据。仅用 RACE 生成的 12,000 条轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-27B，十个多模态基准平均准确率从 53.5 提升至 56.0，相对匹配基础智能体约提升 4.7%。

Insight: 任务监督必须让答案依赖所找到的图像及目标证据，才能避免模型靠文本线索绕过搜索。指代抽象和组合证据分别强化“找到正确实体”和“汇总多项依据”，且少量合成轨迹即可改善多个基准上的表现。

Abstract: Training multimodal search agents requires tasks that make visually grounded search necessary for determining the answer. Textual identity clues and unused target evidence allow shortcuts in synthetic questions, weakening supervision for visual search and evidence integration. We propose RACE, an image-conditioned task synthesis framework that constructs dependencies from images to targets and from target-specific evidence to answers. Referential Abstraction (RA) replaces identity-revealing text with specific visual references or ambiguous relational references resolved by searching among depicted candidates. Compositional Evidence (CE) links retrieved facts about multiple entities in one image or entities from different images into joint questions whose answers require every designated contribution. Both operations also apply to images found during search, allowing new visual observations to determine subsequent search targets or supply answer evidence. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-27B on only 12K teacher-generated trajectories from RACE raises average accuracy across ten multimodal benchmarks from 53.5 to 56.0, a relative improvement of approximately 4.7% over the matched base agent. Our code, data, and model will be publicly available.

[120] VisualEvolve: A Skill Self-Evolution Framework for Visual Reasoning cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Abstract: Visual tool use enables vision-language models (VLMs) to acquire task-relevant evidence, but its effectiveness can be limited by predefined visual operations and the tool-use strategies learned during training. Representing these operations and strategies as reusable external skills allows them to be refined through self-evolution without updating model parameters. However, skill refinement can introduce performance regressions. Meanwhile, inspecting accumulated task execution trajectories together with skill evolution history may lead to evolution evidence overload. To address these challenges, we propose VisualEvolve, a self-evolving framework that evolves executable visual operations and tool-use strategies for VLMs through a Select–Execute–Evolve–Evaluate cycle. Skill-Library Snapshot Selection uses performance on a fixed validation set disjoint from execution tasks to select an effective historical snapshot for subsequent evolution, limiting the propagation of performance regressions. Progressive Evidence Access (PEA) guides capability-gap diagnosis through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy comprising the Skill-Performance Report, Evolution Log, Trajectory Summaries, and relevant raw trajectory segments. Abstract: Deep learning has become the default tool for medical image analysis, driven by the open-source movement. Automated machine learning (AutoML) automates parts of model training, yet helps little in medical imaging: general-purpose approaches lack medical architectures and preprocessing, and medical-focused ones demand engineering expertise and remain confined to narrow tasks such as 3D segmentation. We introduce MedicalMLOmni, a multi-agent AutoML framework that automates model training for diverse single- and multi-modal medical tasks through a natural language interface. Specifically, MedicalMLOmni performs evidence-based planning by retrieving published results to guide model selection and configuration, and employs parallel HPO where multiple agents explore adjustment strategies concurrently. MedicalMLOmni achieves the best score on 19 of 20 benchmarks; PMC-VQA is the exception, where a vision-language model with broader pre-training leads. Backbone-matched controls attribute the gains to the framework, not the backbone. By removing coding requirements, MedicalMLOmni lowers the barrier for domain experts with rich datasets but limited engineering expertise.

Abstract: Code-based video generation offers fine-grained control, targeted editing, and component reuse compared with direct video generation. TL;DR: DISTRACT 用匹配干预测试 VLT 是否真正利用文本目标描述，并交叉控制四种文本先验与四级视觉退化；仅 Molmo2 和 JointNLT 显示定向文本使用证据，且作用依赖较早内部状态。

Abstract: Vision-language trackers (VLTs) accept descriptions intended to assist tracking, but accepting text does not establish that its content changes tracking decisions, particularly as visual evidence deteriorates. We introduce DISTRACT, a matched-intervention benchmark that holds video, target, and distractor fixed while crossing four text priors with four image degradations along a controlled severity axis. We evaluate five VLTs on four datasets using behavioral contrasts, layerwise representation probes, and activation patching. Molmo2 and JointNLT exhibit directed text use. Some effects contract under degradation, whereas others show no significant endpoint change after multiplicity correction. UVLTrack, MMTrack, and DUTrack respond only weakly to changes in text semantics, with no directed behavioral effect surviving FDR correction in the tested settings. Model-internal analyses reveal that at decision-near states, correct-versus-conflicting text is decodable in Molmo2 and JointNLT but not in the other three systems. In the responsive models, decodability peaks at late states where replacement produces little behavioral change, whereas replacing selected earlier states redirects conflict-associated trajectories toward the target. Because matched-correct donors do not significantly outperform unrelated donors, these interventions identify earlier intervention-sensitive states without establishing target-specific semantic rescue. DISTRACT thus exposes when and where text affects tracking under degraded visual evidence.

Abstract: Omni LLMs can naturally locate key audio and visual evidence and integrate information across modalities for audiovisual reasoning. Existing audio-visual reasoning benchmarks that mainly rely on multiple-choice questions usually score models solely based on answer correctness, making it difficult to assess whether a model truly forms a reasoning chain from evidence to the correct answer. In addition, textual or unimodal shortcuts may allow models to answer correctly without analyzing the necessary audio-visual evidence, further weakening the reliability of evaluation benchmarks. To address these issues, we introduce EvaOmni, an evidence-grounded benchmark containing 392 videos and 1,027 purely human-annotated question-answer pairs, covering 15 capabilities and three levels: retrieval and localization, basic understanding, and complex reasoning. Abstract: People adapt how they talk to the person in front of them. However, chat models tend to respond in Standard English regardless of the dialect used by the user. Abstract: Object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) requires an embodied agent to find an instance of a specified category in an unseen environment and stop near it. The agent typically relies on an object detector but must judge online from a limited egocentric video stream, sometimes a single frame, whether a detected candidate is the target. Detectors often confidently mistake similar objects, such as a sofa for a bed, and stopping at a false positive fails the episode. Prevailing systems also approach candidates greedily, leaving them occluded or partially observed. We present CLOSER, an evidence-aware active verification framework that treats stopping as closed-loop evidence acquisition, coupling vision-language model (VLM) verification with active perception. CLOSER fine-tunes an open-source VLM to label each view of a candidate tracked across frames as target, non-target, or uncertain, selects complementary views with an embedding-based diversity gate, and fuses their per-view probabilities with a lightweight network. If the fused judgment remains uncertain, a diffusion policy drives the agent toward the candidate using only the tracked mask and depth until the decision becomes decisive. CLOSER runs locally and plugs into existing ObjectNav systems without retraining them. On Gibson and Habitat-Matterport 3D, it raises average success rate by 3.6 points and success weighted by path length by 2.0 points, showing that verifying evidence before committing improves embodied navigation.

Abstract: Online scene change detection requires integrating incoming observations to determine which regions have changed relative to a reference scene. We update change beliefs as new images arrive using generalised Bayesian evidence fusion. However, the prior probability of change and evidence models are unknown beforehand, and numerical Bayesian inference can be costly. We address these challenges by learning offline from automatic edits to a reference 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model. These edits generate labelled training views without manual annotations. Together with unchanged reference observations, they provide supervision for learning scene-specific evidence models and estimating the effective pixel prior and response statistics. Online prediction combines current evidence with historical support, while closed-form local Bayes factors, parameterized by the learned response statistics, update an accumulated 3D Gaussian field. Object-guided refinement produces the final change masks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on PASLCD and CCS3D, with $67.64%$ and $75.82%$ mIoU, respectively. On PASLCD, the complete pipeline runs at $17.2$,FPS over complete query sequences, the fastest among the evaluated online methods.

Abstract: Smart glasses can help users understand their activities and surroundings, but small vision-language models must preserve useful evidence within limited computation. This is difficult because redundant frames consume the visual budget, while aggressive spatial compression can discard relevant details. Although gaze identifies the wearer’s focus, the question may require evidence from the surrounding scene. To address these constraints, we propose GazeLens, which combines frame selection with gaze- and question-guided token reduction. First, HOG transitions and image sharpness select frames before visual encoding. For each selected frame, Gaussian pooling then forms gaze-focused and complementary summaries. Because their relevance depends on the question, a lightweight gate blends them into a primary token. Together with a global scene token, this retains both focused and contextual summaries within two tokens per frame. On StreamGaze, GazeLens achieves higher macro and micro accuracy than the evaluated full-grid and token-reduction baselines, with lower input-forward FLOPs than the shared-backbone token-reduction baselines.

Motivation: 车道线清晰度和连续性关系到驾驶安全，但维护评估仍常依赖人工。现有自动驾驶数据集主要支持车道检测与场景理解，缺少可用于维护的可见度、连续性和退化标签。

Insight: 面向道路维护的质量指标应测量车道标线本身的对比和连续性，而不是只看通用图像质量。无参考、分割派生的 CSI 与 LCR 可规模化计算；目前与人类排序的一致性验证仅是 50 张图像的试点。

Abstract: Reliable lane markings are important for both human drivers and automated driving systems, but their condition is still commonly assessed through manual inspection. Existing autonomous-driving datasets primarily support lane detection and scene understanding and usually lack maintenance-oriented labels for lane-marking visibility, continuity, or degradation. In this study, we present a geo-referenced smartphone dashcam dataset and baseline framework for lane-marking quality assessment. The dataset contains 63,839 road-facing frames collected across South Texas using a GPS-enabled dashcam application. A subset of 3,339 frames is matched with OpenStreetMap-derived lane-count labels, which are used as weak supervision for lane-count regression and map-consistency evaluation. We propose three lane-quality measures: Contrast Stability Index (CSI), which captures lane-background contrast using Laplacian responses; Line Continuity Ratio (LCR), which estimates structural continuity using edge and Hough-line evidence; and Regression Lane Confidence Score (RLCS), which measures agreement between predicted and map-derived lane counts on matched frames. The baseline prediction models attained strong performance, with CSI (MAE $0.039$, $R^2=0.97$) and LCR (MAE $0.093$, $R^2=0.84$). Notably, CSI and LCR are no-reference, segmentation-derived quality metrics that can be computed without dense human labels. In a pilot validation using 50 human-ranked images, these proposed lane-specific metrics show stronger rank agreement with human lane-quality ordering (e.g., Spearman’s $ρ=0.7316$ and Kendall’s $τ=0.5472$ for LCR) than general image-quality measures such as PSNR, SSIM, BRISQUE, NIQE, and LPIPS. Abstract: Tactile-visual-language models combine vision and touch through a shared language space to recognize objects and materials. In real-world deployment, changes in lighting, contact quality or sensor hardware can shift visual or tactile inputs and degrade recognition performance. When both modalities contribute to a prediction, an unreliable modality can override correct evidence from the other. Since labeled data are generally unavailable during deployment, test-time adaptation offers a way to recover recognition performance from unlabeled observations. In this setting, we find that comparing accumulated observations with the text embeddings of candidate classes reveals modality reliability differences that confidence can miss. Refining features against competing classes also improves recognition beyond geometric alignment alone. These findings motivate SenseAlign, which uses a common semantic reference for test-time modality weighting and feature correction. Alignment Trust weights each modality according to its support for shared predicted classes. Anchor Realignment uses the same class text embeddings to fit a shared feature correction that separates predicted classes from alternatives. Both modules use the same unlabeled history while keeping all encoders frozen. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate strong recognition performance under both natural shifts and controlled corruptions.

Motivation: 理解手物交互中的接触和触觉强度是物理智能系统的重要基础，但触觉测量采集成本高、覆盖有限，限制了触觉表征学习。
