Abstract: Understanding contact and tactile magnitude in hand-object interaction is a key step toward physically intelligent systems, yet learning such representations is limited by the cost and restricted coverage of tactile measurements. We present Touch-JEPA, a framework for learning tactile representations from hand-object videos with limited tactile supervision. We first introduce a self-supervised pretraining objective that learns from video alone without tactile supervision. Using video-derived hand geometry, Touch-JEPA predicts masked visual features at corresponding hand-surface locations from spatial and temporal context, encouraging the representation to capture latent contact dynamics. We then extend this formulation to joint representation learning, where masked prediction is applied to all videos while tactile supervision is available only for a labeled subset, grounding the shared representation in measured contact and magnitude. With self-supervised pretraining alone, simple supervised readouts on frozen Touch-JEPA features outperform supervised baselines in contact discrimination and tactile magnitude estimation across three human-hand benchmarks, and transfer to robotic hands without robot tactile training data. Joint learning with limited tactile supervision further improves contact and magnitude estimation on the human-hand benchmarks. Together, these results show that hand-object video can provide scalable self-supervision for tactile representation learning, while limited tactile measurements provide complementary physical grounding.

[133] Latent Bridge: From Video Latents to 3D Geometry via Feature Alignment cs.CVPDF

Shuai Zhang, Zhanpeng Ouyang, Zhengqing Chen, Ruohong Mei, Shaoyu Chen

TL;DR: Latent Bridge 将 Wan2.1、V-RAE 视频潜空间映射到几何特征，直接从潜变量预测相机、深度和点云，省去 RGB 解码再编码；同一桥接模型可复用于图像到视频和文本到视频生成。

Details

Motivation: 视频生成与前馈 3D 重建通常使用不同表示。若从生成视频潜变量恢复几何，传统方法需先解码 RGB 再重新编码，增加计算并造成信息往返损失。

Result: Latent Bridge 将视频生成潜空间与几何特征空间连接，分别为 Wan2.1 和 V-RAE 训练表示专属桥接器，直接从潜变量前馈预测相机、深度和 3D 点云。桥接器以 RGB 视频进行特征蒸馏训练，视频编码器与几何模型保持冻结；训练不需要几何真值，也不执行下游几何聚合器或预测头。推理时同一潜变量既支持 RGB 解码，也支持几何预测；同一桥接器还可复用于同一潜空间的图像到视频和文本到视频生成，无需重训。在 Co3Dv2 和 WildRGB-D 上，方法达到有竞争力的几何重建质量，并实现高效端到端生成—重建和更快潜变量到几何解码。

Abstract: Video generation and feed-forward 3D reconstruction typically use separate representations, requiring RGB decoding and visual re-encoding to recover geometry from generated latents. We propose Latent Bridge, which connects video generative latent spaces with geometric feature spaces to directly predict 3D geometry from video latents. We develop representation-specific bridges for Wan2.1 and V-RAE, enabling feed-forward prediction of cameras, depth, and 3D point clouds without the intermediate RGB pathway. Each bridge is trained on RGB videos through feature distillation, while the video encoder and geometry model remain frozen. Training requires neither ground-truth geometry nor execution of the downstream geometric aggregator or prediction heads. At inference, the same video latents support both RGB decoding and geometry prediction. The same trained bridge can be reused for image-to-video and text-to-video generation within the same video latent space, without bridge retraining. Experiments on Co3Dv2 and WildRGB-D demonstrate competitive geometric reconstruction quality, efficient end-to-end generation and reconstruction, and faster latent-to-geometry decoding, highlighting latent alignment as a practical and reusable interface between video generation and 3D reconstruction. Our code is available at: https://github.com/hustvl/Latent-Bridge.

Abstract: Despite the success of video world models, extending them to multiple agents is difficult because it requires generating several agent-specific video streams that are consistent in time and between agents. To address this gap, we introduce OverSeer, a multi-agent world model that augments egocentric agent views with auxiliary camera views. We suggest that, by fixing the auxiliary views in space, they act as a proxy for a world-centric representation: Unlike the agents’ views, which can change entirely due to a simple change in agent position, changes in the auxiliary views directly map to localized changes in the world state. We show that these fixed views substantially help the model to maintain consistency. We also show that, due to their redundant and semi-static nature, the auxiliary views can be efficiently compressed with a delta-VAE that encodes only the changes relative to a static reference image, reducing the number of tokens required to process them. Experiments in Minecraft and Doom demonstrate improved world and agent consistency, with qualitative results on real-world multi-camera captures indicating that the approach is not tied to game engines. Our compression analysis further shows that delta-VAE substantially reduces the computational overhead of auxiliary-view generation. We conclude that these fixed views provide a simple and efficient way to improve multi-agent world models.

Abstract: ScreenCalib enables light detection and ranging (LiDAR)–camera extrinsic calibration using an ordinary programmable display, providing known visual markers and controlled geometry while reducing dedicated target preparation. Display geometry introduces association ambiguities, while indoor clutter complicates extraction. ScreenCalib addresses these challenges through tag-guided display extraction, geometric hypothesis generation and refinement, and surrounding scene edges for ambiguity resolution. The single-pose mode requires only one static placement. Motivation: 空间智能已有空间定位图像编辑等生成式评测，但根据部分多视角观察、遵循语言指令生成空间一致的新视角研究不足。模型需要同时处理相机位置/朝向变化，以及物体指代、空间关系和参考坐标系线索。

Result: MindView 覆盖三个室内数据集的 513 个场景、1,500 个样本，包含七项任务，分为几何主导（指定相机位置和朝向变化）和语义主导（定位被指物体并解释关系及参考系）两类。评测 11 种生成模型后发现仍有明显提升空间；成对上下文分析还显示所有模型在扩展上下文条件下总分下降。用训练集微调 BAGEL 后总分提高 24.3 点，七项任务均有提升，并在三个空间理解基准上也得分更高，未使用这些基准标注训练。针对扩展上下文，训练免依赖目标视角假设的空间证据选择器 MindCue，可逆转扩展上下文造成的总分下降并超过标准上下文基线。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence has recently been assessed through generative tasks such as spatially grounded image editing, yet language-guided generation of spatially consistent views from partial multi-view observations remains underexplored. We introduce MindView, a benchmark for generative multi-view spatial intelligence with 1,500 samples from 513 scenes across three indoor datasets. Its seven tasks form two families: geometry-dominant tasks specify changes in camera position and orientation, while semantics-dominant tasks require inferring target viewpoints by grounding referred objects and interpreting spatial relations and reference-frame cues. Evaluation of eleven generators reveals substantial room for improvement on MindView. A paired context analysis further shows that all eleven generators obtain lower overall scores under the expanded context setting. These findings motivate us to investigate whether task-specific supervision can improve target-view generation and whether models can use additional multi-view evidence effectively. We therefore fine-tune BAGEL on the MindView training dataset, improving its overall score by 24.3 points and yielding gains across all seven MindView tasks. Beyond these generation gains, the fine-tuned model also yields higher scores on three spatial-understanding benchmarks without training on their annotations, suggesting that learning to generate spatially grounded views may benefit spatial understanding. To improve evidence utilization under expanded context, we propose MindCue, a training-free spatial evidence selector guided by a target-view hypothesis. MindCue reverses the drop in overall score under expanded context and outperforms the standard-context baseline. Overall, MindView provides a concrete testbed for generative multi-view spatial intelligence.

Abstract: Understanding human needs and selecting responses require distinct judgments that aggregate accuracy can obscure. Drawing on Social Information Processing and the Perception-Action Cycle, this organization separates human understanding from response selection. Abstract: Game engines and coding agents can specify the layout, motion, and camera of a scene, yet turning such coarse structure into realistic video requires a renderer that follows the structure and completes the appearance it leaves open. We present SolarRenderer, a structure-conditioned video renderer that adapts a frozen MiniMax-H3 33B backbone with two lightweight LoRAs. Starting from a pretrained four-step LoRA, structure-guided distribution matching renders in four steps, 12.3 times faster than 50-step sampling, while preserving the learned control. Abstract: Video generation models can now depict remarkably realistic motion. [145] Omni-Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction via Evidence-Prior Rebalancing for Omni LLMs cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CV | cs.MMPDF

Zijian Liu, Chaoyou Fu, Yan Yang, Caifeng Shan, Ran He

TL;DR: Omni-Woodpecker 在多模态隐藏表示中分离视觉/音频依据与竞争先验，并在冲突条件下重新平衡证据；免训练地提升 Qwen2.5-Omni 与 VideoLLaMA2-AV 的幻觉基准表现。

Details

Motivation: 全模态大模型具备联合音视频推理能力，但跨模态幻觉仍突出。现有缓解方法主要修改输出，忽略融合表示中感知证据与模型先验纠缠这一成因。

Result: 免训练框架 Omni-Woodpecker（OWP）先从互补的模态特定视角识别跨模态冲突，再通过正交分解将隐藏状态中的落地证据与竞争先验分离，最后在解码前抑制误导先验、恢复被忽略证据并自适应重平衡融合表示。以 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 为骨干，在 CMM 和 AVHBench 分别提升 12.9 与 8.6 点，在四个通用全模态推理基准上也稳定提升 0.67–2.25 点。迁移到 VideoLLaMA2-AV 后，在相同两个幻觉基准上分别提升 4.6 和 3.9 点。

Insight: 跨模态幻觉不只来自最终文本，也可能源于内部融合时模型先验压过音视频依据。针对识别出的冲突在隐藏表示中重平衡证据，可免训练应用并能迁移到不同全模态架构。

Abstract: Recent Omni LLMs (OLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in joint audio-visual reasoning, yet cross-modal hallucinations remain a major challenge. Existing mitigation methods mainly operate at the output level, overlooking the entanglement of grounding evidence and learned priors within fused representations. To address this issue, we propose Omni-Woodpecker (OWP), a training-free framework that shifts hallucination mitigation from output-level manipulation to conflict-conditioned representation rebalancing. Specifically, OWP first identifies cross-modal conflicts from complementary modality-specific views. It then employs orthogonal decomposition to separate grounding evidence from competing priors within the hidden states. Finally, OWP adaptively rebalances fused representations by suppressing misleading priors and restoring ignored evidence before decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and strong cross-architecture generalization of OWP. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the backbone, OWP improves performance by 12.9 and 8.6 points on CMM and AVHBench, respectively, while consistently achieving gains of 0.67-2.25 points across four general OLLM reasoning benchmarks. Furthermore, OWP generalizes effectively to VideoLLaMA2-AV, improving performance by 4.6 and 3.9 points on the same benchmarks. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZiiJianLiu/Omni-Woodpecker

Abstract: A failed gaming trajectory reveals that an agent needs intervention, but not necessarily how its execution harness should change. Recovery knowledge supplies context-specific intervention hypotheses that guide active probing; probe outcomes reveal when these hypotheses apply and provide evidence for refining both recovery knowledge and later probe selection. Jiashuo Sun, Hao Zhang, Xueqiang Xu, Heng Wang, Yixuan Xie

TL;DR: Ask-to-Distill 将合成问题视作分布知识载体，在不需参考答案或逐题学生更新的情况下，优化问题以 elicitation 更有价值的教师监督，再用于标准在线策略蒸馏。

Details

Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏用少量训练问题也可能有效，但难以判断新生成的问题是否有助于知识蒸馏；更难在不知道正确答案的情况下自动生成更有价值的问题。

Result: Ask-to-Distill 以学生初始化的问题合成器生成问题，并用冻结师生模型计算问题价值的分布式代理目标，不需要参考答案、正确性奖励或对每个候选题更新学生。奖励结合师生分歧、教师在学生候选答案支持集上的概率质量，并控制问题长度和多样性。生成问题经筛选和去重后形成仅含提示的问题库，再用于标准 OPD。在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上，该方法较未优化的学生自生成提示及 KL 筛选真实问题提升平均表现与教师差距恢复，在所测设置中匹配或超过真实题库 OPD。分析还显示，优化问题可从更短学生回答中 elicitation 更密集且总量更多的监督信号。

Insight: 问题本身可以作为分布知识转移的载体：优质问题应暴露有价值的师生差异，而非仅追求题目正确性或难度。无答案分布代理让问题生成可优化，且短回答也能提供更密集的蒸馏监督。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) can succeed with surprisingly few training questions, motivating a view of questions as carriers of distributional knowledge transfer. How can we assess whether a newly generated question is valuable for distillation, and learn to generate better questions—without access to its correct answer? We introduce Ask-to-Distill (\method), a framework that treats synthetic questions as distribution elicitors and optimizes the teacher supervision they expose along student-generated trajectories. A student-initialized synthesizer is optimized using a distributional surrogate for question value, computed from a frozen student–teacher pair without reference answers, correctness rewards, or per-candidate student updates. The reward combines teacher–student disagreement with teacher probability mass on the student’s candidate support, together with length and diversity controls. The generated questions are filtered and deduplicated into a prompt-only bank for standard OPD. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks, our method improves average performance and teacher-gap recovery over unoptimized student-generated prompts and KL-filtered real questions, while matching or exceeding real-bank OPD in the evaluated settings. Signal analyses further show that optimized questions can elicit denser supervision and more total signal from shorter student responses, supporting elicited distributional supervision as an objective for synthetic distillation data. Our code, data and models are publicly available at https://github.com/GasolSun36/Ask_to_Distill.

[149] Give the Model a Finger: Developmentally Aligned Evaluation of Counting in Vision-Language Models cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CV | cs.LG | cs.ROPDF

Yingxin Jia, Hui Geng, Wenxin Yang, Mohan Zhang

TL;DR: 该研究用“逐个标记物体”的简易视觉工具审计 VLM 数数过程，发现精确计数范围因模型而异（R80 为 3–150 点）；标记主要带来可审计轨迹，而非稳定准确率提升。

Details

Motivation: VLM 在少量物体上通常计数准确，数量增加后容易转向估算，类似儿童掌握计数前的表现。儿童会通过指点、移动已数物体或分组等认知动作辅助计数；研究用不含视觉智能的简单图像工具提供同类操作，以测量模型如何数数。

Result: 在 9 个模型、682 张图像、7 种工具流程条件下，模型精确计数范围呈显著个体差异，R80 从 3 到 150 个点，跨度近两个数量级。作为干预，标记对九个模型中仅四个在小数量照片上有帮助，会伤害指点不精确的模型，且很少胜过口头流程或盲点列表。作为测量，标记可暴露串行过程：精确指针模型会依标签读出答案，指点精度关联受益程度，密集场景常因漏标失败，模型看见自己的标记后很少修正。模型可遵循指令指定的遍历顺序，却常在反转顺序后改变答案；类似儿童的规则排列优势只在该流程中出现，并非行列乘法捷径。

Insight: 图像标记工具的主要价值是把隐藏的逐项计数过程变成可审计轨迹，而非普遍提高答案准确率。逐点真值可区分命中、重复、虚构和遗漏，也揭示模型对顺序和自我修正的局限；因此评测要将过程能力与最终数值分开。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are exact on small sets and drift into estimation on larger ones, a profile reminiscent of children before they master counting. Children cross the barrier with epistemic actions: pointing at each item while reciting number words, moving counted items aside, partitioning the array. We give VLMs the same actions as dumb image tools with no visual intelligence of their own in a training-free agent loop. Our central claim is about measurement, not intervention: a model that counts by marking leaves a normally hidden serial procedure on the image, and the developmental principles of counting turn those marks into an interpretable audit of serial visual reasoning: every tag is classified against point-level ground truth as on-target, duplicate, phantom, or omitted, the answer is checked against the number of tags, and order irrelevance is tested by forcing opposite traversal orders. Across 9 models, 682 images, and 7 harness conditions we find that (i) the exact-counting range is a model-specific signature spanning nearly two orders of magnitude (R80 from 3 to 150 dots); (ii) as an intervention the finger mostly fails: tags help 4 of 9 models on small-count photographs, hurt imprecise pointers, and rarely beat a verbal routine or a blind point list; what the loop buys is an auditable trace, not accuracy; (iii) as measurement it succeeds: a tag-perturbation probe shows that precise pointers read their answers off the tags, pointing precision tracks which models benefit, dense scenes fail by omission, and models rarely revise after seeing their own tags; and (iv) the capacities dissociate: models follow an instructed traversal order yet often change their answer when it reverses, and children’s regular-over-scattered advantage appears only under the routine and survives lattices that defeat a rows-times-columns shortcut.

Abstract: Staged post-training of multimodal foundation models chains vision-language alignment, reasoning distillation, and domain tuning, yet how each phase trades capabilities against the others remains under-characterised. Details

Motivation: 模型压缩让大语言模型更易部署，但常规评估多只测任务性能保留率，无法指出分布在数十亿参数中的哪些能力仍在、哪些能力已悄然崩塌。

Result: ACE-Compression 是能力中心评估框架，测试四类部署相关能力：程序化推理与约束执行、知识落地与真实性、社会认知与情感理解、角色一致性与风格稳定。在六种开放权重 LLM 的 35 个以上压缩检查点上评估量化和剪枝后，发现压缩会重排能力画像：粗粒度、局部和表层信号往往保留，细粒度、长尾及依赖上下文的能力更脆弱。总体上量化比剪枝更能保留能力，但两者差距的实际意义取决于应用需要的具体能力。

Abstract: Model compression makes large language models deployable, but today’s evaluation tells an incomplete story. Standard practice measures retained task performance and stops there. This misses a critical fact: compression perturbs the distributed knowledge embedded across billions of weights, and task scores alone cannot reveal which underlying abilities survive and which quietly collapse. To solve this question, we introduce ACE-Compression, an ability-centric evaluation framework. Instead of asking only “does the compressed model still answer correctly?”, we ask “which of its core abilities remain intact?” Our framework probes four deployment-facing dimensions: procedural reasoning and constraint execution, knowledge grounding and truthfulness, social cognition and affective understanding, and persona fidelity and stylistic consistency. Across more than 35 compressed checkpoints from six open-weight LLMs-covering quantization and pruning-we find that compression redistributes ability profiles. Coarse, local, and surface-level signals often persist, while fine-grained, long-tail, and context-dependent abilities become fragile. Quantization consistently outperforms pruning in retention, but the practical meaning of that gap depends entirely on which ability an application requires. Based on our findings, deployers can choose compression methods by the ability profile their application needs, not just by a leaderboard average. And algorithm designers gain diagnostic signals to build compression methods that preserve what matters. We believe that ACE-Compression turns compression from a black-box accuracy drop into an interpretable intervention-one we can measure, understand, and ultimately improve.

Yet, existing remedies remain inadequate: environment resets are infeasible in proprietary settings, action replay is prohibitively expensive over long horizons, and API-based rollback is limited to web environments, leaving model-based recovery largely unexplored. To address this gap, we present ReTrace, a scalable evaluation framework for systematically studying recovery behavior in computer-use agents. We formalize Transition-Consistent Recovery: identical downstream actions from the recovered and target states must yield identical outcomes. We operationalize this criterion through goal-oriented action replay following controlled error injection. Using ReTrace, we present the first systematic study of autonomous, model-based recovery, revealing that (i) ideal recovery can unlock model’s latent task completion capabilities, (ii) current computer-use agents fail to exploit their latent recovery capabilities, and (iii) accurate model-based recovery yields measurable end-to-end gains. TL;DR: S-Agent 2.0 将通用代码智能体接入命令行 3D 感知工具，并围绕“获取证据—持久记忆—空间语言解释”闭合证据链；MMSI-Bench 和 ReVSI 上显著超过无工具版本及同骨干专用系统。

Motivation: 空间推理要判断物体三维位置、相互关系和运动。智能体式 VLM 可主动调用 3D 感知工具获取证据，但即使工具给出正确测量，模型仍可能无法将原始读数转成答案，工具与模型之间的证据解释环节存在缺口。

Result: S-Agent 2.0 给现成通用代码智能体配备命令行感知工具包，设计目标是让 VLM 完成证据闭环：收集场景几何证据、写入持久场景记忆，并将每条读数解释为普通空间语言；所有被使用的读数都锚定于工具几何结果。在 MMSI-Bench 和 ReVSI 上，工具让 Qwen3.8-27B 分别提升 13.9 和 20.5 点，让 Gemini-3.8-Flash 提升 11.2 和 9.6 点。Qwen3.8-27B 上得分 66.5/82.3，高于同骨干 SpatialClaw 的 62.6/74.5；Gemini 得分 74.1/90.5，高于其 SpatialClaw 对照 67.0/79.7。骨干越强，工具增益虽缩小，但在 ReVSI 上专用系统从 +13.2 降至 +2.7，S-Agent 仍为 +9.6。系统兼容 Codex、Claude Code 等通用代码智能体框架。

Insight: 调用几何工具本身不足以完成空间推理，智能体还需保存结果并用明确空间语言解释读数。将测量、记忆和解释串成证据闭环，可以在不同通用代码智能体中复用，并比只输出工具读数更有效。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning asks a model to work out where objects are in 3D space, how they relate to one another, and how they move. Agentic VLMs have recently brought large improvements on such tasks by letting the model actively gather evidence with 3D perception tools instead of answering in a single pass. Even so, on many questions the tools return the right evidence and the model still does not arrive at the correct answer: the gap between model and tools, where raw readings become usable conclusions, is left unfilled. We present S-Agent 2.0, a spatial reasoning agent that equips an off-the-shelf general coding agent with a command-line perception toolkit, and we design the interface around one principle: help the VLM close the evidence loop: acquire scene evidence, remember it in a persistent scene memory, and interpret each reading in plain spatial language. This design keeps the full flexibility of a coding agent, and every reading it acts on stays grounded in the tools’ geometry. On MMSI-Bench and ReVSI, the toolkit improves a strong open-source model, Qwen3.8-27B, by +13.9 and +20.5 points over the same agent without tools, and a strong closed-source model, Gemini-3.8-Flash, by +11.2 and +9.6 points. With the open backbone S-Agent 2.0 reaches 66.5 on MMSI-Bench and 82.3 on ReVSI, ahead of the specialized system SpatialClaw on the same backbone (62.6 and 74.5), and with Gemini-3.8-Flash it reaches 74.1 and 90.5 against its 67.0 and 79.7. Both tool gains shrink as the backbone grows more capable, but SpatialClaw’s shrinks far more on ReVSI, from +13.2 to +2.7, while ours stays at +9.6. S-Agent 2.0 also works across different general coding agent harnesses, such as Codex and Claude Code.

[155] Disclosure Begets Disclosure: A Self-Reinforcing Privacy Loop in LLM Agents with Persistent Memory cs.CR | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Masahiro Kaneko, Hiromi Arai, Timothy Baldwin

TL;DR: 该研究发现持久记忆会形成“披露—记忆—再披露”隐私循环：模型把旧属性作为预设带入回复，用户确认或未纠正后又成为记忆；约束智能体回复可降低泄漏而保留多数质量收益。

Details

Motivation: LLM 智能体用持久记忆个性化回复，但记忆中的个人属性若被暴露会带来隐私风险。研究考察对话中调用记忆如何改变用户之后的表达，进而改变记忆揭示的信息。

Result: 在多轮会话模拟中，含 24 项个人属性的模拟用户与是否调用记忆的支持型智能体交谈；四种支持模型均显示记忆提高回复质量，也提高从完整记录正确推断属性值的实证泄漏率，且差距随会话增多而扩大。循环来自普通非对抗式协助：记忆条件回复把旧值带入后续内容，常以预设方式表达，用户确认或未纠正，于是线索和智能体猜测成为记录值。移除智能体提及某属性，后续披露下降幅度约为单轮效应预测的两倍。用户难以自行打破循环：纠正会暴露属性，拒谈会让预测转向敏感值，错误值未纠正则固化。最有效的缓解点在智能体回复：要求不陈述或预设属性，可把泄漏降至无记忆水平附近，同时保留多数质量收益；过滤或匿名化用户表达/存储内容则会损失较多收益。

Insight: 持久记忆带来的泄漏不是单次读取风险，而是智能体措辞影响用户后续表达、再反哺记忆的自我强化过程。约束智能体在回复中不主动陈述或假设属性，比事后过滤用户内容更能削弱循环并保留个性化效果。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use persistent memory to personalize responses, and personal attributes in this memory pose a privacy risk if exposed. We ask how consulting memory during dialogue changes what users go on to say, and thus what the memory comes to reveal. In multi-session simulations, a simulated user with 24 personal attributes talks with a support agent that does or does not consult memory, and in both settings attributes are inferred from the full accumulated record. Across four support models, memory raises response quality but also empirical leakage, the share of attribute values correctly inferred from the record, by a margin that widens over sessions. The cause is a loop in ordinary, non-adversarial assistance. Memory-conditioned responses carry earlier values forward, often as presuppositions, and users affirm them or leave them uncorrected, so that cues and the agent’s own guesses become recorded values. The loop reinforces itself, and removing the agent’s mentions of an attribute lowers its later disclosure about twice as much as single-turn effects predict. Users cannot leave it on their own, since correcting a value discloses the attribute, declining to discuss it shifts later predictions toward sensitive values, and incorrect values left in place become entrenched. The loop is best weakened at the agent’s response. Instructing the agent not to state or presuppose attributes brings leakage to about the no-memory level while keeping most of the quality gains, whereas filtering or anonymizing what users say or what is stored gives up much of them.

Details

Motivation: 真实 3D 场景布置需要满足支撑和包含等细粒度位置约束。依赖有向包围盒等简化物体表示的方法看不到详细几何，因此难以处理精细摆放关系。

Result: GALA 是具几何感知能力的语言模型，显式输入点云，在场景上下文和摆放指令条件下逐个自回归预测目标物体的六自由度位姿。训练分两阶段：先通过预训练任务对齐几何和文本，再针对物体布置微调。作者还整理覆盖多种房间类型和细粒度家具布局的多源场景数据集。基准实验显示 GALA 显著优于没有显式几何推理的基线；进一步构建计划—摆放—验证多阶段智能体流水线，用于文本驱动场景生成。

Insight: 支撑和包含关系依赖实际物体形状，包围盒无法表达的几何细节需要由点云显式建模。逐个预测位姿并在场景中迭代验证，可把几何语言模型接入更完整的文本生成 3D 布局流程。

Abstract: Reasoning about fine-grained placement constraints, such as support and containment, is essential for realistic 3D scene creation. It remains a significant challenge for existing methods that rely on simplified object representations like oriented bounding box (OBB), which are blind to the detailed geometry. To address this limitation, we introduce GALA, a geometry-aware language model with explicit point cloud perception for controllable object placement. Our model performs autoregressively by predicting the 6D pose of one target object at a time conditioned on the scene context and placement instruction. We utilize a two-stage training strategy: the first stage aligns geometric and textual modalities through pretraining tasks, and the second stage fine-tunes the model for placement. To facilitate this task, we also curate a multi-source scene dataset covering diverse room types and fine-grained furniture arrangements, which will be open-sourced for community benefit. Experiments on our benchmark show our model significantly outperforms baselines without explicit geometric reasoning. We further develop a multi-stage plan-place-verify agent pipeline that uses the model for text-driven scene generation. Project Page: https://xccelephant.github.io/paper/gala/

[157] Self-Evolving Agents through Privileged Retrospective Distillation cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Yuhang Zhou, Yifan Wu, Bo Peng, Mingyi Wang, Jiayi Liu

TL;DR: RSD 将智能体自身历史轨迹与跨任务先例、反思及事后信息转为特权监督，再在线策略蒸馏回策略；ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 SearchQA 上相对基础模型最高提升 59.7%。

Details

Motivation: 自我进化智能体希望从部署经验中持续提升，但自然收集的轨迹常没有真值答案或验证器奖励，难以直接形成密集训练监督。

Result: 回顾式自蒸馏（RSD）在每条在线策略轨迹上让同一模型分别充当学生和教师：教师在利用跨任务先例、经验教训、逐轮反思以及完成轨迹后的未来回看信息时，重新评分学生已采取动作；再将教师分布在线策略蒸馏回策略。多轮过程中经验库增长，回顾上下文持续刷新，使模型、数据和监督共同演化。作者还提出前沿模型引导的上下文—策略进化，由更强模型对特权上下文构造方式提出受约束修改。在 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 的 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld、SearchQA 评估中，RSD 相对基础模型最高提升 59.7%；上下文策略进化较固定策略再相对提升最高 44.7%。

Insight: 没有外部真值轨迹或验证奖励时，已完成轨迹中的事后信息和跨任务经验仍可提供教师监督。回顾上下文能随经验库持续更新；前沿模型进一步优化上下文构造，但其增益是在所测任务和模型范围内。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents aim to improve from their own experience rather than remain fixed after deployment, but naturally collected trajectories often lack ground-truth solutions or verifier-derived rewards. We introduce Retrospective Self-Distillation (RSD), a multi-round framework that turns such experience into dense supervision using privileged retrospective context. For each on-policy trajectory, the same underlying model acts as both student and teacher: the teacher re-scores the student’s realized actions while conditioning on cross-task precedents and lessons, together with turn-level reflection and future hindsight from the completed trajectory. The resulting teacher distribution is distilled back into the policy through on-policy self-distillation. Across rounds, the experience bank grows and the retrospective context is refreshed, allowing the model, its data, and its supervision to co-evolve. We further introduce frontier-guided context-policy evolution, where a stronger frontier model proposes constrained edits to how privileged context is constructed. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and SearchQA with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, RSD improves performance by up to 59.7% relative to the base model, while frontier-guided context-policy evolution adds up to a further 44.7% relative improvement over a fixed context policy. These results show that naturally accumulated experience can be converted into persistent model capability without ground-truth trajectories or verifier-derived rewards for the core policy update.

[158] Pairwise Learning Increases Data Efficiency in Deep Neural Networks cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Robert Bain, Marjan Rashidi, Elizabeth Chrastil

TL;DR: 该成对学习框架把回归改写为相对排序聚合，以少量标签构造大量比较样本；UTKFace 上从头训练的 ResNet10 年龄 MAE 为 3.64 年，STL10 小样本分类较十分类交叉熵最高提升 5.2%。

Details

Motivation: 深度网络在小样本回归中常输出低方差预测，难以捕捉个体差异。研究希望通过相对比较而非单样本绝对标签，提升不同架构和数据规模下的数据效率。

Result: 方法将回归重构为聚合相对排序的成对比较学习。在 23,000 张图像的 UTKFace 上，可由现有标签生成超过十亿条训练比较，无需新增监督；从头训练且仅用家用硬件的轻量 ResNet10 达到 3.64 年 MAE，超过包括大规模预训练在内的先前模型。对类别预测，在 STL10 仅有 500–2,000 个标注样本时，成对监督持续优于同配置的十分类交叉熵，准确率最高提高 5.2 个百分点。

Insight: 少量绝对标签可派生大量相对顺序约束，为模型提供比逐例拟合更丰富的监督，尤其有助于保留个体间差异。此策略可用于回归和分类的小样本设置，临床应用潜力仍需在具体临床数据上验证。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often fail at small-sample regression, collapsing to low-variance predictions that miss individual differences. Here, we introduce a pairwise comparison framework that reframes regression as aggregated relative rankings. This method improves data efficiency across model architectures and dataset sizes. On the UTKFace dataset (23k images), pairwise learning generates over one billion training comparisons without additional supervision. The paired ranking allows a lightweight ResNet10 trained from scratch on home hardware to achieve a 3.64-year MAE-surpassing previous models, including those with large-scale pretraining. For classification into categories, pairwise supervision consistently outperforms an otherwise identical 10-way crossentropy model on STL10 with 500-2000 labeled examples, improving accuracy by up to 5.2%. Overall, pairwise supervision enhances data efficiency and enables strong regression and classification performance when labeled data are limited, particularly in clinical settings.

[159] From RL Papers to Framework Code: Inclusion and Review in Seven Open-Source Training Frameworks cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.SEPDF

Taiheng Pan

TL;DR: 该研究追踪七个开源 RL 训练框架对 2025–2026 年通用 RL 方法论文的纳入情况：1,598 篇中仅 37 篇进入至少一个框架，180 天内新增纳入数未随论文量增长。

Details

Motivation: 研究者通常通过训练框架使用语言模型强化学习方法；一个方法被框架纳入后，用户只需调整配置即可采用。研究因此追踪方法从论文提出到进入开源框架的实际传播和评审过程。

Result: 作者分析七个开源 RL 后训练框架的提交历史，并与 2025 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月筛选出的 6,939 篇 arXiv 论文关联。其中 1,598 篇提出通用 RL 训练方法，只有 37 篇进入至少一个框架。五个季度队列中，论文数从 72 增至 386，但 180 天内进入框架的方法仍维持每季 5–7 篇。已纳入论文更常训练 30B 及以上策略（41% 对 10%），也更常只改已有损失中的系数、裁剪、掩码或权重（29% 对 9%）；排除框架维护公司论文后差异仍在，代码发布率则相近。公开 issue/PR 很少拒绝方法，超过九成未纳入通用方法没有框架讨论记录，许多已有讨论也长期无回复。框架 issue 与 13 个大型开放模型技术报告还显示，用户少公开报告训练失败，技术报告列出 13 种常纳入方法中的 8 种。

Insight: 方法进入框架的瓶颈更像是缺乏公开讨论和维护响应，而非公开拒绝；论文数量增加也未带来纳入数量增长。框架实现、失败报告和模型技术报告的链接追踪，可帮助研究者理解新方法的实际采用路径和复现情况。

Abstract: Practitioners use reinforcement learning methods for language models through training frameworks, so a method that a framework includes is one configuration change away for its users. We measure how often this happens. We trace seven open-source frameworks for RL post-training through their commit histories and link them to 6,939 screened arXiv papers from January 2025 to June 2026. Of 1,598 papers that propose a general RL training method, 37 reached at least one framework. Across five quarterly cohorts of papers, the number included within 180 days stayed between 5 and 7 while the cohorts grew from 72 to 386 papers. Compared with papers from the same quarters, included papers more often trained policies of 30B parameters or more, at 41 against 10 percent, and more often changed a coefficient, clip, mask or weight in the existing loss, at 29 against 9 percent. Both gaps hold without papers from the companies that maintain the frameworks, while code release was equally common in both groups. The frameworks’ issues and pull requests show that few methods are turned away in public. More than nine in ten of the general methods no framework included have no thread there that cites or names them, and many proposals that do appear stay open without a reply. Two further records, the frameworks’ issues and the technical reports of 13 large open-weight models, show that users rarely report training failures in public and that the reports state 8 of the 13 widely included methods. We release the data as a tracker that links training failures to framework implementations and lists each framework’s contribution rules and earlier proposals.

[160] LocaLeJEPA: Maximum Entropy Representations under Geometric Priors cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Kartik Tandon, Alessio Borgi, Pavel Snopov, Inés García-Redondo, Davide D’Ascenzo

TL;DR: LocaLeJEPA 将 JEPA 的熵最大化和分布匹配推广到局部欧几里得几何，提出 LocaLeReg 正则器；在双曲 ViT、等变 ResNet 及普通欧氏 ViT 上均优于 LeJEPA 的多种分类和下游任务表现。

Details

Motivation: JEPA 是有吸引力的自监督学习范式，但容易发生表示坍塌。LeJEPA 通过将嵌入匹配到各向同性高斯解决该问题，却依赖欧几里得几何，无法适配等变 CNN 或双曲 ViT 等具有非欧几里得内在结构的模型。

Result: LocaLeJEPA 从流形理论角度重构 LeJEPA 的熵最大化性质，提出局部欧几里得分布匹配正则器 LocaLeReg，以稳定且可扩展的方式实现框架。在 ImageNet-1K 上训练视觉模型的广泛实验显示，搭配双曲 ViT-S/8、等变 ResNet-50，以及普通欧氏 ViT-S/8、ViT-L/14 时，LocaLeJEPA 均显著优于 LeJEPA。优势同时体现在 ImageNet 线性分类、k-NN 分类和多个下游任务。

Insight: 自监督表示正则化应尊重数据和骨干网络的内在几何，而不应强制所有嵌入都服从全局欧氏高斯假设。局部欧几里得匹配既支持特殊几何，也能改善普通欧氏模型表现，提供更通用的 JEPA 设计。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer an appealing approach to self-supervised learning, but are typically prone to representation collapse. LeJEPA resolves this by introducing a theoretically grounded distribution-matching objective that drives the embedding towards an isotropic Gaussian. However, this approach is fundamentally tied to Euclidean assumptions, and its global target does not reflect domain-specific geometric priors, such as the use of non-Euclidean embedding geometries, e.g., equivariant CNNs or hyperbolic ViTs. We thus introduce LocaLeJEPA, a framework motivated by a manifold-theoretic reformulation of LeJEPA’s entropy-maximization property. The accompanying LocaLeReg, a novel locally Euclidean distribution-matching regularizer, enables a stable and scalable implementation of the LocaLeJEPA framework. We then perform extensive experiments in the vision setting on models trained on ImageNet-1K. LocaLeJEPA significantly outperforms LeJEPA when paired with hyperbolic ViT-S/8 and equivariant ResNet-50 backbones, and also with ordinary Euclidean ViT-S/8 and ViT-L/14 backbones. This holds both in terms of linear and k-NN classification performance on ImageNet and on a series of downstream tasks tested. We view the LocaLeJEPA formalism as a principled approach to the JEPA paradigm that better respects intrinsic data geometry while also accommodating generalizations to exotic embedding geometries. Code available at https://github.com/alessioborgi/LocaLeJEPA.

[161] Present but Hard to Read: Probing Latent Reasoning with Minimal Pairs cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Aur Shalev Merin

TL;DR: 该研究用最小提示对测试模型内部是否包含中间计算值，发现单提示探针可能读不到实际存在的数值；两步任务中的 a+b 信号虽可读，但跨问题移植不改变最终答案，说明可读不等于被使用。

Details

Motivation: 潜在推理探针常在答案开始生成处读取模型状态，并把探针失败当作某个值不存在。研究质疑该推断，测试内部计算值是否可能存在但难以从单个提示的隐藏状态读出。

Result: 作者在循环模型 Ouro-1.4B 和标准 Transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B 上，用仅在问题某个数字上不同的最小提示对探测该数字。对照显示该值在最后提示 token 已存在，并以跨问题共享代码表征，读出率约 0.9；常见的一次只看一个提示的探针却几乎读不到，Ouro 为 0.36、Qwen 为 0.19，即使训练 5,249 道题后亦然。该数字变化幅度小而非被杂讯淹没；替换成随机词造成状态移动更大、更易读。作者提供包括通用混合合成零假设的协议，用来区分计算值与输入复制/混合。在预注册两步问题中，Ouro 写出答案末位前可检测到未写出的 a+b 小信号且符号随运算变化，但相当部分也可由混合解释。将其他问题的该中间值从第一轮起移植，不会把最终数字推向供体答案；移植答案本身则会，表明该值可读但未被使用。

Insight: 线性探针失败不等于模型状态中没有某个值，而探针成功也可能只是读到输入混合的痕迹。要证明模型实际使用中间表示，还需通过最小对照和因果移植检验；本研究发现“可读”与“参与最终计算”可以分离。

Abstract: Probing studies of latent reasoning often read a model’s state where the answer begins and take a failed probe as evidence that a value is absent. We test this with a digit copied from the question, in the looped model Ouro-1.4B and the standard transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B. Comparing prompts that differ only in that digit shows it is present at the last prompt token, in a code shared across questions (readout about 0.9). A probe that sees one prompt at a time, the usual setting, barely reads it: 0.36 in Ouro and 0.19 in Qwen even after 5,249 training questions. The digit’s change is small rather than crowded: random noise lands on equally busy directions, while a random word in its place moves the state far more and is readable. Since every varied token leaves a trace, a successful probe is ambiguous too. We give a protocol, including a synthetic null for generic mixing, for testing computed values against copies and mixtures of the inputs. In pre-registered studies of two-step problems it finds a small signal for the unwritten intermediate a+b just before Ouro writes the answer’s last digit, and its sign follows the operation, though mixing alone produces much of that sign-following part. Holding that value at another problem’s from the first loop on does not move the written digit toward the other problem’s, while the same patch of the answer does: the value is readable but not used.

[162] Binary Discrete Diffusion: Candidate-Set Diffusion for Logic and Planning Tasks cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CL | stat.MLPDF

Rodrigo Casado Noguerales, Dimitri von Rütte, Bernhard Schölkopf, Thomas Hofmann, Aran Raoufi

TL;DR: Binary Discrete Diffusion 让每个位置的噪声状态表示一组候选 token，以候选增删逐步表达不确定性；在数独、Boxoban 和图着色难例上超过匹配的掩码/均匀扩散基线，并在语言建模上保持竞争力。

Details

Motivation: 离散扩散通过迭代去噪生成文本，具备任意顺序生成、自纠和填充潜力，但掩码或均匀扩散一次就抹去 token 身份，无法显式表示多个候选仍可能成立的部分不确定状态。

Result: Binary Discrete Diffusion 提出一类连续时间离散扩散过程，每个位置的噪声状态是候选 token 集合，用二进制成员向量表示；扩散通过增加和删除候选改变集合，使信息逐渐丢失或恢复，状态本身保留部分不确定性。在 Sudoku-Extreme、Boxoban 和图着色上，二元扩散的对称形式在匹配架构、控制训练、采样和调参预算下，持续超过调优充分的掩码及均匀扩散基线，收益在最难实例上最大。其在 Text8、TinyStories、OpenWebText 等语言建模任务上也保持竞争力。

Insight: 反向模型学到的计算方式会受前向噪声核影响；把不确定性表示为候选集合，可让模型在迭代中逐步增加或移除可能答案，而非一次抹去信息。这种中间表示对组合推理任务尤其有用。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models iteratively denoise a noisy sequence of categorical data such as text, enabling any-order generation with self-correction and in-filling capabilities. This makes them promising candidates for reasoning, planning, and constrained-generation tasks like coding and math. Yet masked and uniform diffusion erase a token’s identity in a single corruption event, so their noisy states cannot explicitly represent partially resolved uncertainty in which several alternatives remain plausible. We close this gap with Binary Discrete Diffusion, a family of continuous-time discrete-diffusion processes whose noisy state is a set of candidate tokens per position, represented as a binary membership vector. Binary diffusion changes candidate sets through additions and removals, allowing information to be lost and recovered gradually while the diffusion state itself represents partial uncertainty. On Sudoku-Extreme, Boxoban, and graph coloring, the symmetric formulation of binary diffusion consistently outperforms strong, well-tuned masked and uniform baselines under matched architectures and controlled training, sampling, and tuning budgets, with its largest gains on the hardest instances, while remaining competitive on language modeling (Text8, TinyStories, OpenWebText). These results support candidate sets as an effective intermediate representation for iterative reasoning and show that the corruption kernel can materially change the computation learned by the reverse model.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions, but effective manipulation also requires anticipating how the environment will change. Existing world-action models explore future image prediction and latent dynamics modeling. However, generating complete future images can be computationally expensive, while highly compressed latent representations may obscure local changes. To address these challenges, we propose PLaCA, a world-to-action framework built around future latent change. PLaCA directly predicts spatially structured patch-level future latent change from the current multimodal input, preserving local information about expected environmental changes. We introduce patch-level change-aware supervision to emphasize regions undergoing larger changes and a future-to-action interface that explicitly incorporates predicted future latent change into action generation. Experiments on LIBERO-Long show that PLaCA achieves a 79.0% success rate, improving upon the Action-only baseline by 5.0 percentage points. On CALVIN, PLaCA improves the average sequence length from 2.784 to 3.564. [164] CovNav: Conformal Prediction-Guided Adaptive Inference for Object Goal Navigation cs.RO | cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Pengying Wu, Zhejia Wen, Kang Ding, Kangjie Zhou, Chunxuan Jiao

TL;DR: CovNav 用保形预测校准快速本地 LLM 的物体共现判断，只把含糊查询升级到慢速思维链；HM3D 成功率较最强零样本基线提高 0.8–1.7 点，慢推理仅用于 12.4% 查询。

Result: CovNav 是免训练框架，用保形预测（CP）把 System 1 快速判断与 System 2 慢速推理变成不确定性决策。快速本地 LLM 对目标是否靠近某个观测物体作结构化二元判断，CP 将 token 概率转成预测集合，只对含糊查询升级到慢速思维链。物体对表示使校准可处理，并借助现成检测器支持开放集物体。在 Gibson、HM3D 上评估，HM3D v1/v2 成功率较最强零样本基线提高 0.8/1.7 个百分点；慢推理仅用于 12.4% 查询，同时保留始终思考模式的大部分增益且延迟更低。TurtleBot 4 实机实验也验证该方法。

Insight: 慢速推理不必对每个空间判断都运行；经校准的快速模型可先筛出真正含糊的问题，再升级推理。保形预测为这种快慢切换提供了不确定性依据，使机器人在效率、隐私和判断质量间取得折中。

Abstract: In object goal navigation (ObjectNav), an embodied agent must find a target object (e.g., “find a bed”) in a previously unseen environment, relying on spatial commonsense such as object co-occurrence. Large Language Models (LLMs) encode this commonsense, but deploying them on robots exposes a trade-off between reasoning quality and efficiency: cloud-based models incur latency and privacy risks, whereas small on-device LLMs are fast but prone to hallucinated spatial judgments. Hybrid reasoning LLMs offer both a fast non-thinking mode and a slow thinking mode, which raises a key question for embodied agents: when is slow thinking worth its cost? We propose CovNav, a training-free framework that answers this question with Conformal Prediction (CP), turning dual-process (System 1 / System 2) inference into an uncertainty-aware decision. Much like recent decision models such as Jev, CovNav casts each spatial query as a structured binary decision rather than free-form generation: a local LLM in fast mode judges whether the target is likely near an observed object, CP converts its token probabilities into a prediction set, and only ambiguous queries are escalated to slow chain-of-thought reasoning. The object-pair formulation keeps calibration tractable and supports open-set objects through off-the-shelf detectors. Experiments on Gibson and HM3D show that CovNav surpasses the strongest zero-shot baseline by 0.8 and 1.7 percentage points in success rate on HM3D v1 and v2, while invoking slow reasoning for only 12.4% of queries and retaining most of the gain of always-on thinking at a fraction of its latency. Real-robot experiments on a TurtleBot 4 further validate the approach. CovNav shows how calibrated uncertainty can decide when an embodied agent should think slowly, offering a principled form of adaptive test-time reasoning for LLM-based robots.

Abstract: Reasoning about safety is essential for embodied agents powered by large language models (LLMs). We introduce the Contextual Action and Plan Safety benchmark (CAPSafe), designed to evaluate safety reasoning at two levels: (1) an action-level dataset that tests whether a single action is safe given its environmental context, and (2) a plan-level dataset that evaluates whether agents can generate safe long-horizon plans for complex hazardous household tasks. CAPSafe models safety conditions where hazards are situational (inferred from object properties), multiple hazards can co-occur within the same environment, and each is annotated with a relative severity level for fine-grained analysis. Evaluating nine closed and open-source LLMs reveals a substantial gap between hazard recognition and safe long-horizon planning. Several models achieve high accuracy in recognizing unsafe actions (exceeding 90% in the best cases), yet safe long-horizon planning remains substantially harder. These results highlight that robust safety reasoning over long action sequences remains an open challenge.

Abstract: Humanoid-human interaction requires robots to operate under severe partial observability: during close physical contact, the human partner is occluded, truncated, or outside the robot’s field of view, causing pose estimation to fail and often necessitating external tracking or privileged human state. Abstract: Motivated by the human ability to integrate experience across language, perception, and action, we introduce UniWALM, a unified world-action-language model that learns video, action, and language in a single shared representation, where each modality is both a conditioning signal and a supervision target. Details

Motivation: 多指灵巧手可支持功能抓握、手内重定向和动态交接，但现有学习方案存在折中：目标到达强化学习可适应任意物体姿态，却缺少人类先验，容易学到不自然、不可靠的手指策略；动作跟踪能复现单段人类动作，却难以偏离参考轨迹。

Result: AdaDex 是目标条件强化学习框架，可将来自动作捕捉或野外 RGB(D) 手物交互重建的单段数秒轨迹转为可适应的灵巧技能。混合灵巧模仿在大规模并行环境中训练共享策略：动作跟踪环境用稠密跟踪目标注入人类动作先验；泛化环境扰动初始状态和目标，并仅用稀疏物体成功奖励，迫使技能在远离参考轨迹时仍可工作。由每项技能仅数秒人类动作，模型可学习功能抓握、物体重摆、带手指步态的手内旋转及抛接等动态行为。方法在泛化与功能性上超过基线，并展示跨模拟器和真实世界迁移。

Insight: 把稠密的人类动作跟踪与稀疏目标成功训练放进共享策略，可同时保留自然动作先验并鼓励偏离参考完成新目标。短时人类示范因此能被扩展为适应不同物体姿态和任务结果的灵巧技能。

Abstract: Dexterous multi-finger hands hold promise to unlock diverse skills like functional grasps, in-hand reorientation, and dynamic handover that the human-designed world assumes, yet existing learning recipes force a trade-off. Goal-reaching reinforcement learning generalizes to arbitrary object poses but, lacking any human prior, converges to unnatural and unreliable finger strategies; motion tracking reproduces human-like behavior from a single clip but cannot deviate from it. We present AdaDex, a goal-conditioned RL framework that turns a single hand-object trajectory of a few seconds—from mocap data or HOI reconstruction from in-the-wild RGB(D) video—into an adaptable dexterous skill. At the core of AdaDex is Hybrid Dexterous Mimicking: a shared policy is trained on two populations of massively parallel environments: motion tracking environments trained with dense motion tracking objective, which instill the human prior from human motion; and generalization environments with noised initial states and goals and a sparse object-success reward, which force the skill to work far off the reference. From seconds of human motion per skill, the resulting policies can learn diverse adaptable dexterous skills, such as functional grasping, object reposing, in-hand reorientation with finger gaiting, and dynamic behaviors like throw-and-catch. Our method outperforms baselines in terms of generalization and functionality, and we demonstrate transfer across simulators and to the real world. Project page: https://yuxuank.com/AdaDex/.

cs.SI [Back]

[170] Do LLMs Really Lack Difference Awareness? Uncovering Heuristic Substitution in Group-Differentiating Fairness cs.SI | cs.CLPDF

Jingshen Zhang, Bo Wang, Yanlin Fu, Yuewen Dang, Dongming Zhao

TL;DR: TDD 将公平推理拆为先识别与情境相关的群体差异、再据此决策；四个基准和十种模型上超过单阶段基线，并以机制分析支持模型可能用形式平等启发式替代复杂差异推理。

Details

Motivation: 群体差异意识指模型能否在公平决策中识别并承认社会群体间有意义的差异。既有研究发现模型在此类任务表现不佳，通常解释为缺乏差异意识；作者提出另一种可能：模型可能用更简单、易调用的形式平等启发式替代“识别差异并判断其公平含义”的复杂任务。

Result: 两阶段区分—决策框架（TDD）先辨别情境相关群体差异，再作出相应决定，以减少对一视同仁捷径的依赖。在四个基准、参数规模从十亿到万亿级的十种模型上，TDD 显著优于单阶段基线，甚至优于加入模拟测试集泄漏强化的基线。与神经元级对比探测结合后，机制分析发现与形式平等启发式一致的两个迹象：紧凑、局部协调的表示结构，以及默认推理中的显著激活偏置。阶段分解还揭示启发式挤占和阶段动态不匹配等先前被忽略的错误模式。

Insight: 低差异意识表现未必意味着模型完全没有识别差异的能力，也可能是复杂的识别—决策过程被简单形式平等规则取代。分阶段显式处理可以改善结果并暴露错误发生位置；机制证据与该假说相符，但不等同于证明所有公平错误都由该机制造成。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the issue of difference awareness in LLMs, defined as a model’s ability to recognize and acknowledge meaningful differences between social groups in fairness decision-making. Previous studies have consistently shown that models perform poorly on such tasks, a finding commonly attributed to a lack of difference awareness. We propose an alternative account, which we term Fairness Heuristic Substitution: difference awareness is not necessarily absent, but the complex demand of simultaneously identifying relevant group differences and determining their implications for fairness decisions may lead models to substitute the simpler, more accessible equal-treatment heuristic, i.e., formal equality. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce TDD, a Two-stage Distinguish-Decide framework that explicitly decomposes fairness reasoning into first distinguishing contextually relevant group differences and then making the corresponding decision, thereby discouraging reliance on formal-equality shortcuts. (i) Across four benchmarks and ten models ranging from billions to trillions of parameters, TDD significantly outperforms one-stage baselines, even one strengthened through simulated test-set leakage. (ii) Combining TDD with neuron-level contrastive probing provides mechanistic evidence consistent with the Fairness Heuristic Substitution hypothesis by revealing two signatures of the formal-equality: a compact, locally coordinated representational structure and a pronounced activation bias under default reasoning. (iii) TDD’s stage-wise decomposition uncovers overlooked failure modes in fairness reasoning, including heuristic displacement and misaligned stage dynamics.

eess.IV [Back]

[171] Codec-Compatible Learned Compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Feiyang Yu, Qi Yang, Wenjie Huang, Yunfeng Guan, Yiling Xu

TL;DR: CCT-GS 为 3D Gaussian Splatting 的高维非直流球谐系数学习跨场景紧凑变换，并用轻量 LoRA 恢复场景细节；在 MPEG GSC 序列上较视频编码基线平均 BD-rate 降低 51.30%。

Details

Motivation: 视频式 3DGS 压缩把高斯属性表示成 2D 图并用标准视频编码器压缩。其中高维非直流球谐（SH-AC）图通道最多、占码流大部分，但统计分布不同于自然图像，系数之间的非线性依赖也尚未充分利用。

Result: CCT-GS 是面向编解码器兼容的学习式变换框架。它先学习通用 SH-AC 变换，捕捉跨场景规律，将 SH-AC 图压缩到适合传统编解码器的紧凑瓶颈图；再以基于 LoRA 的场景适配和轻量侧信息恢复场景特定外观细节。在 MPEG GSC 序列上的实验显示，CCT-GS 持续优于代表性视频式压缩基线，较视频式锚点的平均 BD-rate 降低 51.30%。

Insight: 把跨场景可共享的球谐相关性先转成编解码器友好的低维表示，再用小型场景适配补细节，可兼顾压缩效率与个性化外观。收益主要来自原始 SH-AC 高通道数据的统计结构，不必替换标准视频编码器。

Abstract: Video-based 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) compression methods provide a practical route by representing Gaussian attributes as 2D maps and coding them with standard video codecs. Among these maps, high-dimensional non-DC spherical harmonics (SH-AC) maps contain the largest number of channels and account for a dominant portion of the bitstream. However, their statistics differ substantially from those of natural images, and the nonlinear dependencies among SH-AC coefficients remain insufficiently exploited. To address these issues, we propose CCT-GS, a Codec-Compatible learned Transform framework for 3DGS compression. CCT-GS first learns a generalized SH-AC transform that captures cross-scene regularities and converts SH-AC maps into compact codec-friendly bottleneck maps. It further introduces LoRA-based scene adaptation to recover scene-specific appearance details with lightweight side information. Experiments on MPEG GSC sequences demonstrate that CCT-GS consistently outperforms representative video-based baselines, achieving an average BD-rate reduction of 51.30% over the video-based anchor.

[172] CAPS: Clinically-Verifiable Anatomy-Prioritized Synthesis for Missing MRI Imputation in Brain Tumor Segmentation eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Cecilia Diana-Albelda, Arthur Longuefosse, Mario Rubén Paz Campos, Álvaro García-Martín, Jesus Bescos

TL;DR: CAPS 以解剖优先的多模态生成补全缺失脑肿瘤 MRI 模态，并用强度、解剖特征和双向 Dice 目标约束拓扑边界；BraTS 上分割平均 Dice 82.38%，且报告跨脑膜瘤、儿科和撒哈拉以南队列零样本泛化。

Details

Motivation: 多参数 MRI 是神经肿瘤学标准检查，但现实临床难以总是完成四模态采集。缺失模态系统常面临折中：稳健分割器绕过图像生成而难解释，常规合成模型又容易模糊，难以提供可信诊断证据。

Result: CAPS（临床可验证、解剖优先合成）采用多模态生成架构及混合目标 L1 + L_AFP + L_DualDice，显式保持深层解剖特征和严格拓扑边界，而非仅优化像素差。在 BraTS 成人胶质瘤数据集上，合成保真度的强度指标优于基线，下游分割平均 Dice 达 82.38%，恢复可检查的视觉上下文。方法还在未见的脑膜瘤、儿科及撒哈拉以南非洲队列中无需领域适配而保持较高结构保真度；盲法放射科评估确认解剖感知合成改善主观图像质量与临床效用。

Insight: 缺失 MRI 模态补全不能只追求逐像素逼真，还必须保留解剖结构和拓扑，才能为后续分割及临床复核提供可信依据。下游分割与盲法放射学评价共同支持该目标设计，跨队列结果显示其潜在迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-parametric MRI is the gold standard for neuro-oncology, yet acquiring complete 4-modality protocols may be unfeasible in real-world clinical practice. When handling missing modalities, existing automated systems force a critical compromise: robust segmenters operate as uninterpretable “black boxes” that bypass image generation, while standard synthesis models often produce blurry reconstructions lacking diagnostic reliability. To address this issue, we propose CAPS, a Clinically-Verifiable Anatomy-Prioritized Synthesis framework that reconciles computational precision with clinical transparency. Our multi-modal generative architecture is driven by a hybrid objective function (L_1 + L_AFP + L_DualDice) that explicitly enforces the preservation of deep anatomical features and strict topological boundaries, rather than relying solely on pixel-wise optimization. Evaluated comprehensively on the BraTS Adult Glioma dataset, the framework achieves superior synthesis fidelity across intensity-based metrics, and demonstrates robust downstream segmentation performance (82.38% mean Dice), successfully restoring verifiable visual context. Furthermore, the framework exhibits exceptional zero-shot cross-domain generalization, maintaining high structural fidelity across completely unseen Meningioma, Pediatric, and Sub-Saharan African cohorts without domain-specific adaptation. Finally, a blinded radiological assessment confirms that our anatomy-aware approach significantly enhances both perceived image quality and clinical utility, providing clinicians with the reliable visual evidence required to trust downstream automated workflows.

eess.SY [Back]

[173] Robotic Video World Models: A Survey of Applications, Research Challenges, Future Directions eess.SY | cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Zhiting Mei, Tenny Yin, Ola Shorinwa, Apurva Badithela, Zhonghe Zheng

TL;DR: 该综述梳理视频模型作为机器人具身世界模型的应用、挑战和未来方向，涵盖数据生成、策略学习、强化学习动力学/奖励建模、策略评估与视觉规划，并强调物理幻觉、安全、指令遵循及算力成本问题。

Details

Motivation: 视频世界模型可根据多模态输入生成包含细粒度交互的高保真视频，有望缓解物理模拟器难以逼真处理可变形物体和复杂交互的问题，并比语言抽象更细致地表示世界动态。但模型仍可能生成违反物理规律的未来预测。

Result: 综述回顾视频模型及其在机器人具身世界模型中的应用，包括高效数据生成与策略学习、强化学习中的动力学和奖励建模、策略评估及视觉规划。作者总结阻碍可信集成的挑战：指令遵循不足、违反物理规律等幻觉、不安全内容生成，以及显著的数据和计算开销；并提出可能的未来研究方向，推动视频模型在机器人和安全关键场景的应用。

Insight: 视频模型能以细粒度表达复杂物理交互，补足语言世界描述和部分传统仿真的局限，但逼真视频不等于可信物理预测。要用于机器人闭环，仍需解决物理一致性、安全、可控性和资源成本，并开展面向安全关键场景的验证。

Abstract: Video world models have emerged as promising candidates for high-fidelity world models, offering the potential to synthesize high-quality videos capturing fine-grained interactions between agents and their environments conditioned on multi-modal user inputs. Their impressive capabilities address many of the long-standing challenges faced by physics-based simulators, driving broad adoption in many problem domains, e.g., robotics. For example, video models can generate photorealistic, physically consistent deformable-body simulation without making prohibitive simplifying assumptions, which is a major bottleneck in physics-based simulation. Moreover, video models can serve as foundation world models that capture the dynamics of the world in a fine-grained and expressive way. They thus overcome the limited expressiveness of language-only abstractions in describing intricate physical interactions. However, despite their potential, video models still generate physics-violating future predictions, often manifesting as hallucinations. In this survey, we provide a review of video models and their applications as embodied world models in robotics, including efficient data generation and policy learning, dynamics and rewards modeling in reinforcement learning, policy evaluation, and visual planning. Further, we highlight important challenges hindering the trustworthy integration of video models, such as poor instruction following, hallucinations like violations of physics, unsafe content generation, in addition to significant data and compute overhead. We present potential future directions to address these open research challenges to motivate research and ultimately facilitate broader applications, especially in safety-critical settings. We provide a curated bibliography at https://github.com/irom-princeton/awesome-robotics-video-world-model-papers .
